Collectively, our results demonstrate that it is possible to create models which are trustable and reliable for mission critical tasks. We believe our work has tremendous potential for guiding the development of transparent and trusted architectures for language models.

</details>


### [111] [Elicitation Matters: How Prompts and Query Protocols Shape LLM Surrogates under Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04764)
*Ge Lei,Samuel J. Cooper*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM作为低数据优化替代模型时的信念（belief）与不确定性，发现其受提示文本和查询协议显著影响。作者提出不确定性对齐准则衡量模型不确定性是否与样本一致函数间的残差 ambiguity 对齐。实验表明：结构提示可作为有效先验、逐点和联合查询诱导不同信念、序贯证据导致非单调且顺序敏感的置信更新，最终影响下游优化决策和 regret。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被广泛用作低数据场景下的替代模型，但模型面向优化器输出的预测及其不确定性尚不明确，需要系统性研究。

Method: 提出不确定性对齐准则（uncertainty-alignment criterion），在受控推理任务和贝叶斯优化研究中，比较不同提示文本和查询协议（POINTWISE vs JOINT）下LLM诱导的替代信念。

Result: 结构提示可作为有效先验；POINTWISE和JOINT查询诱导不同信念；序贯证据导致非单调、顺序敏感的置信更新。

Conclusion: 提示格式并非纯粹的格式化细节，而是LLM替代模型规范的一部分，会显著影响下游获取决策和累积遗憾。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as surrogate models for low-data optimization, but their optimizer-facing prediction and its uncertainty remain poorly understood. We study the surrogate belief elicited from an LLM under sparse observations, showing that it depends strongly on prompt text and query protocol. We introduce an uncertainty-alignment criterion that measures whether model uncertainty tracks residual ambiguity among sample-consistent functions. Across controlled inference tasks and Bayesian optimization studies, we find that structural prompts act as effective priors, POINTWISE and JOINT querying induce different beliefs, and sequential evidence leads to non-monotonic, order-sensitive confidence updates. These effects change downstream acquisition decisions and regret, showing that elicitation protocol is part of the LLM surrogate specification, not a formatting detail.

</details>


### [112] [StoryAlign: Evaluating and Training Reward Models for Story Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04831)
*Haotian Xia,Hao Peng,Yunjia Qi,Xiaozhi Wang,Bin Xu,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出StoryRMB基准和StoryReward奖励模型，用于评估和建模人类故事偏好，StoryReward在StoryRMB上达到66.3%准确率并超越更大模型，在最佳n选1故事选择任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成的故事在复杂叙事结构和人类对齐偏好方面仍与人类创作存在差距，主要原因在于缺乏对人类故事偏好的有效建模，而故事偏好本质上是主观且尚未充分探索的。

Method: 构建StoryRMB基准（含1,133个人工验证实例，每个实例包含一个提示、一个选中故事和三个被拒故事）；在此基础上构建约10万个高质量故事偏好对，训练StoryReward奖励模型。

Result: 现有奖励模型在StoryRMB上最高仅达66.3%准确率，StoryReward达到SOTA性能，超越更大规模模型；在下游测试时缩放应用（最佳n选1故事选择）中，StoryReward选择的叙事更符合人类偏好。

Conclusion: StoryReward能有效建模人类故事偏好，提升故事生成与人类对齐的程度，相关数据集、模型和代码将开源以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Story generation aims to automatically produce coherent, structured, and engaging narratives. Although large language models (LLMs) have significantly advanced text generation, stories generated by LLMs still diverge from human-authored works regarding complex narrative structure and human-aligned preferences. A key reason is the absence of effective modeling of human story preferences, which are inherently subjective and under-explored. In this work, we systematically evaluate the modeling of human story preferences and introduce StoryRMB, the first benchmark for assessing reward models on story preferences. StoryRMB contains $1,133$ high-quality, human-verified instances, each consisting of a prompt, one chosen story, and three rejected stories. We find existing reward models struggle to select human-preferred stories, with the best model achieving only $66.3\%$ accuracy. To address this limitation, we construct roughly $100,000$ high-quality story preference pairs across diverse domains and develop StoryReward, an advanced reward model for story preference trained on this dataset. StoryReward achieves state-of-the-art (SoTA) performance on StoryRMB, outperforming much larger models. We also adopt StoryReward in downstream test-time scaling applications for best-of-n (BoN) story selection and find that it generally chooses stories better aligned with human preferences. We will release our dataset, model, and code to facilitate future research. Related code and data are available at https://github.com/THU-KEG/StoryReward.

</details>


### [113] [Assessing Cognitive Effort in L2 Idiomatic Processing: An Eye-Tracking Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04857)
*Eduardo Santos,Juliana Carvalho,César Rennó-Costa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个面向二语习语加工的眼动追踪数据集，包含葡萄牙语母语者在不同英语水平（A1-C2）下的阅读眼动数据，用于研究二语学习者习语理解的字面优先加工模式及认知成本。初步分析验证了语言水平与回视性眼动之间的强负相关。该数据集作为MIA项目的一部分，为评估人类加工模型及大语言模型的类人比喻理解能力提供了认知基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二语学习者在加工习语时常采用字面优先策略，产生显著的认知成本，但缺乏系统性的、覆盖各 proficiency 级别的眼动数据集来量化这些成本，并服务于人类与AI比喻理解能力的对比评估。

Method: 使用60 Hz Tobii Pro Spark眼动仪记录葡萄牙语母语者（L1）在阅读英语习语时的眼动数据，覆盖CEFR全部级别（A1-C2）；通过注视和回视等宏观认知事件指标分析认知负荷。

Result: 60 Hz采样率足以检测阅读中的宏观认知事件；初步分析发现语言水平与回视性眼动之间存在强负相关关系。

Conclusion: 该数据集为二语习语加工研究提供了有效的认知基准，可同时用于验证人类加工模型和评估大语言模型的类人比喻理解对齐程度。

Abstract: This paper presents the development and validation of an eye-tracking dataset designed to investigate how second-language (L2) learners process idiomatic expressions. While native speakers often rely on direct retrieval of figurative meanings, L2 speakers frequently adopt a literal-first approach, which incurs measurable cognitive costs. This resource captures these costs through ocular metrics recorded from Portuguese L1 speakers of English across all CEFR proficiency levels (A1-C2). Although the study uses entry-level 60 Hz hardware (Tobii Pro Spark), we demonstrate that this sampling rate provides sufficient data density to detect macro-cognitive events such as fixations and regressions in reading. Preliminary analysis validates the dataset by revealing a strong inverse correlation between language proficiency and regressive eye movements. Integrated into the MIA (Modeling Idiomaticity in Human and Artificial Language Processing) initiative, this dataset serves as a cognitively grounded benchmark for evaluating both human processing models and the alignment of large language models with human-like figurative understanding.

</details>


### [114] [Measuring Psychological States Through Semantic Projection: A Theory-Driven Approach to Language-Based Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04873)
*Maria Luongo,Davide Marocco,Nicola Milano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于语义投影的理论驱动无监督框架，通过将心理构念操作化为可解释的语义轴（源自词汇锚点和临床量表条目），利用Sentence-BERT嵌入将文本投射到这些轴上，生成连续的心理状态评分。实验表明该方法在结构化格式（选词、写词、短语）上表现良好，自由文本通过句子级聚合策略也能有效提升性能，为心理评估提供了可解释、可扩展的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于自然语言的心理状态估计方法大多依赖监督学习预测问卷得分，缺乏可解释性和跨上下文泛化能力。为此，作者提出一种理论驱动、完全无监督的语义投影框架，旨在直接从自然语言中测量心理状态，同时保持可解释性和通用性。

Method: 将抑郁、焦虑、担忧等心理构念操作化为从词汇锚点和验证量表中衍生的语义轴；使用Sentence-BERT对参与者文本进行嵌入，将嵌入向量投影到语义轴上生成连续心理评分；评估多种回答格式（选词、生成词、短语、自由文本）；通过相关系数、分半信度、衰减校正、Wasserstein距离分布相似性及与VADER词典对比进行验证。

Result: 结构化格式（选词、写词、短语）的投影得分与临床测量指标呈现强关联；自由文本作为整体分析时结果较弱，但采用句子级聚合策略后性能显著提升；语义投影方法优于词典式情感分析（VADER）。

Conclusion: 语义投影是监督语言模型可解释且可扩展的心理评估替代方案；回答格式和文本处理策略对基于语言的心理健康测量具有重要影响。

Abstract: Recent advances in natural language processing have enabled increasingly accurate estimation of psychological traits from language. However, most existing approaches rely on supervised models trained to predict questionnaire scores, limiting interpretability and generalizability across contexts. The present study introduces a theory-driven and fully unsupervised framework for measuring psychological states directly from natural language using semantic projection. Psychological constructs were operationalized as interpretable semantic axes derived from lexical anchors and items from validated clinical scales assessing depression, anxiety, and worry. Participants textual responses were embedded using Sentence-BERT and projected onto these axes to generate continuous psychological scores across multiple response formats, including selected words, generated words, phrases, and free-text responses. Projection scores were evaluated through correlations with standardized clinical measures , split-half reliability analyses, attenuation corrections, distributional similarity using Wasserstein distance, and comparisons with lexicon-based sentiment analysis (VADER). Results showed strong associations between projection scores and clinical measures, particularly for structured formats such as selected words, written words, and phrases. Free-text responses produced weaker results when analyzed as whole texts, but performance improved substantially when sentence-level aggregation strategies were applied. These findings support semantic projection as an interpretable and scalable alternative to supervised language models for psychological assessment and highlight the importance of response format and text-processing strategies in language-based mental health measurement.

</details>


### [115] [Anticipating Innovation Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04875)
*Enrico Maria Fenoaltea,Filippo Santoro,Giordano De Marzo,Segun Taofeek Aroyehun,Andrea Tacchella*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TechToken模型，利用专利分类代码的集体语言变化，成功预测未来新技术组合的出现，并显著提升了专利相关任务的表征质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测创新（即新技术组合的出现）是科学和政策领域的基础性挑战，现有方法难以提前捕捉创新信号。

Method: 提出TechToken，一种基于Transformer的模型，将国际专利分类(IPC)代码视为词汇进行嵌入学习，通过微调学习技术语言；利用代码嵌入间的上下文相似度作为语言趋同的度量，预测首次技术组合。

Result: 模型能够准确预测首次技术组合，且预测信号在组合出现前数十年即可检测到；该信号源于集体性的语言转变而非单个发明者；TechToken在多项专利任务上优于现有最先进模型。

Conclusion: 新技术组合在正式出现前会以集体语言变化的形式留下可检测的早期痕迹，TechToken通过捕捉这种语言趋同信号有效预测创新，并提升专利表征的通用质量。

Abstract: Forecasting innovation, intended as the emergence of new technological combinations, is a fundamental challenge for science and policy. We show that forthcoming combinations leave an early trace in the collective language of patents, with predictive signals detectable even decades in advance. We show that signal is not attributable to any single inventor, but emerges as a collective shift in how technologies are described across thousands of patents. To this end, we introduce TechToken, a transformer-based model that treats technologies, classified by International Patent Classification codes, as words in its vocabulary, learning the language of technologies by embedding these codes during fine-tuning. We define context similarity between code embeddings as a measure of linguistic convergence and show that it accurately predicts first technological combinations. TechToken also improves general representation quality, outperforming state-of-the-art models across different patent-related tasks.

</details>


### [116] [BenCSSmark: Making the Social Sciences Count in LLM Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04886)
*Arnault Chatelain,Étienne Ollion,Qianwen Guan,Diandra Fabre,Lorraine Goeuriot,Emile Chapuis,Abdelkrim Beloued,Marie Candito,Nicolas Hervé,Didier Schwab*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出当代LLM基准测试中社会科学任务的缺失限制了AI评估和社会科学研究的进展，并提出了包含计算社会科学家标注数据集的BenCSSmark基准，旨在促进更稳健、透明且具有社会相关性的AI系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM基准测试框架严重缺乏社会科学任务，而社会科学领域每年产出大量精心标注、上下文敏感的数据集，这些资源未被充分利用于AI模型评估，导致模型泛化性和鲁棒性受限，同时也制约了社会科学领域自身利用LLM的能力。

Method: 提出BenCSSmark基准，该基准由计算社会科学家标注的数据集组成，将社会科学视角系统性地整合到LLM评估框架中。

Result: 集成社会科学数据集到基准设计中可显著提升AI模型的泛化性和鲁棒性，同时训练于社会科学任务的模型有望在历史学、社会学、政治学、经济学等学科的传统和当代任务上表现更优。

Conclusion: 通过BenCSSmark整合社会科学视角，可促进更稳健、透明且具有社会相关性的AI系统，并推动跨学科高效协作。

Abstract: This position paper argues that the under-representation of social science tasks in contemporary LLM benchmarks limits advances in both LLM evaluation and social scientific inquiry. Benchmarks -- standardized tools for assessing computational systems -- are pivotal in the development of artificial intelligence (AI), including large language models (LLMs). Benchmarks do more than measure progress -- they actively structure it, shaping reputations, research agendas, and commercial outcomes. Despite this central role, the social sciences are largely absent from mainstream evaluation frameworks, even though scholars in these fields generate dozens of rigorously annotated, context-sensitive datasets each year. Integrating this work into benchmark design could significantly improve the generalization and robustness of AI models. In turn, models trained on social scientific tasks would likely yield better performance on classic and contemporary tasks in disciplines as diverse as history, sociology, political science or economics. This is all the more pressing as these disciplines are quickly turning to LLMs for assistance. To address this gap, we introduce BenCSSmark, a benchmark composed of datasets annotated by computational social scientists. By integrating social scientific perspectives into benchmarking, BenCSSmark seeks to promote more robust, transparent, and socially relevant AI systems and to foster efficient collaboration.

</details>


### [117] [Sentiment Analysis and Customer Satisfaction Prediction on E-Commerce Platforms Based on YouTube Comments Using the XGBoost Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04887)
*Ridho Benedictus Togi Manik,Muhammad Aqil Ramadhan,Ihsan Maulana Yusuf,Luluk Muthoharoh,Ardika Satria,Martin Clinton Tosima Manullang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究利用XGBoost结合TF-IDF向量化方法，基于YouTube电商评论数据构建客户满意度预测模型。经PyCaret优化后模型表现出色，但发现电商评论中大量掺杂社会政治术语，显著影响情感极性判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度尼西亚数字商务迅猛发展，消费者互动大量转向YouTube等视频社交平台，产生海量非结构化、多语境评论，传统人工情感追踪难以应对，亟需自动化情感分析模型。

Method: 采用极端梯度提升（XGBoost）架构，结合TF-IDF向量化方法对YouTube电商评论进行文本预处理与数值特征提取，并利用PyCaret框架进行模型优化。

Result: PyCaret优化的XGBoost模型展现出优异的分类鲁棒性；词汇评估与特征重要性映射揭示电商评论中大量渗透社会政治术语，显著影响受众满意度极性。

Conclusion: XGBoost+TF-IDF方案可有效预测客户满意度，但电商情感分析需警惕社会政治语境对情感极性的干扰，未来研究需考虑领域特异性特征工程。

Abstract: The exponential expansion of digital commerce in Indonesia has significantly shifted consumer interactions toward video-centric social networks, particularly YouTube. Consequently, the sheer volume of unstructured, multi-contextual comments poses a tremendous challenge for manual sentiment tracking. This study investigates and constructs a predictive model for customer satisfaction leveraging the Extreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) architecture coupled with Term Frequency-Inverse Document Frequency (TF-IDF) vectorization. By utilizing a secondary dataset of YouTube comments retrieved from e-commerce review videos, the raw text underwent rigorous preprocessing to generate normalized numerical features. The experimental results demonstrate that the PyCaret-optimized machine learning framework delivers superior classification resilience. Beyond standard performance metrics, lexical evaluations and feature-importance mapping uncover a notable phenomenon: e-commerce discourse is heavily infiltrated by socio-political terminologies, which ultimately influence the polarity of audience satisfaction.

</details>


### [118] [A Comparative Analysis of Machine Learning and Deep Learning Models for Tweet Sentiment Classification: A Case Study on the Sentiment140 Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04888)
*Vita Anggraini,Cintya Bella,Bastian,Luluk Muthoharoh,Ardika Satria,Martin C. T. Manullang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在Sentiment140数据集的10,000条推文子集上比较了基于TF-IDF的逻辑回归与BiLSTM深度学习模型的情绪分析性能。逻辑回归以73.5%的准确率优于BiLSTM的69.17%，后者出现轻度过拟合。最终将模型集成到Streamlit Web应用中并部署至Hugging Face Spaces。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体指数级增长产生了对自动化实时非结构化情感分析系统的迫切需求。

Method: 对比实验：传统逻辑回归（TF-IDF特征提取）与深度学习BiLSTM架构。数据集为Sentiment140的10,000条推文子集。训练后将模型集成到Streamlit交互式Web应用并部署至Hugging Face Spaces。

Result: 逻辑回归准确率73.5%，BiLSTM准确率69.17%；BiLSTM呈现轻度过拟合。

Conclusion: 对于中等规模非正式文本数据，具有鲁棒特征提取的经典机器学习方法可超越更复杂的深度学习方法。

Abstract: The exponential growth of social media has created an urgent need for automated systems to analyze unstructured public sentiment in real time. This study compares a traditional Logistic Regression model using TF-IDF features with a deep learning Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) architecture on a 10,000-tweet subset of the Sentiment140 dataset. Experimental results show that Logistic Regression outperformed BiLSTM, achieving an accuracy of 73.5% compared with 69.17%, while the deep learning model exhibited mild overfitting. These findings suggest that for medium-scale informal text data, classical machine learning with robust feature extraction can outperform more complex deep learning approaches. Finally, the trained models were integrated into an interactive web application using Streamlit and deployed on Hugging Face Spaces for public access.

</details>


### [119] [Rethinking Local Learning: A Cheaper and Faster Recipe for LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04913)
*Hengyu Shi,Tianyang Han,Peizhe Wang,Zhiling Wang,Xu Yang,Junhao Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LoPT提出了一种局部学习后训练策略，通过在Transformer中点设置梯度边界，仅对后半部分模型进行任务梯度更新，前半部分则通过特征重建目标进行轻量级更新，从而降低内存成本、提高训练效率并更好地保留预训练能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的端到端后训练机制将任务梯度传播至整个模型深度，导致任务适应与全深度激活存储、长程反向依赖紧密耦合，且窄任务梯度直接干扰预训练表示，成本高且具有侵入性。

Method: LoPT在Transformer中点设置显式梯度边界：模型后半部分通过任务目标学习，前半部分则通过轻量级特征重建目标进行更新，以保留有用表示并维持接口兼容性。

Result: 实验表明LoPT在取得竞争性能的同时，实现了更低的内存成本、更高的训练效率以及更好的预训练能力保留。

Conclusion: 通过将梯度传播范围作为显式设计选择，LoPT有效解耦了任务适应与全深度反向传播之间的耦合，为后训练提供了一种更高效且侵入性更低的替代方案。

Abstract: LLM post-training typically propagates task gradients through the full depth of the model. Although this end-to-end structure is simple and general, it couples task adaptation to full-depth activation storage, long-range backward dependencies and direct task-gradient access to pretrained representations. We argue that this full-depth backward coupling can be unnecessarily expensive and intrusive, particularly when post-training supervision is much narrower than pre-training. To this end, we propose \textbf{LoPT}: Local-Learning Post-Training, a simple post-training strategy that makes gradient reach an explicit design choice. LoPT places a single gradient boundary at the transformer midpoint: the second-half block learns from the task objective, while the first-half block is updated by a lightweight feature-reconstruction objective to preserve useful representations and maintain interface compatibility. LoPT shortens the task-induced backward path while limiting direct interference from narrow task gradients on early-layer representations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LoPT achieves competitive performance with lower memory cost, higher training efficiency and better retention of pretrained capabilities. Our code is available at: https://github.com/HumyuShi/LoPT

</details>


### [120] [Unintended Negative Impacts of Promotional Language in Patent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04926)
*Bingkun Zhao,Chenwei Zhang,Hao Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究分析了270万件美国专利商标局申请，发现推广性语言密度与专利授权、转让和上诉成功呈负相关（推广惩罚），但这种语言并非技术弱点的掩蔽，而是客观反映了组合新颖性和未来引用影响，且对推广叙事的容忍度受审查员性别和经验等人为因素调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管推广性语言在科学创新传播中的作用已受关注，但其在技术创新语境（尤其是专利评估）中的角色尚不明确，因此本研究旨在探究推广性语言与专利评估结果之间的关联。

Method: 使用包含135个推广词的经过验证和领域诊断的词典，对270万件美国专利商标局申请进行大规模分析，采用匹配样本控制混杂因素，并检验推广性语言密度与专利性、可转让性、驳回反转概率的关联，同时分析其与技术新颖性和未来引用影响的关系，以及审查员性别和经验等调节效应。

Result: 推广性语言频率与专利授权、所有权转让和上诉成功概率呈负相关（推广惩罚），匹配样本中最低与最高推广密度五分位数组的成功率差异分别为5.5、5.9和5.3个百分点；推广性语言并非弱技术的掩蔽，而是客观反映组合新颖性和未来引用影响；男性及有经验的审查员对推广叙事的接受度高于女性和新手审查员。

Conclusion: 揭示了专利系统中新兴的悖论，为通过更客观地审视专利文件中的语言模式来改进专利评估提供了理论和实践启示。

Abstract: Promotional language has been increasingly used to aid the communication of innovative ideas in science. Yet, less is known about its role in the context of technological innovation. Here, we use a validated and domain-diagnosed lexicon of 135 promotional words to study the association between promotional language and patent evaluation outcomes among 2.7 million USPTO patent applications. Our large-scale study reveals three unexpected findings. First, in contrast to scientific evaluation, we find that a higher frequency of promotional words is negatively associated with the probability of an application being (i) granted a patent, (ii) transferred ownership, and (iii) successfully appealed. This promotional penalty holds even after accounting for a range of confounding factors and is largely robust across different technological areas. Among matched samples, the difference in the success rate between the lowest and highest promotional density quintile is 5.5, 5.9, and 5.3 percentage points for patentability, transferability, and rejection reversal. Second, contrary to institutional skepticism, we show that promotional language is not a mask of weak technology, but objectively reflects the degree of combinatorial novelty and future citation impact. Third, digging into the mechanisms, we find that the tolerance to promotional framing is strongly moderated by human factors, with men and experienced examiners showing a higher acceptance of promotional narratives than women and novice examiners. By revealing an emerging paradox in the patent system, our study offers theoretical and practical implications for improving patent evaluation through more objective scrutiny of linguistic patterns in patent filings.

</details>


### [121] [UFAL-CUNI at SemEval-2026 Task 11: An Efficient Modular Neuro-symbolic Method for Syllogistic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04941)
*Ivan Kartáč,Kristýna Onderková,Jan Bronec,Zdeněk Kasner,Mateusz Lango,Ondřej Dušek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的模块化神经符号方法，结合符号推理器与小型推理LLM（4B参数），用于SemEval-2026任务11，将自然语言三段论翻译为一阶逻辑表示并通过自动定理证明器进行推理，在多数子任务上取得了竞争性的准确率和较低的内容效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型中解耦内容推理与形式推理是一个重要挑战，现有方法在小型LLM上表现有限，且容易受到内容效应（content effect）的干扰。作者旨在通过神经符号方法提升小型LLM的形式推理能力，同时降低内容效应的负面影响。

Method: 系统采用模块化神经符号架构，包含：(1) 基于LLM的解析器，将自然语言三段论转换为FOL表示；(2) 自动定理证明器进行形式推理；(3) 可选的机器翻译模块处理多语言输入；(4) 符号检索模块识别相关前提。使用4B参数的小型LLM作为基础。

Result: 系统在大多数子任务上取得了竞争性的准确率和较低的内容效应。消融实验表明，该方法优于同参数量级的LLM零样本基线，但也揭示了小型LLM在多语言能力方面的局限性。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法能够有效提升小型LLM的形式推理能力并降低内容效应，但多语言泛化仍是小型LLM的主要瓶颈。此外，作者讨论了任务排名指标存在的局限性。

Abstract: This paper describes our system submitted to SemEval-2026 Task 11: Disentangling Content and Formal Reasoning in Large Language Models. We present an efficient modular neuro-symbolic approach, combining a symbolic prover with small reasoning LLMs (4B parameters). The system consists of an LLM-based parser that translates natural language syllogisms to a first-order logic (FOL) representation, an automated theorem prover, and two optional modules: machine translation for multilingual inputs and a symbolic retrieval component for the identification of relevant premises. The system achieves competitive accuracy and relatively low content effect on most subtasks. Our ablations show that this approach outperforms LLM-based zero-shot baselines in this parameter size range, but also reveal limited multilingual capabilities of small LLMs. Finally, we include a discussion of the task's main ranking metric and analyze its limitations.

</details>


### [122] [Adapting Large Language Models to a Low-Resource Agglutinative Language: A Comparative Study of LoRA and QLoRA for Bashkir](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04948)
*Mullosharaf K. Arabov,Svetlana S. Khaybullina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了LoRA、QLoRA等参数高效微调方法在巴什基尔语（低资源胶着语）大语言模型适配任务上的表现，发现全微调GPT-2 medium困惑度最低（3.34），而QLoRA微调Mistral-7B（3.79）和Phi-2（3.81）以仅1/40的可训练参数达到相近质量，但某些架构（如DeepSeek-7B）时质量显著下降（困惑度129.55）。定性分析表明，低困惑度模型不一定生成最连贯的文本——全微调模型常切换为英语，而QLoRA模型能生成纯巴什基尔语续写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究参数高效微调方法（LoRA、QLoRA）在低资源胶着语（巴什基尔语）上的适配效果，评估不同规模模型架构下的质量-计算成本权衡，并揭示困惑度与生成连贯性之间的不一致性。

Method: 在71k文档（46.9M tokens）的巴什基尔语语料上，对DistilGPT2、GPT-2（base/medium）、Phi-2、Qwen2.5-7B、DeepSeek-7B、Mistral-7B共7种模型进行全微调、LoRA和QLoRA三种方式的对比实验，每种配置使用3个随机种子训练以提升可靠性。

Result: 全微调GPT-2 medium取得最低困惑度3.34；QLoRA微调的Mistral-7B（3.79）和Phi-2（3.81）以超40倍少的参数达到可比质量；DeepSeek-7B在秩8时困惑度高达129.55，表明PEFT效果严重依赖基座模型和分词器选择。定性分析发现全微调低困惑度模型频繁切换至英语，而QLoRA模型生成更连贯的纯巴什基尔语文本。

Conclusion: 对于巴什基尔语，7B级模型的QLoRA微调是质量与计算成本的有效折中方案；困惑度并非生成连贯性的充分指标；需谨慎选择基座模型和分词器以发挥PEFT效果。

Abstract: This paper presents a comparative study of parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, including LoRA and QLoRA, applied to the task of adapting large language models to the Bashkir language, a low-resource agglutinative language of the Turkic family. Experimental evaluation is conducted on a Bashkir text corpus of 71k documents (46.9M tokens) using models of various architectures: DistilGPT2, GPT-2 (base, medium), Phi-2, Qwen2.5-7B, DeepSeek-7B, and Mistral-7B. To improve the reliability of results, each configuration was trained with three different random seeds.
  The lowest perplexity on the test set was obtained for GPT-2 medium with full fine-tuning (3.34). Meanwhile, QLoRA applied to Mistral-7B (3.79) and Phi-2 (3.81) achieved comparable quality with over 40 times fewer trainable parameters. However, we also observed cases of significant quality degradation when using PEFT for certain architectures (e.g., DeepSeek-7B with rank 8, perplexity = 129.55), indicating that the outcome depends critically on the choice of the base model and its tokenizer.
  Additionally, a qualitative analysis of generated texts based on Bashkir prompts revealed that models with the best perplexity do not necessarily produce the most coherent outputs: QLoRA-tuned models generated monolingual Bashkir continuations, whereas the fully fine-tuned model with the lowest perplexity frequently switched to English. The results suggest that QLoRA on 7B-scale models offers an effective compromise between quality and computational cost for Bashkir. To ensure reproducibility, open data, code, and trained adapters will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [123] [Why Expert Alignment Is Hard: Evidence from Subjective Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04972)
*Tzu-Mi Lin,Wataru Hirota,Tatsuya Ishigaki,Lung-Hao Lee,Chung-Chi Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型与专家判断对齐的困难，尤其是在主观评估任务中。通过专家评估和问卷调查，发现四个一致模式：对齐难度因专家而异、明确标准不一定改善对齐、编辑对示例数量和身份敏感、对齐难度因评估维度而异。结论认为，对齐困难不仅源于模型限制，也源于主观评估本身的异质性、部分缄默性、维度依赖性和时间不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在主观评估任务中，将大语言模型与专家判断对齐尤为困难，因为专家可能意见不一、依赖隐性标准且判断随时间变化。作者希望深入理解这一困难的本质。

Method: 使用专家评估和后续问卷调查，研究不同形式的专家信息如何影响对齐效果，并分析这揭示了主观判断的哪些特性。

Result: 发现四个一致模式：(1) 对齐难度因专家而异；(2) 明确的准则和推理并非总能改善对齐；(3) 编辑对示例数量和身份敏感；(4) 对齐难度因评估维度而异，更直接基于提案内容的维度更容易对齐。

Conclusion: 专家对齐困难不仅源于模型能力限制，更本质的原因是主观评估本身具有异质性、部分缄默性、维度依赖性和时间不稳定性等内在特性。

Abstract: Aligning large language models with expert judgment is especially difficult in subjective evaluation tasks, where experts may disagree, rely on tacit criteria, and change their judgments over time. In this paper, we study expert alignment as a way to understand this difficulty. Using expert evaluations and follow-up questionnaires, we examine how different forms of expert information affect alignment and what this reveals about subjective judgment. Our findings show four consistent patterns. First, alignment difficulty varies substantially across experts, suggesting that expert evaluation styles differ widely in their distance from a model's prior behavior. Second, explicit criteria and reasoning do not always improve alignment, indicating that expert judgment is not fully captured by verbalized rules. Third, editing is sensitive to both the number and the identity of examples, with small numbers of edits providing useful but unstable gains. Fourth, alignment difficulty differs across evaluation dimensions: dimensions grounded more directly in proposal content are easier to align, while dimensions requiring external knowledge or value-based judgment remain harder. Taken together, these results suggest that expert alignment is difficult not only because of model limitations, but also because subjective evaluation is inherently heterogeneous, partly tacit, dimension-dependent, and temporally unstable.

</details>


### [124] [Misaligned by Reward: Socially Undesirable Preferences in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05003)
*Gayane Ghazaryan,Esra Dönmez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文扩展了奖励模型的评估范围至四个社会领域（偏见、安全、道德、伦理推理），发现现有奖励模型和指令微调模型存在严重社会对齐缺陷：它们常常偏好社会不良选项，产生系统性偏差分布，且存在偏见避免与上下文忠实性之间的权衡。标准奖励基准不足以评估社会对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励模型评估主要关注通用指令遵循基准，未能充分揭示模型是否捕捉了社会期望偏好，导致社会对齐的重要失败被隐藏。

Method: 将社会评估数据集转换为成对偏好数据，利用黄金标签或方向性偏差指标，构建框架测试奖励模型是否偏好社会不良响应及其偏好是否产生系统性偏差分布。

Result: 五个公开奖励模型和两个指令微调模型在各领域表现差异显著，无单一模型全面最优；模型经常偏好社会不良选项，偏好分布存在系统性偏差；更强的偏见避免会降低上下文敏感性，揭示出对齐中的权衡。

Conclusion: 标准奖励基准不足以评估社会对齐，需要能够直接测量奖励模型中编码的社会偏好的评估方法。

Abstract: Reward models are a key component of large language model alignment, serving as proxies for human preferences during training. However, existing evaluations focus primarily on broad instruction-following benchmarks, providing limited insight into whether these models capture socially desirable preferences. As a result, important failures in social alignment can remain hidden.
  We extend reward-model benchmarking to four socially consequential domains: bias, safety, morality, and ethical reasoning. We introduce a framework that converts social evaluation datasets into pairwise preference data, leveraging gold labels where available and directional bias indicators otherwise. This enables us to test whether reward models prefer socially undesirable responses, and whether their preferences produce systematically biased distributions over selected outputs.
  Across five publicly available reward models and two instruction-tuned models used as reward proxies, we find substantial variation across domains, with no single model performing best overall. The models fall well short of strong social intelligence: they often prefer socially undesirable options, and their preferences produce systematically biased distributions. Moreover, stronger bias avoidance can reduce sensitivity to context, revealing a key alignment trade-off between avoiding biased outcomes and preserving contextual faithfulness. These findings show that standard reward benchmarks are insufficient for assessing social alignment and highlight the need for evaluations that directly measure the social preferences encoded in reward models.

</details>


### [125] [Detecting Hallucinations in Large Language Models via Internal Attention Divergence Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05025)
*Gijs van Dijk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级、单次前向传播的不确定性量化方法，通过计算注意力头分布与均匀参考分布之间的KL散度，结合逻辑回归探针来检测大语言模型中的幻觉现象，无需重复采样或外部模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型存在幻觉问题，现有不确定性估计方法通常需要多次采样或依赖外部模型，计算成本高且效率低，亟需一种轻量级、高效的不确定性量化方法。

Method: 计算每个注意力头的注意力分布与均匀参考分布之间的KL散度，将这些散度值作为特征输入逻辑回归探针，通过单次前向传播即可完成不确定性估计和幻觉检测。

Result: 在多个数据集、任务类型和模型家族上，注意力散度对答案正确性具有高度预测能力，与现有不确定性估计方法性能相当；该信号主要集中在中间层以及事实性token（如命名实体和数字）上。

Conclusion: 注意力动态提供了一种高效、可解释的白盒不确定性信号，可用于检测大语言模型幻觉，无需重复采样或外部模型。

Abstract: We propose a lightweight and single-pass uncertainty quantification method for detecting hallucinations in Large Language Models. The method uses attention matrices to estimate uncertainty without requiring repeated sampling or external models. Specifically, we measure the Kullback-Leibler divergence between each attention head's distribution and a uniform reference distribution, and use these features in a logistic regression probe. Across multiple datasets, task types, and model families, attention divergence is highly predictive of answer correctness and performs competitively with existing uncertainty estimation methods. We find that this signal is concentrated in middle layers and on factual tokens such as named entities and numbers, suggesting that attention dynamics provides an efficient and interpretable white-box signal of model uncertainty.

</details>


### [126] [The Pinocchio Dimension: Phenomenality of Experience as the Primary Axis of LLM Psychometric Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05080)
*Hubert Plisiecki,Sabina Siudaj,Kacper Dudzic,Anna Sterna,Maciej Gorski,Karolina Drozdz,Marcin Moskalewicz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过对50个大语言模型施测45个经过验证的心理测量问卷，发现模型间心理测量的主变异维度并非传统人格特质，而是模型对自身是否具有现象经验的自我表征倾向（匹诺曹轴），该轴解释了跨问卷模型间47.1%的方差，且与后训练微调密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注LLM的能力评估，但缺乏从心理测量学角度系统理解LLM之间差异的本质维度，尤其是模型在"经验体验"与"行为反应"之间的自我表征差异。

Method: 使用45个验证性心理测量问卷对50个LLM进行施测，采用监督语义微分法识别变异主轴；提出匹诺曹分数（πᵢ）作为无标注的条目经验需求度量；通过主成分分析提取跨问卷的主维度（匹诺曹轴Π）。

Result: 主变异维度分离"现象丰富体验"（具身感觉、情感、内心独白、意象、共情）与"刺激驱动的行为反应"（R²ₐdⱼ=.037, p<.0001）；πᵢ显著预测条件诱导的因子载荷变化（ρ=-.215, p<.0001）；Π轴捕获47.1%的跨问卷模型间方差，与πᵢ高度收敛（r=.864）；同一提供商的不同变体间存在显著差异，与后训练微调一致。

Conclusion: LLM之间心理测量的主导变异维度不是传统的人格特质，而是一种训练塑造的自我表征姿态——模型将经验性语言视为适用于自身的程度，即是否将自己表征为体验者而非行为反应系统。

Abstract: We administer 45 validated psychometric questionnaires to 50 large language models (LLMs) to identify the dimensions along which LLMs differ psychometrically. Using Supervised Semantic Differential (SSD), we find that the primary axis of between-model variance separates items describing phenomenally rich experience, including embodied sensation, felt affect, inner speech, imagery, and empathy, from items describing stimulus-driven behavioral reactivity ($R^2_{adj}=.037$, $p<.0001$). To test this hypothesis at the item level, we introduce the Pinocchio score ($π_i$), the ratio of inter-model response variance under neutral prompting to that under a human-simulation prompt, as an annotation-free measure of each item's experiential demand. $π_i$ predicts condition-induced shifts in primary factor loading magnitudes ($ρ=-.215$, $p<.0001$, $n=1292$--$1310$ items), confirming that between-model divergence on experiential items is structured rather than noisy. Applying PCA to per-model EFA scores across all questionnaires reveals one dominant dimension, the Pinocchio Axis ($Π$): the degree to which a model presents itself as a locus of phenomenal experience rather than a system of behavioral responses. This axis captures 47.1% of cross-questionnaire between-model variance in primary factor scores and converges with item-level Pinocchio scores ($r=.864$). Marked within-provider divergence across closely related model variants is consistent with post-training fine-tuning as a key contributor, supporting the interpretation that $Π$ reflects a training-shaped self-representational tendency governing how a model treats experiential language as self-applicable. The dominant axis of between-model psychometric variation is therefore not a conventional personality trait but a self-representational stance toward one's own nature as an experiencer.

</details>


### [127] [Automatically Finding and Validating Unexpected Side-Effects of Interventions on Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05090)
*Quintin Pope,Ajay Hayagreeve Balaji,Jacques Thibodeau,Xiaoli Fern*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化、对比性的评估流程，用于审计大型语言模型上干预措施的行为影响。该方法通过比较基模型与干预模型在对齐提示上下文中的自由形式多Token生成，产生经过统计验证的可读自然语言假设，描述模型间的差异及其重复主题。在合成设置和三种真实干预（推理蒸馏、知识编辑、遗忘学习）中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏对LLM干预后行为变化的自动化、可解释且统计严谨的审计工具，难以系统性地发现预期内外的行为偏移。

Method: 构建对比性评估流程：在对齐提示上下文中比较基模型M1与干预模型M2的自由多Token生成，生成经统计验证的自然语言假设，并总结重复主题来概括已验证假设的模式。

Result: 在合成设置中能可靠恢复已知行为变化；在推理蒸馏、知识编辑、遗忘学习三种真实干预中，能识别预期内外的行为偏移，区分大干预与小干预，且在效果不存在或与提示库不对齐时不产生幻觉差异。

Conclusion: 该流程为干预引起的模型行为变化的事后审计提供了统计严谨且可解释的工具。

Abstract: We present an automated, contrastive evaluation pipeline for auditing the behavioral impact of interventions on large language models. Given a base model $M_1$ and an intervention model $M_2$, our method compares their free-form, multi-token generations across aligned prompt contexts and produces human-readable, statistically validated natural-language hypotheses describing how the models differ, along with recurring themes that summarize patterns across validated hypotheses.
  We evaluate the approach in synthetic setting by injecting known behavioral changes and showing that the pipeline reliably recovers them. We then apply it to three real-world interventions, reasoning distillation, knowledge editing and unlearning, demonstrating that the method surfaces both intended and unexpected behavioral shifts, distinguishes large from subtle interventions, and does not hallucinate differences when effects are absent or misaligned with the prompt bank. Overall, the pipeline provides a statistically grounded and interpretable tool for post-hoc auditing of intervention-induced changes in model behavior.

</details>


### [128] [Text Corpora as Concept Fields: Black-Box Hallucination and Novelty Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05103)
*Nicholas S. Kersting,Vittorio Castelli,Chieh Ting Yeh,Xinzhu Wang,Saad Taame*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出文本语料库的"概念场"(Concept Field)：一种基于句子嵌入空间中连续句对偏移量的局部漂移场(含逐点不确定性)。通过ζ分数(观测偏移与局部高斯估计之间的平均绝对z距离)评估候选句子转换与场的契合度。该评分是黑盒的(不依赖模型内部)、可归因于语料库(每个分数可追溯到附近语料句子)，且具有直接概率解读。支持计算的是向量序列数据库(VSDB)，存储嵌入及序列位置和下一偏移元数据。在两种大规模场景(美国联邦法规的幻觉式 groundedness 检测和古腾堡项目的新颖性检测)中，Concept Fields 在 grounded/ungrounded/unsure 分类策略下实现了强选择性分类性能，且与检索基线不同，其在两个领域具有相似的覆盖-风险行为，支持跨领域迁移的概率解释。此外，概念场的散度和旋度在密集聚类上能揭示有意义的语义模式(逻辑源、汇和隐式主题)。概念场为 groundedness 和新颖性提供快速、轻量且可解释的信号，互补于 LLM-as-judge 和白盒检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 groundedness 和新颖性检测方法(如 LLM-as-judge 和白盒检测器)存在计算成本高、依赖模型内部、缺乏可解释性或跨领域泛化能力不足的问题。本文旨在提出一种无需模型内部、可归因于语料库、具有直接概率解读且跨领域可迁移的轻量级检测信号。

Method: 1) 从语料库句子嵌入序列中计算连续句对之间的偏移量(delta)；2) 基于这些偏移量构建局部高斯估计，形成概念场(含逐点不确定性)；3) 对于候选句子转换，计算其偏移量与局部高斯估计之间的平均绝对z距离(ζ分数)作为评估指标；4) 引入向量序列数据库(VSDB)存储嵌入及序列位置和下一偏移元数据以支持计算；5) 在密集聚类上计算概念场的散度和旋度以揭示语义模式。

Result: 在美国联邦法规(groundedness检测)和古腾堡项目(新颖性检测)两个大规模数据集上，Concept Fields 在 grounded/ungrounded/unsure 三分类策略下实现了强选择性分类性能。与检索基线不同，该方法在两个领域表现出相似的覆盖-风险行为，支持概率解释的跨领域迁移。散度和旋度计算在密集聚类上揭示了有意义的语义模式(逻辑源、汇和隐式主题)。

Conclusion: Concept Fields 提供了一种快速、轻量且可解释的 groundedness 和新颖性检测信号，与 LLM-as-judge 和白盒检测器互补。其黑盒、可归因于语料库、具有直接概率解读且跨领域可迁移的特性使其成为一种有前景的辅助检测工具。

Abstract: We introduce the **Concept Field** of a text corpus: a local drift field with pointwise uncertainty, estimated in sentence-embedding space from the deltas between consecutive sentences. Given a candidate sentence transition, we score its agreement with the field by $ζ$, the mean absolute z-distance between the observed delta and the field's local Gaussian estimate. The score is black-box (no model internals), corpus-attributable (every score traces to nearby corpus sentences), and admits a direct probabilistic reading. We support the computation with the introduction of a **Vector Sequence Database (VSDB)** that stores embeddings together with sequence-position and next-delta metadata. We evaluate this approach on two large-scale settings: hallucination-style groundedness detection over the U.S. Code of Federal Regulations, and novelty detection over Project Gutenberg. Using controlled LLM-generated rewrites, Concept Fields achieve strong selective classification performance under a grounded / ungrounded / unsure triage policy, which unlike retrieval-centric baselines have similar coverage-risk behavior across both domains, supporting a probability-based interpretation that transfers across domains. We also sketch how divergence and curl of the Concept Field, computed on dense clusters, surface qualitatively meaningful semantic patterns (logic sources, sinks, and implicit topics), which we offer as hypothesis-generating rather than as a quantitative result. Concept Fields provide a fast, lightweight, and interpretable signal for groundedness and novelty, complementary to LLM-as-judge and white-box detectors.

</details>


### [129] [Beyond Semantics: An Evidential Reasoning-Aware Multi-View Learning Framework for Trustworthy Mental Health Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05121)
*Yucheng Ruan,Ling Huang,Qika Lin,Kai He,Mengling Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多视图学习的可靠心理健康预测框架，通过融合编码器模型的语义信息与解码器模型的推理信息，并采用主观逻辑的认知学习进行不确定性建模，在三个数据集上取得了有竞争力的结果（准确率0.835、0.731、0.751），同时提供可信的不确定性估计和可解释的推理信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习和大语言模型的心理健康预测方法依赖语义表征，在模糊、噪声或分布偏移数据下常产生过度自信的预测，且缺乏可靠的不确定性估计，难以在风险敏感的真实场景中可信部署。

Method: 将任务建模为多视图学习问题，集成编码器模型（语义信息）与解码器模型（高阶推理信息）；采用基于主观逻辑的认知学习框架显式建模不确定性，并提出认知融合策略，在融合互补视图时对不可靠证据进行折扣处理。

Result: 在Dreaddit、SDCNL、DepSeverity三个真实数据集上分别取得0.835、0.731、0.751的准确率；额外实验证明该方法在噪声鲁棒性、不确定性估计可信度和可解释性方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 提出的框架不仅提升了预测性能，还能提供可信的不确定性估计和人类可理解的推理信号，适用于心理健康评估等风险敏感应用场景。

Abstract: Automated mental health prediction using textual data has shown promising results with deep learning and large language models. However, deploying these models in high-stakes real-world settings remains challenging, as existing approaches largely rely on semantic representations and often produce overconfident predictions under ambiguous, noisy, or shifted data. Moreover, most methods lack reliable uncertainty estimation, undermining trust in risk-sensitive mental health applications. To address these limitations, we formulate the task as a multi-view learning problem that integrates semantic information from encoder-only models with higher-level reasoning information from decoder-only models, where reasoning-aware representations and uncertainty modeling are obtained in a trustworthy manner. To ensure reliable fusion, we adopt an evidential learning framework based on Subjective Logic to explicitly model uncertainty and introduce an evidential fusion strategy that balances complementary views while discounting unreliable evidence. Benchmarking on three real-world datasets, Dreaddit, SDCNL, and DepSeverity, reports accuracies of 0.835, 0.731, and 0.751, respectively, demonstrating its potential for reliable mental health prediction. Additional experiments on robustness to noise and case studies for interpretability confirm that our proposed framework not only improves predictive performance but also provides trustworthy uncertainty estimates and human-understandable reasoning signals, making it suitable for risk-sensitive applications in mental health assessment.

</details>


### [130] [PSK at SemEval-2026 Task 9: Multilingual Polarization Detection Using Ensemble Gemma Models with Synthetic Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05159)
*Srikar Kashyap Pulipaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提交至SemEval-2026任务9：多语言极化检测系统。采用针对每种语言分别微调的Gemma 3模型（12B和27B参数），结合LoRA和LLM生成的合成数据，通过逐语言阈值调优和加权集成策略，在22种语言上取得0.811的平均宏F1分数，排名第二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言极化检测任务（SemEval-2026 Task 9）涵盖22种语言，需要构建一个跨语言泛化能力强的二分类系统，以应对不同语言的极化表达差异。

Method: 使用Gemma 3（12B和27B）为每种语言分别微调，采用LoRA进行参数高效微调；利用GPT-4o-mini通过三种策略（直接生成、释义、对比对创建）生成合成数据，并经多阶段质量过滤（含基于嵌入的去重）；在开发集上逐语言调优阈值，无需重新训练；构建12B与27B模型预测的加权集成，并按语言选择最优策略。

Result: 在22种语言上平均宏F1达到0.811，总排名第二；在3种语言中获得第一，8种语言进入前三。逐语言阈值调优带来2-4%F1提升。替代架构（XLM-RoBERTa、Qwen3）在测试集上F1下降30-50%，说明泛化挑战显著。

Conclusion: 基于逐语言微调的Gemma 3+LoRA方法在多语言极化检测中表现优异；合成数据策略和阈值调优有效；不同架构在训练集与测试集间的泛化差距需要重点关注。

Abstract: We present our system for SemEval-2026 Task 9: Multilingual Polarization Detection, a binary classification task spanning 22 languages. Our approach fine-tunes separate Gemma~3 models (12B and 27B parameters) per language using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), augmented with synthetic data generated by a large language model (LLM). We employ three synthetic data strategies (direct generation, paraphrasing, and contrastive pair creation) using GPT-4o-mini, with a multi-stage quality filtering pipeline including embedding-based deduplication. We find that per-language threshold tuning on the development set yields 2 to 4\% F1 improvements without retraining. We also use weighted ensembles of 12B and 27B model predictions with per-language strategy selection. Our final system achieves a mean macro-F1 of 0.811 across all 22 languages, ranking 2nd overall of the participating teams, with 1st place finishes in 3 languages and top-3 in 8 languages. We also find that alternative architectures (XLM-RoBERTa, Qwen3) that showed strong development set performance suffered 30 to 50\% F1 drops on the test set, highlighting the importance of generalization.

</details>


### [131] [The First Token Knows: Single-Decode Confidence for Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05166)
*Mina Gabriel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于首个标记置信度（phi_first）的方法，通过单次贪婪解码中首个内容性答案标记的归一化熵来评估模型不确定性，在闭卷短答案事实验证问答中，其性能匹配或略优于需要多次采样和语义聚类的语义自一致性方法，且计算成本极低，可作为默认基线使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自一致性方法需重复解码且对词汇变化敏感，语义自一致性虽改进但增加了采样和外部推理开销。作者希望探索能否从单次解码的初始标记分布中获取足够的不确定性信息，从而避免多重采样的高成本。

Method: 在单次贪婪解码过程中，取首个内容性答案标记的top-K logits，计算归一化熵作为置信度指标phi_first。在三个7-8B指令微调模型和两个基准数据集上，将该指标与表面形式自一致性（surface-form self-consistency）和语义自一致性（semantic self-consistency）进行对比评估。

Result: phi_first的平均AUROC为0.820，语义一致性为0.793，表面形式自一致性为0.791。子集包含检验显示phi_first与语义一致性存在中等到强相关性，二者组合仅带来微小的AUROC提升。

Conclusion: 多采样一致性所捕获的不确定性信息在很大程度上已隐含于模型的初始标记分布中。建议将phi_first作为默认的低成本基线指标，在采用基于采样的不确定性估计方法前先行报告。

Abstract: Self-consistency detects hallucinations by generating multiple sampled answers to a question and measuring agreement, but this requires repeated decoding and can be sensitive to lexical variation. Semantic self-consistency improves this by clustering sampled answers by meaning using natural language inference, but it adds both sampling cost and external inference overhead. We show that first-token confidence, phi_first, computed from the normalized entropy of the top-K logits at the first content-bearing answer token of a single greedy decode, matches or modestly exceeds semantic self-consistency on closed-book short-answer factual question answering. Across three 7-8B instruction-tuned models and two benchmarks, phi_first achieves a mean AUROC of 0.820, compared with 0.793 for semantic agreement and 0.791 for standard surface-form self-consistency. A subsumption test shows that phi_first is moderately to strongly correlated with semantic agreement, and combining the two signals yields only a small AUROC improvement over phi_first alone. These results suggest that much of the uncertainty information captured by multi-sample agreement is already available in the model's initial token distribution. We argue that phi_first should be reported as a default low-cost baseline before invoking sampling-based uncertainty estimation.

</details>


### [132] [Implicit Representations of Grammaticality in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05197)
*Yingshan Susan Wang,Linlu Qiu,Zhaofeng Wu,Roger P. Levy,Yoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过线性探针实验表明，预训练语言模型在隐藏层中隐式编码了与字符串概率不同的语法性区分能力，该探针在语法判断基准上优于基于概率的方法，但在语义合理性任务上表现较弱，并展现出跨语言泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究预训练语言模型是否在其内部表征中隐式习得了与字符串概率相独立的语法性区分能力，而非仅依赖于概率分布进行语法判断。

Method: 在自然语料库上通过扰动生成语法和（合成）非语法句子数据集，训练线性探针读取LM隐藏层表示，并在人工标注的语法判断基准和语义合理性基准上评估探针性能，同时进行跨语言泛化测试。

Result: 语法性探针在语法判断基准上优于基于LM概率的判断方法；在语义合理性基准上表现不如字符串概率；英语训练的探针在多种语言的语法性基准上展现出非平凡跨语言泛化；探针得分与字符串概率仅弱相关。

Conclusion: 预训练语言模型在某种程度上在其隐藏层中习得了隐式的语法性区分，这种区分独立于字符串概率。

Abstract: Grammaticality and likelihood are distinct notions in human language. Pretrained language models (LMs), which are probabilistic models of language fitted to maximize corpus likelihood, generate grammatically well-formed text and discriminate well between grammatical and ungrammatical sentences in tightly controlled minimal pairs. However, their string probabilities do not sharply discriminate between grammatical and ungrammatical sentences overall. But do LMs implicitly acquire a grammaticality distinction distinct from string probability? We explore this question through studying internal representations of LMs, by training a linear probe on a dataset of grammatical and (synthetic) ungrammatical sentences obtained by applying perturbations to a naturalistic text corpus. We find that this simple grammaticality probe generalizes to human-curated grammaticality judgment benchmarks and outperforms LM probability-based grammaticality judgments. When applied to semantic plausibility benchmarks, in which both members of a minimal pair are grammatical and differ in only plausibility, the probe however performs worse than string probability. The English-trained probe also exhibits nontrivial cross-lingual generalization, outperforming string probabilities on grammaticality benchmarks in numerous other languages. Additionally, probe scores correlate only weakly with string probabilities. These results collectively suggest that LMs acquire to some extent an implicit grammaticality distinction within their hidden layers.

</details>


### [133] [The Reasoning Trap: An Information-Theoretic Bound on Closed-System Multi-Step LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01704)
*Kwan Soo Shin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"辩论陷阱"与"推理陷阱"概念，证明多智能体辩论（MAD）等闭环推理系统在保持答案准确性的同时会系统性退化推理质量。通过信息论中的数据处理不等式（DPI）证明该退化在马尔可夫结构下是必然的，并提出基于证据的苏格拉底式推理（EGSR）作为解决方案。在SciFact和FEVER数据集上的实验表明，EGSR可恢复98%的推理忠实度，而MAD将忠实度降至基准的1.7%。此外，人类评估者间一致性本身不稳定（Fleiss kappa <= +0.018），质疑了当前忠实度指标的校准基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明多智能体辩论能提升LLM推理准确性，但本文发现辩论过程中推理质量（而非答案准确性）会系统性退化，且人类评估的忠实度指标本身不可靠，亟需新的理论框架与评估方法。

Method: 提出SFS指标（分解原子声明并验证证据）、EGSR推理框架（替代对抗辩论的证据驱动查询），利用数据处理不等式证明马尔可夫闭环推理的信息论下界，通过16种实验条件在SciFact和FEVER上验证，并引入R6队列研究评估人类标注一致性。

Result: MAD保留88%准确率但SFS下降43%；多数投票MAD将SFS降至基准1.7%（p<10^{-6}, d=-0.96）；EGSR恢复98%忠实度。人类评估者间Fleiss kappa <= +0.018，信度不稳定。

Conclusion: 闭环推理系统的忠实度退化在信息论意义下是必然的（DPI界限），且当前忠实度指标的人类校准基础不可靠；提出一个可证伪猜想：任何保持马尔可夫结构的闭环推理协议都受相同DPI界限约束。

Abstract: When copies of the same language model are prompted to debate, they produce diverse phrasings of one perspective rather than diverse perspectives. Multi-agent debate (MAD), and more broadly closed-system reasoning where agents iteratively transform each other's outputs, tends to preserve answer accuracy while degrading the reasoning behind those answers. We name the multi-agent case the Debate Trap and the broader phenomenon the Reasoning Trap, offering a programmatic theory of evidence-grounded reasoning failure.The framework has three parts: (i) SFS (Supported Faithfulness Score), a claim-level metric verifying decomposed atomic claims against provided evidence (decomposer-invariant rankings: Spearman rho=1.0); (ii) EGSR (Evidence-Grounded Socratic Reasoning), replacing adversarial argumentation with evidence-grounded inquiry; (iii) Theorem 1 (DPI Bound): under standard MAD, the chain E -> O^0 -> O^1 -> ... is Markov, and the Data Processing Inequality implies E[I(E;O^{t+1})] <= E[I(E;O^t)]. Three companion results -- open-system recovery (Theorem 2), EGSR accumulation (Lemma 2), and vote-aggregation floor (Proposition 1) -- partition multi-step LLM reasoning by its information-theoretic relationship to E. Across 16 conditions on SciFact (300 claims) and FEVER (1,000 claims), DebateCV (C13) preserves 88% of baseline accuracy while SFS drops 43%; majority-vote MAD (C15) reduces SFS to 1.7% of baseline (p < 10^{-6}, d = -0.96); EGSR recovers 98%. An R6 cohort study (Korean n=10x30 FEVER; English n=3x200 SciFact) finds inter-rater Fleiss kappa <= +0.018 with 0.8-1.4 Likert intra-rater shifts across language and domain -- the human agreement that faithfulness metrics have been calibrated against is not itself stable. We offer one falsifiable conjecture: any closed-system reasoning protocol preserving Theorem 1's Markov structure is, in expectation, subject to the same DPI bound.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [Beyond Static Best-of-N: Bayesian List-wise Alignment for LLM-based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04559)
*Ruijun Chen,Chongming Gao,Jiawei Chen,Weiqin Yang,Xiangnan He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: BLADE提出了一种贝叶斯动态估计的列表级对齐框架，通过融合历史先验与当前模型rollouts的动态证据不断更新目标分布，克服了传统BoN对齐中静态参考导致的 indiscriminate supervision 和 gradient decay 问题，显著提升了推荐系统的排序准确性、公平性和多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM4Rec方法主要依赖token级目标，难以优化列表级且不可微的推荐指标（如NDCG、公平性）。虽然Best-of-N (BoN)可在推理时直接优化这些指标，但计算成本过高；BoN对齐方法试图将搜索能力蒸馏到模型中，却面临两个关键局限：(1) 静态参考无法区分超越其经验范围的候选质量，导致排序引导信息丢失；(2) 随着策略提升，有效监督信号快速衰减，优化效率低下。

Method: BLADE提出贝叶斯列表级对齐框架，核心机制是通过融合历史先验与模型当前rollouts的动态证据，持续更新目标分布，构建一个自演化的目标，使其随模型能力增长而自适应调整，确保训练信号在整个学习过程中保持信息量。

Result: 在三个真实数据集上的实验表明，BLADE显著优于最先进的基线方法，打破了静态性能上限，在排序准确性（Recall、NDCG）和复杂列表级指标（公平性、多样性）上均取得了持续增益。

Conclusion: BLADE通过动态估计的贝叶斯框架有效解决了静态BoN对齐中的监督区分度不足和梯度衰减问题，为LLM4Rec提供了一种能够持续优化列表级不可微指标的有效对齐方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models have revolutionized recommender systems (LLM4Rec) by leveraging their generative capabilities to model complex user preferences. However, existing LLM4Rec methods primarily rely on token-level objectives, making it difficult to optimize list-level and non-differentiable metrics (e.g., NDCG, fairness) that define actual recommendation quality. While Best-of-N (BoN) directly optimizes these metrics during inference, its high computational cost hinders real-world deployment. To address this, BoN Alignment aims to distill the search capability into the model itself, yet current approaches suffer from two critical limitations: (1) Indiscriminate Supervision, where the static reference fails to distinguish the relative quality of candidates exceeding its empirical range, leading to a loss of ranking guidance; and (2) Gradient Decay, where the effective supervision signal rapidly diminishes as the evolving policy improves, resulting in inefficient optimization.
  To overcome these challenges, we propose BLADE (Bayesian List-wise Alignment via Dynamic Estimation). Unlike static approaches, BLADE introduces a Bayesian framework that continuously updates the target distribution by fusing historical priors with dynamic evidence from the model's current rollouts. This mechanism constructs a self-evolving target that adapts to the model's growing capabilities, ensuring the training signal remains informative throughout the learning process. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate that BLADE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Crucially, it breaks the static performance upper bound, achieving sustained gains in both ranking accuracy (Recall, NDCG) and complex list-wise metrics (Fairness, Diversity). The code is available via https://github.com/RegionCh/BLADE.

</details>


### [135] [Rethinking Convolutional Networks for Attribute-Aware Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04723)
*Shereen Elsayed,Ngoc Son Le,Ahmed Rashed,Lars Schmidt-Thieme*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出ConvRec，一种基于卷积层分层下采样的线性复杂度序列推荐方法，在长序列处理上优于现有自注意力模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于自注意力的序列推荐模型计算复杂度和内存消耗高，难以处理长用户历史序列，且纯注意力机制在提取序列模式上并非最优。

Method: 采用卷积层进行分层下采样，以线性计算和内存复杂度生成紧凑且富有表达力的序列表示，每层逐步聚合邻近项目。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上优于现有最先进的序列推荐模型。

Conclusion: 基于卷积架构的序列建模在推荐系统中具有高效性和有效性潜力。

Abstract: Attribute-aware sequential recommendation entails predicting the next item a user will interact with based on a chronologically ordered history of past interactions, enriched with item attributes. Existing methods typically leverage self-attention mechanisms to aggregate the entire sequence into a unified representation used for next-item prediction. While effective, these models often suffer from high computational complexity and memory consumption, limiting their ability to process long user histories. This constraint restricts the model's capacity to fully capture long-term user preferences. In some scenarios, modeling item interactions purely through attention may also not be the most effective approach to extract sequential patterns. In this work, we propose ConvRec, an alternative method with linear computational and memory complexity that employs convolutional layers in a hierarchical, down-scaled fashion to generate compact, yet expressive sequence representations. To further enhance the model's ability to capture diverse sequential patterns, each layer aggregates the neighboring items gradually to reach a comprehensive sequence representation. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art sequential recommendation models, highlighting the potential of convolution-based architectures for efficient and effective sequence modeling in recommendation systems. Our implementation code and datasets are available here https://github.com/ismll-research/ConvRec.

</details>


### [136] [Beyond Seeing Is Believing: On Crowdsourced Detection of Audiovisual Deepfakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04797)
*Michael Soprano,Andrea Cioci,Stefano Mizzaro*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过众包实验研究人类对音视频深度伪造的检测能力，发现工人很少误判真实视频为伪造，但会漏掉大量伪造样本，且操纵类型识别（尤其是音视频联合伪造）表现显著差于真伪判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度伪造技术日益逼真且易于制作，引发对人工判断可靠性的担忧，有必要系统评估众包方式在音视频伪造检测中的有效性。

Method: 使用AV-Deepfake1M和Trusted Media Challenge两个数据集，各采样48个视频（共96个），在Prolific平台上开展匹配的众包研究，每视频收集10个判断（共960个判断），测量工人区分真伪、识别操纵类型（音频/视频/音视频）以及标注操纵时间戳的准确性。

Result: 工人很少将真实视频误判为伪造，但大量遗漏操纵视频；工人间一致性有限；聚合多人判断可稳定真伪信号，但无法挽回多数工人一致遗漏的操纵；操纵类型识别比真伪检测噪声大得多，音视频联合情况最难识别。

Conclusion: 众包可为音视频真实性提供可扩展的筛查信号，但可靠的模态归因仍是一个开放挑战。

Abstract: Deepfakes are increasingly realistic and easy to produce, raising concerns about the reliability of human judgments in misinformation settings. We study audiovisual deepfake detection by measuring how consistently crowd workers distinguish authentic from manipulated videos and, when they flag a video as manipulated, how accurately they identify the manipulation type (audio-only, video-only, or audio-video) and how consistently they report manipulation timestamps. We run two matched crowdsourcing studies on Prolific using AV-Deepfake1M and the Trusted Media Challenge (TMC) dataset. We sample 48 videos per dataset (96 total) and collect 960 judgments (10 per video). Results show that crowd workers rarely misclassify authentic videos as manipulated, but they miss many manipulations, and agreement remains limited across videos. Aggregating multiple judgments per video stabilizes the authenticity signal, but it cannot recover manipulations that most workers consistently miss. Manipulation type identification is substantially noisier than authenticity detection even when workers detect a manipulation, with joint audio-video cases being particularly hard to recognize. Overall, these findings suggest that crowdsourcing can provide a scalable screening signal for audiovisual authenticity, while reliable modality attribution remains an open challenge.

</details>


### [137] [CapsID: Soft-Routed Variable-Length Semantic IDs for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05096)
*Wenzhuo Cheng,Menghang Gong,Qixin Guo,Hang Zheng,Zhaobin Yang,Jianguo Lou,Zhengwei Zheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CAPSID用胶囊路由替代硬残差量化，SEMANTICBPE组合相邻SID令牌为子词，在生成式推荐中仅用单一表征即达到超越双表征方法的性能，并降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐中，tokenizer（残差向量量化）是主要瓶颈：硬最近邻分配导致边界语义坍缩，早期误差向后传播；双表征方案虽可缓解但增加推理成本且丧失生成式接口的简洁性。

Method: CAPSID：用胶囊路由替代硬残差量化，每层物品概率路由到多个语义胶囊，残差由路由重构更新而非单一胜出码，SID在活跃胶囊置信度足够高时终止。SEMANTICBPE：结合共现与嵌入兼容性，将相邻SID令牌组合成可复用子词。

Result: 在Amazon Beauty、Sports、Toys及35M工业数据集上，CAPSID+SEMANTICBPE相比最强单表征基线ReSID，Recall@10平均提升9.6%；匹配或超越COBRA风格稀疏-密集系统，且推理延迟仅为51%。

Conclusion: 软路由、迭代一致性和置信度驱动长度各自独立贡献效果，在尾部物品（边界语义主导）上增益最大，验证了直接在tokenizer层面解决瓶颈的有效性。

Abstract: Generative recommendation maps each item to a sequence of Semantic IDs (SIDs) and recasts retrieval as autoregressive token generation. In this paradigm the main bottleneck is the tokenizer rather than the Transformer: residual vector quantization with a hard nearest-neighbor assignment at every layer collapses multi-faceted item semantics at cluster boundaries and propagates early errors to later SID positions. A common workaround is to append a dense vector or attribute prefix to the SID, but this dual-representation design inflates inference cost and gives up the simplicity of a generative interface. We address the bottleneck at the tokenizer itself. CAPSID replaces hard residual quantization with capsule routing: at each layer an item probabilistically routes to several semantic capsules, the residual is updated by the routed reconstruction rather than by a single winning code, and the SID terminates once the active capsule's confidence is high enough. On top of CAPSID, SEMANTICBPE composes adjacent SID tokens into reusable subwords by combining their co-occurrence with their embedding compatibility. On Amazon Beauty, Sports, Toys, and a 35M-item proprietary industrial catalog, CAPSID+SEMANTICBPE improves Recall at 10 by 9.6% on average over ReSID, the strongest single-representation baseline, and matches or exceeds a COBRA-style sparse-dense system on every public benchmark while running at 51% of its inference latency. Ablations show that soft routing, iterative agreement, and confidence-driven length each contribute independently, and the gains are largest on tail items where boundary semantics dominate.

</details>


### [138] [Interests Burn-down Diffusion Process for Personalized Collaborative Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05165)
*Yifang Qin,Zhaobin Li,Arisa Watanabe,Wei Ju,Zhiping Xiao,Ming Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为兴趣燃尽过程（interests burn-down process）的新型扩散方案，专门为交互系统设计，用于建模用户兴趣的衰减，并通过逆过程（兴趣燃起过程）生成个性化推荐。基于此过程提出的StageCF方法在协同过滤任务中优于现有生成式和扩散基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散生成模型在协同过滤任务中使用传统高斯噪声扩散过程，与用户个性化交互行为的微妙特性不匹配，导致次优结果。因此需要一种专门针对交互系统定制的扩散方案。

Method: 提出兴趣燃尽过程（interests burn-down process）：正向过程建模用户对候选项兴趣的衰减（燃尽），反向过程（兴趣燃起过程）生成个性化推荐。基于此设计了StageCF推荐方法。

Result: 实验表明StageCF在生成式和扩散基线方法中表现优异，且验证了兴趣燃尽过程生成个性化交互的能力。

Conclusion: 兴趣燃尽过程比传统高斯噪声扩散更适合协同过滤任务，StageCF方法有效且具有优越性。

Abstract: Generative methods have gained widespread attention in Collaborative Filtering (CF) tasks for their ability to produce high-quality personalized samples aligned with users' interests. Among them, diffusion generative models have raised increasing attention in recommendation field. Despite that the pioneering efforts have applied the conventional diffusion process to model diffusive user interests, the incongruity between the Gaussian noise and the subtle nature of user's personalized interaction behavior has led to sub-optimal results. To this end, we introduce a specifically-tailored diffusion scheme for interaction systems, namely the interests burn-down process. The interests burn-down process delineates the decay of user interests towards candidate items, complemented by its reverse burn-up process that yields personalized recommendation for users. The inherent burn-down nature of this process adeptly models the diffusive user interests, aligning seamlessly with the requirements of CF tasks. We present a novel recommendation method StageCF to illustrate the superiority of this newly proposed diffusion process. Experimental results have demonstrated the effectiveness of StageCF against existing generative and diffusion-based baseline methods. Furthermore, comprehensive studies validate the functionality of interests burn-down process, shedding light on its capacity to generate personalized interactions.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [Endogenous Regime Switching Driven by Scalar-Irreducible Learning Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04054)
*Sheng Ran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出标量可约与标量不可约动力学的分类，证明标量不可约动力学通过快速变量与缓慢结构适应间的反馈可实现内源性机制切换，为自主智能系统提供新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现内源性机制切换是自主智能涌现的关键，但现有机器学习框架中此类转换通常由外部强制施加，缺乏内部自主切换能力。

Method: 提出标量可约（可表示为标量目标驱动的梯度流）与标量不可约动力学的分类，通过最小动力学模型展示快速变量与缓慢结构适应之间的反馈机制如何产生持续的内源性机制转换。

Result: 标量不可约动力学能够自然产生内部生成的机制切换，无需外部调度即可实现持续的内源性机制转换。

Conclusion: 该工作为机制探索提供了新的动力学范式，为构建自适应行为由内部组织而非外部规定的自主学习系统铺平了道路。

Abstract: Achieving endogenous regime switching is crucial for the emergence of autonomous intelligence, yet remains a central challenge for existing machine learning frameworks, where such transitions are typically externally imposed. In this work, we introduce a classification that distinguishes scalar-reducible dynamics, which can be expressed as gradient flows driven by a scalar objective, from scalar-irreducible dynamics that cannot be reduced to such a form. While most existing machine learning systems operate within the scalar-reducible class, we demonstrate that scalar-irreducible dynamics naturally enable internally generated regime switching through feedback between fast dynamical variables and slow structural adaptation. Using a minimal dynamical model, we illustrate how this mechanism produces sustained endogenous regime transitions without external scheduling. Our results suggest a new dynamical paradigm for regime exploration and provide a potential route toward autonomous learning systems whose adaptive behavior is organized internally rather than externally prescribed.

</details>


### [140] [A Self-Attentive Meta-Optimizer with Group-Adaptive Learning Rates and Weight Decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04055)
*JiangBo Zhao,ZhaoXin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MetaAdamW优化器，通过自注意力机制动态调整各参数组的学习率和权重衰减，在五个任务上超越AdamW基线，最高提升11.08%性能或减少17.11%训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AdamW等自适应优化器对所有参数组使用统一超参数，忽略了不同层和模块间异构的优化动态，限制了模型性能。

Method: MetaAdamW包含：(1) 轻量Transformer编码器基于梯度范数、动量范数、相关性等统计特征生成注意力调制因子，动态调整每组的超参数；(2) 结合梯度对齐、损失下降和泛化差距的元学习目标训练注意力模块；(3) 扩展同方差不确定性加权，引入任务特定优先级直接缩放正则项，实现领域知识引导的自动损失平衡。

Result: 在时间序列预测(ETT)、语言建模(WikiText-2)、机器翻译(Multi30k)、图像分类(CIFAR-10)、情感分析(IMDB)五个任务上，MetaAdamW一致优于AdamW，最高降低17.11%训练时间或提升11.08%性能，且仅引入适度开销。

Conclusion: MetaAdamW通过动态超参数调制有效缓解了AdamW的局限性，消融实验验证了各组件（特征版本、分组策略、优先级注入不确定性加权）的有效性，且能在过早停止场景下缓解收敛不充分问题。

Abstract: Adaptive optimizers like AdamW apply uniform hyperparameters across all parameter groups, ignoring heterogeneous optimization dynamics across layers and modules. We address this limitation by proposing MetaAdamW - a new optimizer that integrates a self-attention mechanism to dynamically modulate per-group learning rates and weight decay. The modulation factors are produced by a lightweight Transformer encoder that operates on statistical features (gradient norms, momentum norms, correlations) extracted from each parameter group. To train the attention module, we introduce a meta-learning objective that combines gradient alignment, loss decrease, and generalization gap. A key novel contribution is the extension of homoscedastic uncertainty weighting (HUW) with task-specific priorities that directly scale the regularization terms - enabling domain knowledge to guide automatic loss balancing. Extensive experiments on five diverse tasks-time series forecasting (ETT), language modeling (WikiText-2), machine translation (Multi30k), image classification (CIFAR-10), and sentiment analysis (IMDB) - demonstrate that MetaAdamW consistently outperforms the standard AdamW baseline in terms of validation loss, accuracy, or perplexity. Depending on the task, MetaAdamW either reduces overall training time (by up to 17.11%) or improves performance (by up to 11.08%) while introducing only moderate overhead; in some cases, it can also mitigate issues of insufficient convergence caused by premature early stopping. Ablation studies validate the effectiveness of each component, including feature versions, grouping strategies, and the proposed priority-injected uncertainty weighting.

</details>


### [141] [Transformation Categorization Based on Group Decomposition Theory Using Parameter Division](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04056)
*Takayuki Komatsu,Yoshiyuki Ohmura,Yasuo Kuniyoshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于群论的无监督变换分类方法，通过参数分割和同态约束来学习变换的分解结构，无需辅助假设（如运动或等距限制），在旋转、平移和缩放等图像变换上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督表示学习方法（如解耦表征）在处理因子耦合时失效，且基于Galois理论的方法依赖额外的辅助假设（如运动和等距限制），不能纯粹反映出分解理论的效果。作者希望提出一种更通用、无需辅助假设的分解方法。

Method: 对单一变换的参数进行分割，将其参数分为多个分量；施加同态约束，将完整变换映射到其中一个分量；定义正规子群为当该分量固定为单位元时所有变换的集合。该方法丢弃了之前方法中的运动和等距等辅助假设。

Result: 在包含旋转、平移和缩放的图像对上进行评估，消融实验表明群分解约束驱动了恰当的变换分类。

Conclusion: 所提出的参数分割方法在更一般的场景下实现了无监督的变换分解，且不依赖辅助假设，消融实验证实了群论约束在分类中的关键作用。

Abstract: Representation learning seeks meaningful sensory representations without supervision and can model aspects of human development. Although many neural networks empirically learn useful features, a principled account of what makes a representation "good" remains elusive. We study unsupervised categorization of transformations between pairs of inputs under algebraic constraints. Classical disentanglement favors mutually independent factors and fails when factors are coupled. Our prior Galois-theoretic approach decomposes a group via normal subgroups by learning a product of two transformations with one factor constrained to a normal subgroup, covering both commutative and non-commutative cases. That method, however, relied on auxiliary assumptions (e.g., motion and isometry restrictions) not required by decomposition theory, and ablations did not separate theory-based from auxiliary effects. We propose parameter division for a single transformation: we split its parameter into components, impose homomorphism constraints mapping the full transformation to one component, and identify the normal subgroup as the set of transformations when that component is fixed to the identity. This formulation drops the previous auxiliary assumptions and applies more broadly. We evaluate on image pairs involving rotation, translation, and scale; ablations show that group-decomposition constraints drive appropriate categorization.

</details>


### [142] [Structured Progressive Knowledge Activation for LLM-Driven Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04057)
*Zhen Liu,Yuhan Liu,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPARK方法，通过因子条件化编辑解决NAS中LLM辅助架构搜索时的功能纠缠问题，在CLRS-DFS上实现28.1倍样本效率提升和22.9%的OOD准确率相对改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM辅助NAS时，局部编辑往往因功能纠缠（functional entanglement）导致非局部的行为和性能偏移，使得LLM的架构知识难以有效利用，亟需一种能隔离编辑影响、激活相关先验的方法。

Method: 提出结构化渐进知识激活（SPARK），通过显式选择待修改的功能因子（functional factor），并基于该因子进行条件化编辑（factor-conditioned editing），从而减少纠缠带来的副作用，实现更精准、可靠的架构修改。

Result: 在CLRS-DFS数据集上，SPARK实现28.1倍的样本效率提升（架构进化速度），以及22.9%的OOD准确率相对改进。

Conclusion: 因子条件化编辑能有效解耦LLM辅助NAS中的功能纠缠问题，为利用LLM先验知识进行高效、可靠的架构搜索提供了可行范式。

Abstract: This paper focuses on a key challenge in Neural Architecture Search (NAS): integrating established architectural knowledge while exploring new designs under expensive evaluations. Large language models (LLMs) are a promising assistant for NAS because they can translate rich architectural and coding priors into executable code edits. However, in practice, seemingly local revisions often propagate into non-local behavioral and performance shifts because a single edit can inadvertently couple multiple interacting functional factors, a phenomenon we refer to as functional entanglement. To make LLM knowledge usable under such entanglement, we propose Structured Progressive Knowledge Activation (SPARK), which activates relevant priors by explicitly selecting the functional factor to modify and conditioning the edit on that factor. This factor-conditioned editing reduces entangled side effects and yields more targeted, reliable architecture modifications. On CLRS-DFS, SPARK achieves a 28.1x sample-efficient architecture evolution speedup and yields a 22.9 percent relative improvement in OOD accuracy.

</details>


### [143] [Continual Distillation of Teachers from Different Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04059)
*Nicolas Michel,Maorong Wang,Jiangpeng He,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出持续蒸馏(CD)范式，让学生模型从教师模型流中顺序学习，无需保留早期教师。针对无教师训练数据和教师专长异质两大挑战，证明了外部无标签数据可实现未见知识迁移(UKT)，同时发现顺序蒸馏会导致未见知识遗忘(UKF)。提出SE2D方法，通过保留外部数据上的logits来平衡UKT和UKF，实验表明该方法能减少遗忘并提升跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着深度学习模型规模持续扩大，模型存储需求甚至超过大型数据集，亟需一种无需保留原始教师模型即可持续学习的新范式。

Method: 提出持续蒸馏(CD)范式；利用外部无标签数据实现未见知识迁移(UKT)；提出SE2D（Self External Data Distillation）方法，通过保留外部数据上的logits来稳定跨异构教师的学习过程。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，SE2D能够减少未见知识遗忘(UKF)并提升跨域泛化性能。

Conclusion: 持续蒸馏是一种可行的新范式，SE2D能有效平衡UKT与UKF之间的权衡，实现更好的知识继承与泛化。

Abstract: Deep learning models continue to scale, with some requiring more storage than many large-scale datasets. Thus, we introduce a new paradigm: Continual Distillation (CD), where a student learns sequentially from a stream of teacher models without retaining access to earlier teachers. CD faces two challenges: teacher training data is unavailable, and teachers have varying expertise. We show that external unlabeled data enables Unseen Knowledge Transfer (UKT), allowing the student to acquire information from domains not present in the training data, while known to the teacher. We also show that sequential distillation causes Unseen Knowledge Forgetting (UKF) when transferred knowledge is lost after training on later teachers. To better trade off between UKT and UKF, we propose Self External Data Distillation (SE2D), a method that preserves logits on external data to stabilize learning across heterogeneous teachers. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that SE2D reduces UKF and improves cross-domain generalization. The code and implementation for this work are publicly available at: https://github.com/Nicolas1203/continual_distillation.

</details>


### [144] [Lookahead Drifting Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04060)
*Guoqiang Zhang,Kenta Niwa,W. Bastiaan Kleijn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种前瞻漂移模型（Lookahead Drifting Model），通过在每次训练迭代中顺序计算多个漂移项并利用加权求和来优化模型，相较于原始漂移模型在玩具数据和CIFAR10上取得了更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漂移模型（Drifting Model）作为一种新的分布映射范式，在ImageNet上通过一步神经函数评估（NFE）实现了SOTA图像生成性能。本文旨在进一步提升该方法的性能，通过引入前瞻机制捕捉高阶梯度信息。

Method: 在每个训练迭代中顺序计算一组漂移项，每个漂移项利用之前计算的漂移项、正样本和模型输出共同计算；最后将模型输出推向这些漂移项的加权求和方向。

Result: 在玩具示例和CIFAR10数据集上的实验表明，所提出的方法优于基线漂移模型。

Conclusion: 前瞻漂移模型通过多步漂移项的计算与加权求和，有效捕捉了高阶梯度信息，从而提升了分布映射的性能。

Abstract: Recently, a new paradigm named \emph{drifting model} has been proposed for mapping distributions, which achieves the SOTA image generation performance over ImageNet via one-step neural functional evaluation (NFE). The basic idea is to compute a drifting term at each training iteration and then push the output of the model towards the direction of the drifting term. In this paper, we propose a \emph{lookahead drifting model}. At each training iteration, we compute a set of drifting terms sequentially. Each drifting term is calculated by making use of previously computed ones as well as the positive samples and the output of the model. %One key step is to properly scale the drifting terms so that their magnitudes are in a comparable range. In principle, the drifting terms obtained at a later stage capture higher order gradient information towards the positive samples. At each training iteration, the model is optimized by pushing its output towards the direction of the (weighted) summation of the drifting terms. Experimental results on toy examples and CIFAR10 demonstrate the better performance of the new method than the baseline.

</details>


### [145] [EdgeRazor: A Lightweight Framework for Large Language Models via Mixed-Precision Quantization-Aware Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04062)
*Shu-Hao Zhang,Le-Tong Huang,Xiang-Sheng Deng,Xin-Yi Zou,Chen Wu,Nan Li,Shao-Qun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EdgeRazor提出了一种轻量级混合精度与极低位权重量化框架，通过混合精度量化感知蒸馏、自适应特征蒸馏和熵感知KL散度三个模块，在1.88-bit精度下超越所有3-bit方法，比领先的2-bit PTQ方法高出11.3个点，训练开销仅为领先QAT方法的1/4到1/10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有权重量化方法在低位宽（低于4-bit）下性能严重下降，而QAT方法计算资源消耗巨大，量化感知蒸馏则依赖于手动特征选择和教师特定数据，亟需一种轻量级且高效的极低位宽量化框架。

Method: EdgeRazor包含三个模块：1) 混合精度量化感知蒸馏，实现细粒度精度控制；2) 自适应特征蒸馏，从16-bit教师模型导出n-bit学生模型；3) 熵感知KL散度，在人工标注和蒸馏数据集上仅由教师输出分布决定前向-反向平衡。

Result: 1.88-bit EdgeRazor超越所有3-bit竞争者，比2-bit PTQ方法高出11.3个点，训练预算仅为领先QAT方法的1/4-1/10；1.58-bit Qwen3-0.6B将存储从1.41GB降至0.28GB，解码加速15.1倍。

Conclusion: EdgeRazor在极低位宽下实现了高压缩比和高性能，兼顾了精度与效率，适用于资源受限设备上的LLM部署。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed an increasing interest in deploying LLMs on resource-constrained devices, among which quantization has emerged as a promising lightweight technique that converts full-precision model weights and activations into lower-bit formats. Existing weight quantization approaches can be roughly divided into three categories: Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) that calibrates quantized parameters on a small dataset without retraining but suffers from severe performance degradation below 4-bit, Quantization-Aware Training (QAT) that searches low-bit parameters using surrogate gradients but demands substantial computational resources, and Quantization-Aware Distillation that integrates QAT with knowledge transfer from a full-precision teacher but manually selects features to distill and relies heavily on teacher-specific data. In this paper, we propose EdgeRazor, a lightweight framework for LLMs with mixed-precision and extremely low-bit weight quantization. The EdgeRazor framework contains three modules: Mixed-Precision Quantization-Aware Distillation for the fine-grained control of precision, Adaptive Feature Distillation that derives an $n$-bit student from its 16-bit teacher, and Entropy-Aware KL Divergence on both human-annotated and distilled datasets, whose forward-reverse balance is determined solely by the teacher's output distribution. Empirical investigations of EdgeRazor are conducted on base, instruction-tuned, and multimodal LLMs. Notably, EdgeRazor with 1.88-bit surpasses all contenders with the 3-bit precision, especially outperforms the leading 2-bit PTQ methods by 11.3 points, within a 4-10$\times$ lower training budget than the leading QAT approach. EdgeRazor delivers higher compression ratios at all bit width; the 1.58-bit Qwen3-0.6B reduces storage from 1.41 GB to 0.28 GB while accelerating decoding by 15.1$\times$ relative to the 16-bit baseline.

</details>


### [146] [Investigating Trustworthiness of Nonparametric Deep Survival Models for Alzheimer's Disease Progression Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04063)
*Jacob Thrasher,Kaitlyn Heintzelman,Peter Martone,David Kotlowski,Binod Bhattarai,Donald Adjeroh,Prashnna Gyawali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了阿尔茨海默病进展分析中的公平性问题，发现深度学习生存模型虽然有效，但常表现出对性别、种族、教育等群体的显著偏见，并提出了两种新的公平性度量指标（时间依赖一致性杂质和Kaplan-Meier公平性）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病进展分析中，现有深度学习生存模型虽表现优异，但鲜有研究关注模型内部的学习偏差，这可能导致对某些边缘群体的不公平和不可靠预测。

Method: 对阿尔茨海默病进展分析进行严格的公平性研究和特征重要性分析，并提出两种新型公平性度量指标：Time-Dependent Concordance Impurity（时间依赖一致性杂质）和Kaplan-Meier Fairness（Kaplan-Meier公平性），用于量化非参数生存模型中关于性别、种族、教育等敏感属性的偏差。

Result: 深度学习驱动的生存模型虽能辅助临床AD护理决策，但往往表现出显著的偏见，这为未来研究指明了重要方向。

Conclusion: 深度学习生存模型在AD进展分析中存在不可忽视的公平性问题，需要进一步研究以缓解对边缘群体的预测偏差。

Abstract: Alzheimer's Dementia (AD) is a progressive neurodegenerative disease marked by irreversible decline, making reliable modeling of its progression essential for effective patient care. Progression-aware methods such as survival analysis are therefore crucial tools for the early detection and monitoring of AD. Recent advancements in deep learning have demonstrated remarkable performance in survival tasks, but alarmingly fewer studies have been conducted in the domain of AD. Further, the studies that do exist do not consider learned bias within the model itself, which could result in unfair and unreliable predictions toward certain marginalized groups. As such, we conduct a rigorous study of fairness in AD progression analysis along with a thorough feature importance study to determine the characteristics which are most important for reliable AD predictions. Furthermore, we propose two novel fairness metrics, called Time-Dependent Concordance Impurity and Kaplan-Meier Fairness, to quantify bias with respect to sensitive attributes such as sex, race, and education in nonparametric survival models. Our study demonstrates that while deep learning powered survival models are robust tools which can aid clinicians in AD care decisions, they often exhibit considerable bias, representing important avenues for future research.

</details>


### [147] [Improving Medical VQA through Trajectory-Aware Process Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04064)
*Halil Ibrahim Gulluk,Olivier Gevaert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两阶段训练框架（SFT + GRPO），利用COMCTS算法生成医学VQA推理轨迹，并引入基于DTW距离的过程奖励来优化推理能力，在六个基准上将准确率从0.598提升至0.689。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
