*Nicholas J. Cooper,François G. Meyer,Michael L. Roberts,Carlos Zapata-Carratalá,Lijun Chen,Danna Gurari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一理论框架，通过显式建模张量运算结构，分析深度学习的架构复杂度演化，并自动构建基于新型张量运算的创新架构。发现了40年来突破性架构与复杂度增长的联系，识别出多类未探索的高复杂度架构，并开源3000+架构数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习理论缺乏对张量运算底层结构的显式建模，导致无法系统分析架构复杂度的演化规律，也无法自动构建基于新型张量运算的创新架构。

Method: 构建统一理论框架，显式建模张量运算的低层结构信息，基于此框架分析过去40年深度学习的架构复杂度演化，并识别未探索的高复杂度架构类别。

Result: 揭示了突破性架构与不同类型架构复杂度增长之间的关联；识别出多个尚未被探索的高复杂度架构类别；收集并公开了3000+高复杂度架构数据集。

Conclusion: 该框架填补了深度学习理论在张量运算底层结构建模方面的空白，可用于分析历史架构演化规律并自动构建新型架构，为未来更高复杂度的深度学习架构设计提供了系统化路径。

Abstract: We introduce a unified theoretical framework for the rigorous analysis and systematic construction of deep neural networks (DNNs). This framework addresses a gap in existing theory by explicitly modeling the structure of tensor operations -- lower level information that is often abstracted. Our framework enables two novel objectives: (1) analysis of the evolution of architectural complexity over deep learning history, and (2) automatic construction of novel architectures based on new types of tensor operations. Our study of DNNs introduced over the past 40 years reveals a connection between groundbreaking architectures and increases in different types of architectural complexity. Moreover, we identify several large classes of higher complexity architectures that have not yet been explored. We then collect a dataset of 3,000+ higher complexity architectures, which we publicly release at: https://github.com/combinatoriallabs/ArchitecturalComplexity.

</details>


### [183] [Learning-based Statistical Refinement for Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04332)
*Rihuan Ke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于学习的统计精炼方法，在未知噪声分布且无干净图像或校准数据的情况下，利用噪声数据的统计信息提升给定去噪器的结果质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去噪方法通常需要精确的图像和噪声建模，但在实际中因模型不完美、噪声假设不可靠或数据质量低导致结果次优；尤其在无干净样本且未知噪声分布时，去噪结果与噪声统计特性不一致。

Method: 基于贝叶斯框架构建辅助信号，在噪声条件像素独立假设下，无需精确噪声分布信息即可评估去噪结果与噪声统计的一致性，并通过统计精炼提升去噪质量。

Result: 所提方法能够增强去噪结果与噪声统计的一致性，提升去噪质量。

Conclusion: 在无干净图像和噪声分布先验的受限条件下，利用噪声数据本身的统计信息可有效改进去噪性能。

Abstract: This work proposes a learning-based statistical refinement method for improving the denoising results of a given denoiser without knowing the precise noise distribution or accessing clean images or calibration data. While there are many existing successful denoising approaches for handling different kinds of noise, they typically require accurate modelling of the images and the noise (implicitly or explicitly), and hence the denoising results can be suboptimal due to different practical factors such as imperfect models, unreliable noise assumptions, or low quality data. In particular, when clean image samples are not available and there is a lack of knowledge of the underlying noise distribution, which is the case in various practical situations, the results may not well align with the noise statistics. The unawareness of the useful statistical information leads to suboptimal results. This work aims to make the best use of the statistical information to improve the consistency between the given denoising results and the noise statistics, under the assumption that the noise is conditionally pixel-wise independent given the clean signal. A method, based on a Bayesian formulation of an auxiliary signal in the noisy data, is proposed for evaluating the consistency of the denoising results, without precise information on noise distribution. By leveraging the statistical information from noisy data, the method enhances the statistical noise consistency and improves denoising quality.

</details>


### [184] [Structural Equivalence and Learning Dynamics in Delayed MARL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04345)
*Jules Sintes,Ana Bušić,Jiamin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了部分可观测多智能体系统中观测延迟与动作延迟之间的形式化等价关系，证明两者在解空间上同构，但实际学习动态存在本质差异，并利用该等价性实现了零样本策略迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作仅将无限时域单智能体场景下观测延迟与动作延迟的等价性推广到任意时域部分可观测合作多智能体问题，并统一延迟配置到纯观测延迟系统。

Method: 通过形式化证明建立OD与AD在Dec-POMDP中的等价性，利用观测-动作历史分析；在TI-MDP中证明局部增广状态的可计算性；通过数值实验对比两种延迟下的学习动态差异。

Result: OD与AD在解空间上结构同构，但实际学习动态因TD算法的因果信用分配错误和操作约束而不同；在非转移独立环境下等价性失效；成功实现从AD到OD的零样本策略迁移。

Conclusion: 延迟类型在结构上等价但学习动态不同，利用结构等价性可绕过学习挑战，为复杂延迟系统提供统一高效的求解方法。

Abstract: We formally establish the equivalence between Observation Delay (OD) and Action Delay (AD) in cooperative partially observable multi-agent systems using observation-action histories. We show that both systems generate identical admissible joint-policy sets, and their induced state-action-observation trajectories are identical in distribution, leading to identical optimal solutions in Decentralized Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (Dec-POMDPs). This formally generalizes existing infinite-horizon single-agent results to any-horizon partially observable cooperative multi-agent problems with decentralized policy execution, and allows any mixed-delay configuration to be reduced to a pure OD system. We further prove that in Transition-Independent MDPs (TI-MDPs), the observation-action history reduces to a tractable minimal local augmented state.
  However, we show through numerical experiments that although the optimal solution spaces are structurally isomorphic, the practical learning dynamics are fundamentally different. First, using the minimal local augmented state, the equivalence no longer holds when transitions are not independent. Second, operational constraints and causal credit-assignment errors in Temporal Difference (TD) algorithms induce different learning behaviors across regimes. Finally, leveraging this structural equivalence to bypass these learning challenges, we demonstrate successful multi-agent zero-shot policy transfer from OD to AD, paving the way for unified, efficient solution methods in complex delayed systems.

</details>


### [185] [Probing Structural Mathematical Reasoning in Language Models with Algebraic Trapdoors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04352)
*Igor Rivin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于SL(3, Z)子群构造问题的结构化数学推理基准测试集，利用密码学风格的验证者-证明者不对称性来评估语言模型。实验发现，一个前沿模型在指数变体问题上花费152分钟进行推理，明确识别出核侧成员问题为瓶颈，尝试构造性验证后选择了"不知道"而非提交不确定的余核候选——展示了在开放可判定性边界上的校准后元认知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试无法有效区分语言模型是否真正内化了代数先验知识（如Aschbacher类、McLaughlin定理、性质(T)、同余子群性质），还是仅依赖通用计算能力。作者希望构建一个能暴露模型在开放可判定性问题边界上行为差异的基准。

Method: 基于SL(3, Z)中的子群构造问题，构建验证者-证明者不对称性：构造时信息(N, K)可在O(1)闭形式下确定算术不变量（指数、素数满射或成员关系），但求解者缺乏该信息，必须通过Aschbacher分类分析或SL(3, Z)中可判定性未知的成员查询来推导。基准将模型行为分为四类：承诺正确、承诺错误、弃权正确、弃权错误。

Result: 在五个代表性推理轨迹上对两个最先进模型进行实验。关键结果：在一个指数变体问题上，一个模型花费152分钟推理，明确识别出核侧成员问题为瓶颈，尝试构造性验证，最终选择"不知道"而非提交其计算的余核候选——展示了校准后元认知能力。

Conclusion: 该基准暴露了标准答案评分混淆的四类模型行为分类（承诺正确、承诺错误、弃权正确、弃权错误），证明了评估模型在开放可判定性边界上的推理与元认知能力的必要性。

Abstract: We introduce a benchmark suite for evaluating structural mathematical reasoning in language models, built on subgroup-construction problems in SL(3, Z) with cryptographic-style verifier-prover asymmetry. Each instance presents a finitely generated subgroup as a list of integer matrices and asks for an arithmetic invariant -- index, surjection-at-prime, or membership -- that the construction-time information (N, K) pins down in O(1) closed form, but that the solver, lacking that information, must derive by either Aschbacher-classification analysis or by a membership query in SL(3, Z) of unknown decidability. The benchmark therefore distinguishes models with internalized algebraic priors (Aschbacher classes, McLaughlin's theorem, Property (T), the congruence subgroup property) from models that rely on general-purpose computation. We report empirical results across five representative reasoning traces from two state-of-the-art models. The headline result: on the index variant, one model spent 152 minutes of reasoning, explicitly identified the kernel-side membership question as the bottleneck, attempted constructive verification, and abstained with "DON'T KNOW" rather than commit to its computed cokernel candidate -- demonstrating calibrated meta-cognition on the open-decidability boundary that the benchmark was designed to probe. We argue that the benchmark exposes a four-way classification of model behavior (commit-correct, commit-wrong, abstain-correct, abstain-wrong) that standard answer-key scoring conflates.

</details>


### [186] [Mitigating Label Shift in Tabular In-Context Learning via Test-Time Posterior Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04363)
*Seunghan Lee,Jaehoon Lee,Jun Seo,Sungdong Yoo,Minjae Kim,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,SoonYoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPFN在标签偏移下容易过拟合多数类，为此提出DistPFN——首个面向表格基础模型的测试时后验调整方法，通过重新缩放预测概率来削弱训练先验、增强模型后验贡献，无需修改架构或额外训练；进一步引入DistPFN-T，利用温度缩放自适应控制调整强度。在250+个OpenML数据集上，该方法在标签偏移场景下显著提升TabPFN系列模型的分类性能，同时在无偏移场景下保持强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TabPFN作为表格数据的基础模型，虽然通过上下文学习在合成数据上取得了优异表现，但研究发现其对标签偏移（label shift）鲁棒性差，容易过拟合训练数据集中的多数类，严重限制了其在现实不平衡场景下的应用。

Method: 提出DistPFN，一种测试时后验调整方法：通过重新缩放预测的类概率，降低训练先验（即上下文的类分布）的影响，同时增强模型预测后验的贡献，无需修改模型架构或进行额外训练。进一步提出DistPFN-T，引入温度缩放机制，根据先验与后验之间的差异自适应地控制调整强度。

Result: 在超过250个OpenML数据集上进行了评估，DistPFN和DistPFN-T在标签偏移场景下显著提升了各类TabPFN模型的分类性能，同时在标准设置（无标签偏移）下保持了强劲的性能。

Conclusion: DistPFN是首个面向表格基础模型的测试时后验调整方法，有效解决了TabPFN在标签偏移下的脆弱性问题，且无需架构修改或额外训练，是一种轻量且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: TabPFN has recently gained attention as a foundation model for tabular datasets, achieving strong performance by leveraging in-context learning on synthetic data. However, we find that TabPFN is vulnerable to label shift, often overfitting to the majority class in the training dataset. To address this limitation, we propose DistPFN, the first test-time posterior adjustment method designed for tabular foundation models. DistPFN rescales predicted class probabilities by downweighting the influence of the training prior (i.e., the class distribution of the context) and emphasizing the contribution of the model's predicted posterior, without architectural modification or additional training. We further introduce DistPFN-T, which incorporates temperature scaling to adaptively control the adjustment strength based on the discrepancy between prior and posterior. We evaluate our methods on over 250 OpenML datasets, demonstrating substantial improvements for various TabPFN-based models in classification tasks under label shift, while maintaining strong performance in standard settings without label shift. Code is available at this repository: https://github.com/seunghan96/DistPFN.

</details>


### [187] [Online Nonstochastic Prediction: Logarithmic Regret via Predictive Online Least Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04364)
*Chih-Fan Pai,Yang Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究非随机扰动下边缘稳定部分可观测线性动力系统的在线预测问题，提出一种带有预测提示的无约束在线最小二乘法，在有/无模型知识条件下均能达到对数遗憾界，实现自适应实例最优预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准在线学习方法依赖有界域/梯度，无法处理边缘稳定系统中潜在的无界轨迹增长问题，亟需适用于非随机扰动下边缘稳定系统的在线预测方法。

Method: 提出无约束在线最小二乘法，通过定制预测提示来稳定学习过程；利用任意稳定Luenberger预测器构造提示，使提示残差一致有界；针对无模型情形，为对称系统设计通用提示。

Result: 在有模型知识时，提示残差一致有界，实现对数遗憾；在无模型知识时（对称系统），同样维持对数遗憾界。

Conclusion: 该方法相较于经典固定增益观测器，在非随机扰动下实现了自适应、实例最优的在线预测性能。

Abstract: We study online prediction for marginally stable, partially observed linear dynamical systems under nonstochastic disturbances. Our objective is to minimize the cumulative squared prediction loss and compete with the best-in-hindsight Luenberger predictor. Standard online learning methods typically rely on bounded domains/gradients, and thus their guarantees may fail to deal with potentially unbounded trajectories in marginally stable systems. In this paper, we introduce an unconstrained online least squares method that stabilizes the learning process via tailored predictive hints. With model knowledge, we prove that hints constructed from any stabilizing Luenberger predictor render the hint residuals uniformly bounded, achieving logarithmic regret despite unbounded trajectory growth. We also discuss model-free prediction and introduce a simple universal hint for symmetric systems, under which logarithmic regret is maintained without model knowledge. Our results provide an adaptive, instance-wise optimal online predictor compared to classical fixed-gain observers under nonstochastic disturbances.

</details>


### [188] [Extending Differential Temporal Difference Methods for Episodic Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04368)
*Kris De Asis,Mohamed Elsayed,Jiamin He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种广义的差分时序差分方法，将奖励中心化扩展到情节性问题，证明其保持策略排序不变，并验证了其在多种算法和环境中提升样本效率的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分TD方法原本针对无限时域问题设计，其中的奖励中心化在情节性问题中可能改变最优策略，限制了其应用。本文旨在将奖励中心化安全地扩展到情节性问题。

Method: 提出一种广义的差分TD方法，证明其在终止条件下保持策略排序；与线性TD形式等价从而继承理论保证；将多种流式强化学习算法扩展为其差分版本。

Result: 理论证明该广义方法在情节性问题中保持策略排序；实验表明奖励中心化能在多种基算法和环境中提升样本效率。

Conclusion: 广义差分TD有效将奖励中心化扩展到情节性问题，既保持理论性质又提升了实际性能。

Abstract: Differential temporal difference (TD) methods are value-based reinforcement learning algorithms that have been proposed for infinite-horizon problems. They rely on reward centering, where each reward is centered by the average reward. This keeps the return bounded and removes a value function's state-independent offset. However, reward centering can alter the optimal policy in episodic problems, limiting its applicability. Motivated by recent works that emphasize the role of normalization in streaming deep reinforcement learning, we study reward centering in episodic problems and propose a generalization of differential TD. We prove that this generalization maintains the ordering of policies in the presence of termination, and thus extends differential TD to episodic problems. We show equivalence with a form of linear TD, thereby inheriting theoretical guarantees that have been shown for those algorithms. We then extend several streaming reinforcement learning algorithms to their differential counterparts. Across a range of base algorithms and environments, we empirically validate that reward centering can improve sample efficiency in episodic problems.

</details>


### [189] [$p$-adic Manifold Learning and Benchmark Tasks from Impartial Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04374)
*Tomoki Mihara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了p进流形学习的概念，设计了解该问题的算法，并提出了基于公平游戏的基准测试任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流形学习方法大多基于实数域（p=∞），缺乏对p进数域下数据几何结构的探索，无法有效处理某些具有p进拓扑特性的数据。

Method: 提出p进流形学习的数学框架，设计相应算法，并构建基于公平游戏的基准测试任务来验证方法有效性。

Result: 成功提出了p进流形学习问题及求解算法，并建立了新的基准测试任务。

Conclusion: p进流形学习为处理具有p进拓扑结构的数据提供了新范式，公平游戏基准任务为评估该方法提供了标准化测试平台。

Abstract: We introduce $p$-adic manifold learning, propose an algorithm to solve it, and propose benchmark tasks from impartial games.

</details>


### [190] [Critical Windows of Complexity Control: When Transformers Decide to Reason or Memorize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04396)
*Sarwan Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Transformer在组合泛化任务中，记忆与推理的胜负决定于一狭窄的关键训练窗口。权重衰减在该窗口内施加，其效果等同甚至优于全程施加；窗口位置随初始化尺度系统变化，且小初始化会缩小推理吸引域，挑战了常规认知。但该现象在模算术grokking任务中不出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将复杂度控制视为静态超参数选择，未回答训练过程中何时该控制起决定性作用。本文旨在精确定位Transformer从记忆转向推理的关键时间窗口。

Method: 在受控组合泛化任务（SCAN）上，系统性地在训练不同阶段施加权重衰减，观察其对外分布准确率的影响，并分析初始化尺度、优化步数等变量的作用。

Result: (i)仅在训练25%的窗口施加权重衰减，OOD准确率达0.93，与全程施加的0.91相当；(ii)相同正则化预算放在训练中期，OOD准确率比放在早期高5-9倍；(iii)窗口边界极陡峭，仅偏移100步即可使OOD从0.15跃至0.61；(iv)窗口位置依赖于初始化尺度，小初始化反而缩小推理吸引域。该现象在模算术grokking中不出现。

Conclusion: Transformer的推理能力并非由全程静态超参数决定，而是在训练早期一个狭窄的时间窗口内被关键塑造。这挑战了小初始化普遍更好的认知，且揭示了任务特异性。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that Transformers' compositional generalization is governed by \emph{complexity control}, initialization scale and weight decay, which steers training toward low-complexity reasoning solutions rather than high-complexity memorization. Existing analyses, however, treat complexity control as a single static hyperparameter choice, leaving open \emph{when} during training this control is actually decisive. We show that the memorization-versus-reasoning fate of a Transformer is determined within a sharp, identifiable window of training. On a controlled compositional task we find that (i)~weight decay applied for a single 25\%-of-training window matches full-training weight decay in out-of-distribution (OOD) accuracy ($0.93$ vs $0.91$); (ii)~holding total regularization budget constant, placing it in the middle of training yields $5{-}9\times$ higher OOD accuracy than placing it early; (iii)~the boundary of the critical window is remarkably sharp, window onset shifted by as little as $100$ optimization steps causes mean OOD to jump from chance ($0.15$) to reasoning-regime ($0.61$); (iv)~the window's position depends systematically on initialization scale, but the basin of attraction for reasoning solutions \emph{shrinks} at small initialization, contradicting the prevailing recommendation that smaller initialization is uniformly better. We further show that the critical-window phenomenon is task-specific: it does not appear on grokking with modular arithmetic, where properly tuned constant weight decay matches scheduled weight decay.

</details>


### [191] [Beyond Rigid Geometries: The Spline-Pullback Metric for Universal Diffeomorphic SPD Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04406)
*Tushar Das,Subrata Dutta,Sarmistha Neogy,Koushlendra Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Spline-Pullback Metric（SPM），通过参数化全局微分同胚的B样条实现SPD矩阵黎曼几何的通用逼近，包含Spectral-SPM和Cholesky-SPM两种实例，在3个数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SPD深度学习依赖固定代数黎曼度量，类似手工特征，刚性强、表达力有限；现有参数化方法常违反主矩阵函数公理，导致空间折叠、全局满射性丧失及谱奇异点梯度崩塌。

Method: 通过秩不变、单调约束的B样条参数化全局微分同胚，作为严格递增C¹微分同胚的稠密万能逼近器，理论上统摄现有pullback度量，实现局部非线性谱建模。

Result: 在3个数据集上使用Linear Probes、SPDNets和深度黎曼ResNets均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: SPM从静态度量选择转向通用几何逼近，提供全局双射的pullback几何，避免了秩交换不连续性和梯度不稳定性。

Abstract: The integration of Symmetric Positive Definite (SPD) matrices into deep learning has historically relied on fixed algebraic Riemannian metrics. Analogous to hand-crafted features in classical machine learning, these static formulations impose rigid geometries limiting network expressivity and adaptability. Recent attempts to parameterize these geometries often violate the axioms of primary matrix functions through unconstrained powers or rank-dependent scaling, inviting spatial folding, loss of global surjectivity, and gradient collapse at spectral singularities. In this paper, we introduce the Spline-Pullback Metric (SPM), instantiated as Spectral-SPM and Cholesky-SPM, marking a paradigm shift from static metric selection to universal geometric approximation. By parameterizing the global diffeomorphism via a rank-invariant, monotonically constrained B-spline, SPM acts as a dense universal approximator for strictly increasing $C^1$ diffeomorphisms and theoretically subsumes existing pullback metrics while enabling localized non-linear spectral modelling. Topologically, SPM provides a globally bijective pullback geometry precluding rank-swapping discontinuities and gradient instabilities. Empirically, SPM achieves a state-of-the-art performance across 3 datasets utilizing Linear Probes, SPDNets, and deep Riemannian ResNets.

</details>


### [192] [Counterfactual identifiability beyond global monotonicity: non-monotone triangular structural causal models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04413)
*Pengcheng Tan,Jiang Chen,Dehui Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出非单调三角结构因果模型（NM-TM-SCM），在放弃全局单调性假设下，通过机制可逆性和上下文无关逆传输实现完全反事实可识别性，并开发了CausalInverter模型在非单调机制中取得系统性反事实增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局单调性假设在具身交互中常被违反（相同外生扰动在不同接触情境下引发相反响应），需要探索放弃该假设后仍能保持可识别性的结构条件。

Method: 提出NM-TM-SCM：保留三角递归结构，用机制可逆性和上下文无关逆传输替代全局单调性；证明该条件等价于外生同构并保证完全反事实可识别性；实例化为CausalInverter，包含三角可逆层、方向门控和传输稳定性正则化。

Result: 在合成非单调机制中，随非单调性增强结构偏置带来系统性反事实增益；MuJoCo Door任务实现完美事件级反事实恢复，连续角度误差低于Transformer基线，恢复稳定性显著优于Transformer和条件流预测器；MuJoCo Push任务（非单调性较弱）中低数据预测器保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 识别出全局单调三角模型与无约束黑箱世界模型之间的更广泛可识别性边界，为具身交互中的反事实推理提供了理论基础和实用方法。

Abstract: Structural causal models provide a unified semantics for interventions and counterfactuals, but most identifiability results rely on restrictive assumptions like global monotonicity, which are often violated in embodied interaction, where the same exogenous perturbation can induce opposite responses under different contact contexts. We ask what structure still suffices once global monotonicity is dropped. We introduce non-monotone triangular structural causal models (NM-TM-SCM), which retain triangular recursion but replace global monotonicity with mechanism-wise invertibility and context-independent inverse transport. We prove that these conditions are equivalent to exogenous isomorphism and imply complete counterfactual identifiability, and we give a counterexample showing that local invertibility alone is insufficient. We instantiate the theory in CausalInverter, with triangular invertible layers, orientation gates, and transport-stability regularization. On synthetic non-monotonic mechanisms, the structural bias yields systematic counterfactual gains as non-monotonicity increases. On MuJoCo Door, our model achieves perfect event-level counterfactual recovery, lowers continuous angle error relative to a Transformer baseline, and delivers substantially more stable recovery than Transformer and conditional-flow predictors. On MuJoCo Push, where non-monotonicity is weaker, the same low-data predictors remain competitive or better, consistent with a bias-variance boundary. These results identify a broader identifiable regime between globally monotone triangular models and unconstrained black-box world models.

</details>


### [193] [FAAST: Forward-Only Associative Learning via Closed-Form Fast Weights for Test-Time Supervised Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04651)
*Guangsheng Bao,Hongbo Zhang,Han Cui,Yanbin Zhao,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FAAST，一种前向传播的联想适配方法，通过单遍分析将标注样本编译成快速权重，无需反向传播或记忆/上下文依赖，在分类和语言建模基准上，适配时间减少90%以上，内存节省高达95%，性能与反向传播适配相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练模型适配面临两难：反向传播训练成本高，而基于记忆或上下文学习推理开销大。需要一种更高效的适配方法。

Method: FAAST（Forward-only Associative Adaptation）是一种纯前向的联想适配方法，通过单遍分析将标注示例解析编译为快速权重，消除记忆或上下文依赖，实现恒定时间推理，并将任务适配与预训练表示解耦。

Result: 在图像分类和语言建模基准上，FAAST在性能上匹配或超越基于反向传播的适配方法，适配时间减少90%以上；与基于记忆/上下文的适配方法相比，内存使用最多降低95%。

Conclusion: FAAST是一种高效、可扩展的监督任务适配方案，特别适用于资源受限的模型场景。

Abstract: Adapting pretrained models typically involves a trade-off between the high training costs of backpropagation and the heavy inference overhead of memory-based or in-context learning. We propose FAAST, a forward-only associative adaptation method that analytically compiles labeled examples into fast weights in a single pass. By eliminating memory or context dependence, FAAST achieves constant-time inference and decouples task adaptation from pretrained representation. Across image classification and language modeling benchmarks, FAAST matches or exceeds backprop-based adaptation while reducing adaptation time by over 90\% and is competitive to memory/context-based adaptation while saving memory usage by up to 95\%. These results demonstrate FAAST as a highly efficient, scalable solution for supervised task adaptation, particularly for resource-constrained models. We release the code and models at https://github.com/baoguangsheng/faast.

</details>


### [194] [Demystifying Manifold Constraints in LLM Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04418)
*Kang An,Jiaxiang Li,Donald Goldfarb,Shiqian Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MACRO优化框架，证明流形约束在大语言模型预训练中可替代RMS归一化和解耦权重衰减等启发式技术，通过约束前向激活尺度和维持旋转平衡实现稳定训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM预训练依赖大量启发式稳定技术（如归一化层、权重衰减），但这些显式约束的作用机制和动机尚不明确。本文旨在系统解构流形约束在LLM预训练中的角色，澄清其与启发式技术的相互作用关系。

Method: 提出MACRO（Msign-Aligned Constrained Riemannian Optimizer），一个可证明收敛的单循环优化框架。通过理论分析和实验评估，将流形约束与RMS归一化、解耦权重衰减等机制分离，独立验证其作用。

Result: 流形约束能独立约束前向激活尺度并维持稳定的旋转平衡，从而取代RMS归一化和解耦权重衰减的功能。在大规模LLM架构上，MACRO在保持严格黎曼优化理论保证的同时取得了极具竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 显式流形约束可替代传统LLM预训练中的启发式稳定技术，为设计更简洁、理论上更严格的优化方法提供了新方向。

Abstract: The empirical success of large language model (LLM) pre-training relies heavily on heuristic stabilization techniques, such as explicit normalization layers and weight decay. While recent constrained optimization approaches that explicitly restrict weights may improve numerical stability and performance, the mechanism and motivation for adding constraints still remain elusive. This paper systematically demystifies the role of explicit manifold constraints in LLM pre-training. By introducing the Msign-Aligned Constrained Riemannian Optimizer (MACRO)-a provably convergent, single-loop optimization framework-our study disentangles weight regularization heuristics from interacting mechanisms like RMS normalization and decoupled weight decay. Theoretical analyses and comprehensive empirical evaluations reveal that manifold constraints independently bound forward activation scales and enforce stable rotational equilibrium, thereby subsuming the roles of these heuristic mechanisms. Evaluations on large-scale LLM architectures demonstrate that MACRO achieves highly competitive performance while rigorously preserving the theoretical guarantees of exact Riemannian optimization.

</details>


### [195] [Uncertainty-Aware Exploratory Direct Preference Optimization for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04874)
*Huatian Zhang,Zhendong Mao,Lei Zhang,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出不确定性感知的探索性直接偏好优化方法（UE-DPO），通过token级认知不确定性来识别并修正多模态大模型在视觉感知上的认知缺陷，缓解幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DPO方法依赖模型自评估的视觉敏感度信号来分配训练权重，但该敏感度由正在训练中的模型估计，存在自我参照偏差：强化已学好的视觉线索而忽略难以感知的关键细节，限制了深度对齐。

Method: 1）量化模型无法将token预测锚定到给定图像的认知不确定性；2）基于不确定性感知的探索强度，对偏好样本中视觉缺陷token施加更多学习压力，同时减轻对非偏好样本中有益知识的过度惩罚；3）提供理论证明。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法在缓解多模态大模型幻觉方面具有有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: UE-DPO通过不确定性引导的探索机制，使模型能够主动发现并修正自身认知缺陷，实现更细粒度的视觉保真度对齐。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) has proven to be an effective solution for mitigating hallucination in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) by learning from preference pairs. One of its key challenges lies in how to transfer the sequence-level preference into fine-grained supervision on visual fidelity. To safeguard vision-related tokens that are prone to hallucination, existing methods typically allocate training emphasis according to the model's self-assessed visual sensitivity signals. However, such sensitivity, estimated by a model still under training, introduces self-referential bias: reinforcing already well-learned visual cues while neglecting hard-to-perceive but critical details, thereby limiting deeper alignment. In this work, we propose an Uncertainty-aware Exploratory Direct Preference Optimization (UE-DPO) method for MLLMs, which enables the model to uncover its cognitive deficiencies and actively explore for self-correction, guided by token-level epistemic uncertainty. Specifically, we first quantify the uncertainty from the model's failure to ground token predictions in the given image. Then, based on an uncertainty-aware exploration intensity, we encourage more learning pressure on visually deficient tokens in preferred samples, and alleviate the over-penalization of beneficial knowledge in dispreferred samples. Further, we provide a theoretical justification for our method, and extensive experiments demonstrate its effectiveness and robustness.

</details>


### [196] [Self-Attention as Transport: Limits of Symmetric Spectral Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04893)
*Dominik Dahlem,Diego Maniloff,Mac Misiura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，大语言模型中的注意力路由失效有两种模式（过度集中或过度分散），且所有基于谱的方法对注意力方向是结构性的盲区（无法区分操作符与其转置），唯有不对称系数G能作为方向控制参数。结合二分Cheeger界，提出双轴诊断框架（φ容量+G方向），并在多模型上验证了极性反转预测（LC-AUROC 0.62–0.84）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有谱方法仅分析对称化的注意力算子，忽略了注意力的方向性信息；需要一种能同时捕捉注意力容量和方向特征的诊断框架，以检测和区分大模型的幻觉模式。

Method: (1) 证明所有转置不变谱诊断对注意力方向结构性盲区，量化推出不对称系数G是唯一方向控制参数；(2) 推导因果架构的封闭形式二分Cheeger景观，得到均匀因果注意力的n无关下界φ≥1/5及窗口注意力的O(w/n)下界；(3) 构建双轴诊断（φ容量+G方向），提出极性反转可验证预测。

Result: 长度控制评估下，LC-AUROC在0.62–0.84区间；在HaluEval和MedHallu数据集上极性反转与预测一致，验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 注意力失效的模式不仅是数值差异，更是形状差异；双轴诊断框架能有效区分瓶颈型和扩散型幻觉，极性反转是可验证的预测性指标。

Abstract: Large language models hallucinate in predictable ways: attention routing fails by over-concentrating on a narrow set of positions, or by spreading so diffusely that relevance is diluted, and the shape of the failure carries diagnostic signal. A widely used family of spectral methods analyzes the symmetric component of the degree-normalized attention operator, which governs transport capacity; we prove that every transpose-invariant spectral diagnostic of this operator is structurally orientation-blind (it cannot distinguish an operator from its transpose, and therefore cannot detect information-flow direction), with a quantitative converse establishing the asymmetry coefficient $G$ as the unique control parameter for direction.
  Pairing this with a closed-form bipartite-Cheeger landscape for canonical causal architectures, we show that uniform causal attention satisfies an $n$-independent floor $φ\ge 1/5$ with worst cut at $t^\ast/n \approx 0.32$, while window attention pierces the floor as $O(w/n)$; failure modes are shape-different, not just value-different. The resulting two-axis diagnostic ($φ$ for capacity, $G$ for direction) yields a falsifiable polarity prediction: bottleneck- and diffuse-dominated benchmarks should exhibit opposite polarity. Under length-controlled evaluation, transport features retain interpretable signal (LC-AUROC from 0.62 to 0.84) on tested models up to 8B parameters, with polarity reversing as predicted between HaluEval and MedHallu.

</details>


### [197] [Discovering Sparse Counterfactual Factors via Latent Adjustment for Survey-based Community Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04460)
*Fatima Ashraf,Muhammad Ayub Sabir,Junbiao Pang,Yufang Zhou,Yan Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于潜在表征的稀疏反事实群体干预框架，通过Shapley归因识别关键变量，结合熵正则化最优传输和稀疏惩罚，从交通调查数据中生成政策可行的干预策略，实现目标群体向参考群体的分布对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通调查分析多为描述性或预测性，缺乏能生成稀疏、政策可行的干预策略的方法。需要一种能从调查数据中直接推导出可操作干预方案的技术。

Method: 1) 固定基非负潜在表征确保前后可比性与可映射性；2) Shapley归因识别目标相关潜在因子；3) 熵正则化最优传输最小化干预后目标分布与参考分布的差异；4) 加权ℓ₂,₁惩罚促进共享政策杠杆的稀疏性。

Result: 在真实交通调查数据集上，该方法能生成紧凑、可解释的政策可行干预方案，具有明确的调整幅度，提高了群体层面转化率，并保持了干预稀疏性。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效填补了从调查数据到政策干预的鸿沟，实现了可解释、稀疏且可行的群体干预策略生成。

Abstract: Transportation surveys are widely used to understand travel preferences and adoption barriers, yet most survey-based analyses remain descriptive or predictive and rarely provide sparse, policy-feasible intervention strategies. We study sparse counterfactual community intervention from survey responses, where the goal is to shift a target respondent group toward a desired reference group through controllable survey-variable adjustments. We formulate this task as a policy-feasible distributional alignment problem using a fixed-basis nonnegative latent representation that preserves pre/post comparability and provides a stable map from latent factors to original variables. To make latent movement actionable, target-relevant latent factors are identified through Shapley-guided attribution and transferred to controllable variables as intervention priorities. Feasible group-level adjustments are then learned by minimizing an entropy-regularized optimal-transport discrepancy between the post-intervention target distribution and the reference distribution, together with a weighted $\ell_{2,1}$ penalty that promotes shared policy-lever sparsity. Experiments on real-world transportation survey datasets show that the proposed framework produces compact and interpretable policy-feasible interventions with explicit adjustment magnitudes, improves population-level conversion, and preserves intervention sparsity. Code and datasets are publicly available at: https://github.com/pangjunbiao/latent-group-alignment.git

</details>


### [198] [Reinforcement Learning for Compositional Generalization with Outcome-Level Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04920)
*Xiyan Fu,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用结果级别的强化学习（特别是GRPO算法）来提升模型的组合泛化能力，实验表明相比监督微调，强化学习通过重塑输出分布显著改善了模型对未见组合的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督微调方法在token级别训练模型模仿目标输出，无法捕捉全局组合结构，导致组合泛化能力不足。本文探索能否通过结果级别的强化学习来改善这一问题。

Method: 采用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) 算法，基于最终输出的反馈（包括简单的二值结果奖励和提供额外组合反馈的复合奖励）来优化模型。

Result: 在多个组合泛化基准上，强化学习相比监督微调显著提升了组合泛化能力。监督模型倾向于过拟合高频训练组合，而强化学习通过重塑输出分布来改善泛化，尤其是在复杂组合类型上效果更明显。

Conclusion: 结果级别的强化学习能够有效提升组合泛化能力，其核心机制在于重塑输出分布并减少对高频组合的过拟合。

Abstract: Compositional generalization refers to correctly interpret novel combinations of known primitives, which remains a major challenge. Existing approaches often rely on supervised fine-tuning, which encourages models to imitate target outputs. This token-level training paradigm fails to capture the global compositional structure required for generalizing to unseen combinations. In this work, we investigate whether compositional generalization can instead be improved through outcome-level reinforcement learning. We adopt Group Relative Policy Optimization to optimize models based on feedback on their final outputs. Within this framework, we explore both a simple binary outcome reward and a composite reward that provides additional composition feedback. Experiments on multiple compositional benchmarks show that reinforcement learning improves compositional generalization compared to supervised fine-tuning. Further analysis reveals that supervised models tend to overfit frequent training compositions, whereas reinforcement learning improves compositional generalization by reshaping the output distribution, particularly for more complex composition types.

</details>


### [199] [Conceptors for Semantic Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04980)
*Ilias Triantafyllopoulos,Young-Min Cho,Ren Tao,Miranda Muqing Miao,Sunny Rai,Lyle Ungar,Sharath Chandra Guntuku,Neville Ryant,João Sedoc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用Conceptor（软投影矩阵）替代单方向向量进行激活导向的LLM行为控制，通过保留概念的多维子空间，实现更好的概念分离性、布尔代数组合运算和更少的退化输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的激活导向方法将每个概念简化为单一方向向量，忽略了其几何结构的丰富性，限制了表达能力和可控性。

Method: 使用Conceptor（从双极概念激活池中估计的软投影矩阵）保留完整的多维子空间；通过Conceptor quota提供无参数层选择诊断；利用闭合布尔代数（AND、OR、NOT）进行概念组合。

Result: Conceptor在概念可分离性预测上达到r=0.96的Pearson相关性；在五轴设计空间评估中，在概念子空间多维的层上匹配或优于加性基线，且显著减少退化输出。

Conclusion: Conceptor导向是一种几何上有原则、可组合且更安全的替代方案，优于基于有限对比对的单方向导向方法。

Abstract: Activation-based steering provides control of LLM behavior at inference time, but the dominant paradigm reduces each concept to a single direction whose geometry is left largely unexamined. Rather than selecting a single steering direction, we use conceptors: soft projection matrices estimated from activations pooled across both poles of a bipolar concept, which preserve the concept's full multidimensional subspace. A geometric analysis shows the bipolar subspace strictly subsumes the single-vector baseline. We further show that the conceptor quota provides a parameter-free layer-selection diagnostic, predicting concept separability with Pearson correlations up to r=0.96 across three instruction-tuned models and three semantic dimensions. Beyond selection, conceptors admit a closed-form Boolean algebra (AND, OR, NOT): we evaluate conceptor compositionality on thematically related sub-concepts. Across a systematic five-axis design-space evaluation, conceptors match or outperform additive baselines at layers where concept subspaces are multi-dimensional while producing substantially fewer degenerate outputs. Conceptor steering is a geometrically principled, compositional, and practically safer alternative to single-direction steering from a limited number of contrastive pairs.

</details>


### [200] [Automated Formal Proofs of Combinatorial Identities via Wilf-Zeilberger Guidance and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04472)
*Beibei Xiong,Hangyu Lv,Junqi Liu,Yisen Wang,Shaoshi Chen,Jianlin Wang,Zhengfeng Yang,Lihong Zhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WZ-LLM是一个神经符号框架，结合Wilf-Zeilberger方法与LLM证明器，在Lean 4中自动化组合恒等式的形式化证明，在LCI-Test上达到34%的证明成功率，超越多个强基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化组合恒等式的形式化证明对LLM证明器极具挑战，因为长程证明规划困难且无约束搜索易爆炸。符号方法（如WZ方法）可通过构造辅助函数和递推关系实现机械化证明，但需要与LLM能力相结合以提升自动化程度。

Method: 提出WZ-LLM框架：(1) 将WZ证明方案转化为Lean 4中可执行的证明草图；(2) 使用LLM证明器处理可机器校验的子目标；(3) 通过Lean内核验证的自举循环训练专用WZ-Prover，结合专家验证迭代和DAPO强化学习微调。

Result: 在LCI-Test（100个经典组合恒等式）上达到34%的证明成功率，优于DeepSeek-V3和Goedel-Prover-V2等强基线；在CombiBench和PutnamBench-Comb上也取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 该框架提供两种互补优势：对WZ方法无法处理的恒等式具有更好的直接证明能力；当WZ草图引导专用证明器时，端到端成功率显著提高。

Abstract: Automating formal proofs of combinatorial identities is challenging for LLM-based provers, as long-horizon proof planning is required and unconstrained search quickly explodes. Symbolic methods such as the Wilf-Zeilberger (WZ) method can achieve a mechanized proof of combinatorial identities by constructing special auxiliary functions and demonstrating that they satisfy specific recurrence relations. We propose WZ-LLM, a neuro-symbolic framework that turns WZ proof plans into executable proof sketches in Lean 4 and uses an LLM-based prover to discharge the resulting machine-checkable subgoals. We also train a dedicated WZ-Prover via a Lean-kernel-verified bootstrapping loop with expert-verified iteration, followed by DAPO-based refinement. Experiments show that WZ-LLM achieves a 34% proof success rate on LCI-Test (100 classic combinatorial identities), outperforming strong baselines such as DeepSeek-V3 and Goedel-Prover-V2, and delivering consistent gains on CombiBench and PutnamBench-Comb. These results indicate that our framework provides two complementary strengths: improved direct proving for identities beyond the scope of WZ, and substantially higher end-to-end success when WZ sketches guide a specialized prover.

</details>


### [201] [Self-Induced Outcome Potential: Turn-Level Credit Assignment for Agents without Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04984)
*Senkang Hu,Yong Dai,Xudong Han,Zhengru Fang,Yuzhi Zhao,Sam Tak Wu Kwong,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SIOP方法，通过对最终答案进行语义聚类作为潜在未来状态，实现无验证器的回合级信用分配，在7个搜索增强智能体推理基准上提升了平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程LLM智能体依赖中间信息收集回合，但训练反馈通常仅在最终答案处获得，因为过程级奖励需要高质量的人工标注。现有回合级塑形方法需要答案监督或稳定的任务特定验证器，而无标签强化学习方法主要在答案或轨迹层面提取信号，无法将信用分配给中间回合。

Method: SIOP将最终答案的语义聚类视为潜在未来结果状态，用于基于势能的回合级信用分配。对每个查询采样多个轨迹，将最终答案聚类为语义结果模式，构建可靠性感知的目标分布，然后通过可计算的聚类级近似奖励那些增加可靠未来状态后验支持的回合。

Result: 在7个搜索增强的智能体推理基准上，SIOP在平均性能上优于无验证器的结果级基线，并接近有金答案监督的结果基线。

Conclusion: SIOP将信息势能塑形从金答案监督泛化到无需任务特定金验证器的场景，同时避免了标准GRPO使用的广播式轨迹级优势。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents depend on intermediate information-gathering turns, yet training feedback is usually observed only at the final answer, because process-level rewards require high-quality human annotation. Existing turn-level shaping methods reward turns that increase the likelihood of a gold answer, but they require answer supervision or stable task-specific verifiers. Conversely, label-free RL methods extract self-signals from output distributions, but mainly at the answer or trajectory level and therefore cannot assign credit to intermediate turns. We propose Self-Induced Outcome Potential (SIOP), which treats semantic clusters of final answers as latent future outcome states for potential-based turn-level credit assignment. For each query, SIOP samples multiple rollouts, clusters final answers into semantic outcome modes, and builds a reliability-aware target distribution over these states. It then rewards turns for increasing posterior support for reliable future states using a tractable cluster-level approximation. The objective generalizes information-potential shaping from gold-answer supervision to settings without task-specific gold verifiers while avoiding the broadcasted rollout-level advantages used by standard GRPO. We formalize the framework, characterize its supervised gold-answer limit, and show that SIOP improves average performance over verifier-free outcome-level baselines on seven search-augmented agentic reasoning benchmarks while approaching a gold-supervised outcome baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/dl-m9/SIOP.git.

</details>


### [202] [Data-dependent Exploration for Online Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04477)
*Zhen-Yu Zhang,Yuting Tang,Jiandong Zhang,Lanjihong Ma,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DEPO方法，通过历史数据构建不确定性奖励来鼓励在线RLHF中的探索，在理论上提供数据相关的遗憾界，实证上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线RLHF中，现有探索策略通常依赖on-policy期望来推导奖励，但有限的历史偏好数据难以可靠估计，导致策略过早放弃包含高价值行为的未探索区域。

Method: 提出DEPO（Data-dependent Exploration for Preference Optimization），利用历史数据为高不确定性区域构建额外的不确定性奖励，鼓励向潜在高价值区域探索。

Result: 理论上给出了数据相关的遗憾界，比最坏情况下的界更紧；实证上在多个基准测试中一致优于强基线方法，展现出更高的样本效率。

Conclusion: DEPO是一种简单、可扩展的在线RLHF探索方法，通过数据驱动的不确定性奖励有效提升探索效率，理论分析与实验验证均证明了其有效性。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has emerged as a promising paradigm for aligning large language models (LLMs) by continuously collecting new preference feedback during training. A foundational challenge in this setting is exploration, which requires algorithms that enable the LLMs to generate informative comparisons that improve sample-efficiency in online RLHF. Existing exploration strategies often derive bonuses via on-policy expectations, which are difficult to estimate reliably from the limited historical preference data available during training; as a result, the policy can prematurely down-weight under-explored regions that may contain high-value behaviors. In this paper, we propose data-dependent exploration for preference optimization (DEPO), a simple and scalable method that leverages historical data to construct an extra uncertainty bonus for high-uncertainty regions, encouraging exploration toward potentially high-value data. Theoretically, we provide a data-dependent regret bound for the proposed algorithm, showing that it adapts to the hardness of the learning task itself and can be tighter than worst-case bounds in practice. Empirically, the proposed method consistently outperforms strong baselines across benchmarks, demonstrating improved sample efficiency.

</details>


### [203] [Continual Knowledge Updating in LLM Systems: Learning Through Multi-Timescale Memory Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05097)
*Andreas Pattichis,Constantine Dovrolis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Memini提出了一种受生物记忆启发的关联记忆系统，通过Benna-Fusi模型的快慢耦合动力学，在有向图中实现知识的自组织、巩固和选择性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM部署后无法适应持续变化的世界，而外部记忆系统通常需要显式管理而非自适应。生物记忆通过多时间尺度耦合动力学实现高效学习，受此启发，作者希望让外部记忆具备类似的自组织能力。

Method: 构建有向图结构的关联记忆，每条边携带两个耦合的内部变量（快变量和慢变量），遵循Benna-Fusi突触巩固模型，通过快慢动力学的耦合实现记忆的自适应演化。

Result: 从单一的动力学机制中涌现出三种能力：对片段的敏感性（episodic sensitivity）、渐进式巩固（gradual consolidation）、选择性遗忘（selective forgetting）。

Conclusion: 外部记忆应被重新构想为一个学习基质，通过自身动力学实现知识的自组织，而非被显式管理。

Abstract: LLMs are trained once, then deployed into a world that never stops changing. External memory compensates for this, but most systems manage it explicitly rather than letting it adapt on its own. Biological memory works differently: coupled multi-timescale dynamics make new associations immediately usable, strengthen what repetition confirms, and let the rest fade. We argue that external memory should follow a similar principle. In Memini, this view takes the form of an associative memory that organizes knowledge as a directed graph. Each edge carries two coupled internal variables, one fast and one slow, following the Benna-Fusi model of synaptic consolidation. From this coupling, episodic sensitivity, gradual consolidation, and selective forgetting emerge as facets of a single mechanism, reframing external memory as a learning substrate that reorganizes through its own dynamics.

</details>


### [204] [Quadrature-TreeSHAP: Depth-Independent TreeSHAP and Shapley Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04497)
*Ron Wettenstein,Rory Mitchell,Peng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Quadrature-TreeSHAP，一种基于高斯-勒让德求积的Path-Dependent TreeSHAP重表述，仅需8个固定求积点即可达到机器精度，实现数值稳定、深度无关且支持任意阶Shapley交互值，在CPU和GPU上均取得显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有树集成模型解释方法（如Path-Dependent TreeSHAP）在运行时间对树深度敏感、数值稳定性不足、以及难以支持高阶交互值计算之间存在权衡，亟需一种同时解决上述问题的方法。

Method: 将Shapley值和任意阶交互值表示为加权Banzhaf交互多项式在特征参与概率p上的积分（0到1），利用Gauss-Legendre求积公式近似该积分，仅需8个固定求积点即可达到机器精度；该固定点形式消除了内层计算对树深度的依赖，支持高效SIMD执行。

Result: 在12个XGBoost基准上，Quadrature-TreeSHAP在CPU上比TreeSHAP快1.06x-10.59x，在GPU上比GPUTreeSHAP快1.84x-6.95x；Shapley成对交互在CPU上快3.80x-58.11x，高阶交互相比TreeSHAP-IQ加速高达1200x。

Conclusion: Quadrature-TreeSHAP在数值稳定性、计算效率和对高阶交互的支持上全面优于现有方法，且已集成到XGBoost中，可作为树集成模型解释的通用替代方案。

Abstract: Shapley values are a standard tool for explaining predictions of tree ensembles, with Path-Dependent SHAP being the most widely used variant. Despite substantial progress, existing methods still exhibit trade-offs between depth-dependent runtime, numerical stability, and support for higher-order interactions. To address these challenges, we introduce Quadrature-TreeSHAP, a quadrature-based reformulation of Path-Dependent TreeSHAP that is numerically stable, naturally extends to any-order Shapley interaction values and is practically insensitive to tree depth. Our implementation supports both CPU and GPU and is integrated into XGBoost.
  Our method is based on a weighted-Banzhaf interaction polynomial, which expresses Banzhaf interaction values as expectations under a feature participation probability $p$. Shapley values and any-order interaction values are then recovered by integrating these polynomials over $p$ from 0 to 1. We evaluate these integrals using Gauss-Legendre quadrature, and show that, in practice, only 8 fixed quadrature points are sufficient to reach machine precision. In fact, Quadrature-TreeSHAP with 8 fixed points achieves greater numerical stability than TreeSHAP. This fixed-point formulation removes depth dependence from the inner computation and enables efficient SIMD execution.
  We confirm these advantages empirically. On 12 XGBoost benchmarks, Quadrature-TreeSHAP computes Shapley values 1.06x-10.59x faster than TreeSHAP on CPU and 1.84x-6.95x faster than GPUTreeSHAP on GPU. Shapley pairwise interactions are 3.80x-58.11x faster on CPU, with higher-order interactions achieving speedups of up to 1200x compared to TreeSHAP-IQ.

</details>


### [205] [FL-Sailer: Efficient and Privacy-Preserving Federated Learning for Scalable Single-Cell Epigenetic Data Analysis via Adaptive Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04519)
*Guangyi Zhang,Yi Dai,Yiyun He,Junhao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FL-Sailer，首个专为scATAC-seq数据设计的联邦学习框架，通过自适应杠杆分数采样（降维80%）和不变VAE架构（互信息最小化解耦生物信号与技术混杂）解决超高维、极端稀疏和跨机构异质性三大挑战，在合成和真实数据集上性能超越集中式方法，并提供了收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: scATAC-seq可实现高分辨率染色质可及性图谱绘制，但隐私法规和数据规模限制阻碍了多机构数据共享；联邦学习虽提供隐私保护方案，却面临scATAC-seq数据超高维度、极端稀疏性和严重跨机构异质性的根本性挑战。

Method: FL-Sailer包含两大核心创新：(i) 自适应杠杆分数采样，选择生物学可解释的特征并将维度降低80%；(ii) 不变VAE架构，通过互信息最小化将生物信号与技术混杂因素解耦。同时提供了收敛性理论保证。

Result: 在合成和真实表观基因组数据集上的大量实验表明，FL-Sailer不仅实现了此前不可行的多机构协作，还通过将自适应采样作为隐式正则化器抑制技术噪声，从而超越了集中式方法。

Conclusion: 当联邦学习针对领域特定挑战进行定制时，可以成为协作表观基因组研究的优越范式。

Abstract: Single-cell ATAC-seq (scATAC-seq) enables high-resolution mapping of chromatin accessibility, yet privacy regulations and data size constraints hinder multi-institutional sharing. Federated learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving alternative, but faces three fundamental barriers in scATAC-seq analysis: ultra-high dimensionality, extreme sparsity, and severe cross-institutional heterogeneity. We propose FL-Sailer, the first FL framework designed for scATAC-seq data. FL-Sailer integrates two key innovations: (i) adaptive leverage score sampling, which selects biologically interpretable features while reducing dimensionality by 80%, and (ii) an invariant VAE architecture, which disentangles biological signals from technical confounders via mutual information minimization. We provide a convergence guarantee, showing that FL-Sailer converges to an approximate solution of the original high-dimensional problem with bounded error. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real epigenomic datasets demonstrate that FL-Sailer not only enables previously infeasible multi-institutional collaborations but also surpasses centralized methods by leveraging adaptive sampling as an implicit regularizer to suppress technical noise. Our work establishes that federated learning, when tailored to domain-specific challenges, can become a superior paradigm for collaborative epigenomic research.

</details>


### [206] [Power Distribution Bridges Sampling, Self-Reward RL, and Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04542)
*Akiyoshi Tomihari,Issei Sato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文揭示了幂分布（power distribution）在采样、自奖励KL正则化强化学习（RL）和自蒸馏之间的桥梁作用，证明了幂分布是序列级对数概率作为奖励时KL正则化RL的闭式最优解，并由此提出幂自蒸馏方法，在推理任务上以更低推理成本匹配或超越幂采样的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究对RL是否真正驱动LLM强推理能力提出质疑，同时蒸馏和推理时采样（包括幂采样）被证明能有效提升LLM性能，但RL、蒸馏和采样之间的关系尚不明确。本文旨在理清三者之间的内在联系。

Method: 1) 从采样角度证明：廉价局部近似无法在没有后缀信息的条件下复现序列级幂分布；2) 从RL角度证明：幂分布是以模型序列级对数概率为奖励时KL正则化RL的闭式最优解；3) 基于此识别提出幂自蒸馏（power self-distillation），即通过教师样本上的监督训练将幂采样的成本摊销化。

Result: 在推理任务上的实验表明：幂采样能提升自奖励，真实奖励的提升取决于其与自奖励的对齐程度，幂自蒸馏能以更低的推理成本匹配或超越幂采样的性能。

Conclusion: 幂分布桥接了采样、自奖励KL正则化RL和自蒸馏三个范式，幂自蒸馏可实现自奖励锐化，其下游真实奖励的改进由真实奖励与自奖励在幂分布下的协方差决定。

Abstract: Recent analyses question whether reinforcement learning (RL) is responsible for strong reasoning in large language models (LLMs). At the same time, distillation and inference-time sampling, including power sampling, have emerged as effective ways to improve LLM performance. However, the relationship among RL, distillation, and sampling remains unclear. In this study, we focus on the power distribution, the target distribution of power sampling, and show that the power distribution bridges sampling, self-reward KL-regularized RL, and self-distillation. From the sampling perspective, we show that inexpensive local approximations cannot reproduce sequence-level power without information about possible suffixes. From the RL perspective, the power distribution is the closed-form optimizer of KL-regularized RL when the model's sequence-level log-probabilities are used as the reward. This identification leads to power self-distillation, an offline distillation surrogate that shares the same target distribution and amortizes the cost of power sampling into supervised training on teacher samples. We further show that power self-distillation can achieve self-reward sharpening, while improvement in a downstream true reward is governed by the covariance between true reward and self-reward under the power distribution. Experiments on reasoning tasks support our analysis: power sampling raises self-reward, true-reward gains depend on alignment with self-reward, and power self-distillation can match or exceed the performance of power sampling at much lower inference cost.

</details>


### [207] [Event-Based Early Warning of Vineyard Disease Risk from Environmental Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04548)
*Ivica Dimitrovski,Ivan Kitanovski,Danco Davcev,Slobodan Kalajdziski,Kosta Mitreski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于事件的葡萄园病害风险早期预警方法，将传统的日级别分类任务重新定义为预测未来3-7天内疾病风险期的转变，通过定义最小无病间隔来减少标签碎片化，并在多年代气象数据上评估了XGBoost、LSTM和TCN等模型，结果表明该事件式公式支持实用的短期预警，不同模型在事件召回率、提前时间和误报之间表现出不同的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将病害预测公式化为日常存在分类，容易偏向持续性预测，且对可操作的短期预警支持有限。

Method: 将任务重新定义为预测未来3-7天窗口内标注的病害风险期的转变；通过定义最小无病间隔来减少二元标签中短暂中断导致的碎片化；构建包含湿度动态、降雨累积、温度变异性和季节性循环时间编码的输入表示；评估XGBoost、LSTM和TCN等代表性方法。

Result: 事件式公式支持实用的短期预警；不同模型在事件召回率、提前时间和误报行为之间表现出不同的权衡。

Conclusion: 强调了问题公式化在环境时间序列学习中的重要性，并展示了事件式预测对葡萄园病害预警系统的价值。

Abstract: Accurate early warning of vineyard disease risk from environmental observations is essential for timely intervention and more sustainable crop protection. However, many existing studies formulate disease prediction as daily presence classification, which can favor persistence-driven predictions and provide only limited support for actionable short-horizon warning. In this paper, we present an event-based approach for early warning of vineyard disease risk from environmental time series and evaluate it through a vineyard case study. Rather than predicting daily disease status, the task is reformulated to predict transitions into annotated disease-risk periods within a future window of 3-7 days. To reduce fragmentation caused by short interruptions in the binary labels, new events are defined only after a minimum disease-free gap. This formulation encourages models to capture environmental precursors associated with upcoming risk periods instead of merely reproducing temporal persistence. Using multi-year agro-meteorological data, we construct input representations that capture humidity dynamics, rainfall accumulation, temperature variability, and seasonal structure through cyclic temporal encoding. We evaluate representative methods from classical machine learning and deep learning, including XGBoost, Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks, and Temporal Convolutional Networks (TCNs), using both standard classification metrics and an event-oriented early warning protocol. The results show that the event-based formulation supports practical short-horizon warning, while the compared models exhibit distinct trade-offs between event recall, lead time, and false-alert behavior. Overall, the study underscores the importance of problem formulation in environmental time-series learning and demonstrates the value of event-based prediction for vineyard disease warning systems.

</details>


### [208] [Dream-MPC: Gradient-Based Model Predictive Control with Latent Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04568)
*Jonathan Spieler,Sven Behnke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Dream-MPC提出了一种结合策略先验与梯度上升优化的模型预测控制方法，通过不确定性正则化和优化迭代的时间摊销，在24个连续控制任务上显著提升策略性能，超越了无梯度MPC和现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的混合MPC方法依赖无梯度优化，在高维控制任务中计算成本高；而梯度优化方法在经验上表现较差。作者旨在结合两者优势，提出一种高效的基于梯度的MPC方法。

Method: 从策略 rollout 生成少量候选轨迹，利用学习的世界模型对每条轨迹执行梯度上升优化，引入不确定性正则化，并通过复用先前优化动作实现优化迭代的时间摊销。

Result: 在24个连续控制任务上，Dream-MPC显著提升了底层策略的性能，优于无梯度MPC和当前最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 梯度优化的MPC方法通过合理的设计（不确定性正则化、时间摊销）可以有效克服以往梯度方法的劣势，在计算效率和性能上取得更好平衡。

Abstract: State-of-the-art model-based Reinforcement Learning (RL) approaches either use gradient-free, population-based methods for planning, learned policy networks, or a combination of policy networks and planning. Hybrid approaches that combine Model Predictive Control (MPC) with a learned model and a policy prior to leverage the advantages of both paradigms have shown promising results. However, these approaches typically rely on gradient-free optimization methods, which can be computationally expensive for high-dimensional control tasks. While gradient-based methods are a promising alternative, recent works have empirically shown that gradient-based methods often perform worse than their gradient-free counterparts. We propose Dream-MPC, a novel approach that generates few candidate trajectories from a rolled-out policy and optimizes each trajectory by gradient ascent using a learned world model, uncertainty regularization and amortization of optimization iterations over time by reusing previously optimized actions. Our results on 24 continuous control tasks show that Dream-MPC can significantly improve the performance of the underlying policy and can outperform gradient-free MPC and state-of-the-art baselines. We will open source our code and more at https://dream-mpc.github.io.

</details>


### [209] [HeterSEED: Semantics-Structure Decoupling for Heterogeneous Graph Learning under Heterophily](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04594)
*Xinyi Li,Ming Li,Lu Bai,Lixin Cui,Feilong Cao,Ke Lv,Yunliang Jiang,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HeterSEED提出了一种语义-结构解耦框架，通过异构语义通道和结构感知异质性通道分别捕获局部语义与异/同配邻域信息，并自适应融合，在含强异质性的异构图上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实异构图中普遍存在异质性（heterophily），即相连节点常具有不同标签或语义角色。现有异构图表征方法主要依赖特征相似性进行消息聚合，在异质性场景下会传播误导信息，因为特征相似性与关系语义可能不一致。

Method: HeterSEED包含两个解耦通道：（1）异构语义通道，捕获类型和关系感知的局部语义；（2）结构感知异质性通道，通过伪标签引导划分同配/异配邻域，并利用基于元路径的结构权重分别聚合。最后通过节点级自适应融合机制将两通道输出结合，生成上下文相关的节点表示。

Result: 在五个真实异构图上（含百万节点/亿级边规模的大图），HeterSEED一致优于代表性异构GNN及近期异质性感知基线，在强异质性场景下优势尤为明显。

Conclusion: 理论分析证明HeterSEED在异质性异构图上严格比依赖特征相似性的标准异构GNN更具表达能力，并能证明减少异配邻域引入的预测偏差。

Abstract: Many real-world heterogeneous graphs exhibit pronounced heterophily, where connected nodes often have dissimilar labels or play different semantic roles. In such settings, standard heterogeneous graph neural networks that aggregate messages along metapaths or meta-relations primarily based on feature similarity can propagate misleading information, since feature similarity may be misaligned with underlying relational semantics. In this paper, we propose HeterSEED, a semantics-structure decoupling framework for heterogeneous graph learning under heterophily. HeterSEED decouples representation learning into a heterogeneous semantic channel that captures type- and relation-aware local semantics and a structure-aware heterophily channel that separates homophilic and heterophilic neighborhoods via pseudo-label-guided partitioning and aggregates them using metapath-based structural weights. A node-level adaptive fusion mechanism then combines the two channels to produce context-dependent node representations. Theoretically, we establish that, on heterogeneous graphs under heterophily, HeterSEED is strictly more expressive than standard heterogeneous graph neural networks that rely primarily on feature similarity and provably reduces the prediction bias introduced by heterophilic neighbors. Experiments on five real-world heterogeneous graphs, including two large-scale networks at the million-node and hundred-million-edge scale, demonstrate that HeterSEED consistently outperforms representative heterogeneous graph neural networks and recent heterophily-aware baselines, especially in strongly heterophilic regimes.

</details>


### [210] [A Queueing-Theoretic Framework for Stability Analysis of LLM Inference with KV Cache Memory Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04595)
*Chengyi Nie,Nian Si,Zijie Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个将计算和GPU内存约束同时纳入LLM推理分析的排队论框架，推导了服务稳定性的充要条件，并通过实验验证了预测精度（偏差通常在10%以内），为GPU集群的精准配置提供了理论工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理面临计算和KV缓存内存的双重瓶颈，现有方法缺乏统一理论框架来系统分析这两方面约束对服务稳定性的影响，导致GPU资源过度采购或性能违规。

Method: 构建排队论框架，显式建模计算约束和KV缓存内存约束；推导服务的稳定/不稳定条件（即队列不发散的条件）；结合到达率与稳定服务率计算所需集群规模。

Result: 理论预测的稳定性条件与真实GPU生产环境的实验结果高度吻合，偏差通常在10%以内。

Conclusion: 该排队论框架为LLM推理服务的GPU资源配置提供了精确的理论指导，可有效避免过度采购或性能不足的问题。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of large language models (LLMs) has created significant challenges for efficient inference at scale. Unlike traditional workloads, LLM inference is constrained by both computation and the memory overhead of key-value (KV) caching, which accelerates decoding but quickly exhausts GPU memory. In this paper, we introduce the first queueing-theoretic framework that explicitly incorporates both computation and GPU memory constraints into the analysis of LLM inference. Based on this framework, we derive rigorous stability and instability conditions that determine whether an LLM inference service can sustain incoming demand without unbounded queue growth. This result offers a powerful tool for system deployment, potentially addressing the core challenge of GPU provisioning. By combining an estimated request arrival rate with our derived stable service rate, operators can calculate the necessary cluster size to avoid both costly over-purchasing and performance-violating under-provisioning. We further validate our theoretical predictions through extensive experiments in real GPU production environments. Our results show that the predicted stability conditions are highly accurate, with deviations typically within 10%.

</details>


### [211] [Library learning with e-graphs on jazz harmony](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04622)
*Zeng Ren,Maddy Bowers,Xinyi Guan,Martin Rohrmeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于库学习的计算模型，通过归纳和推理爵士和弦进行中的模式，模拟人类对音乐模式的内化学习过程，并利用e-graph上的演绎解析与库学习实现高效搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够获取高度结构化的音乐模式直觉，但这些模式通常需要多次反复聆听和反思才能完全内化。现有计算模型缺乏对这种内化过程的模拟，作者希望构建一个能捕捉人类音乐模式学习过程的计算模型。

Method: 基于库学习框架：给定和弦进行语料库，在由基本和声关系组成的程序空间中搜索，为每个片段枚举可能的程序，然后联合学习一个和声模式库和重构后的程序。为高效遍历程序和库的联合空间，将演绎解析与e-graph上的库学习相结合。

Result: 通过评估程序和库的直觉性（intuitiveness），以及与人工编写的和声推导的相似性，考察模型在多大程度上捕捉了人类音乐模式学习的特征。

Conclusion: 该模型为理解人类如何内化音乐模式提供了一种计算框架，通过程序合成和库学习的方式模拟了从反复聆听中抽象出结构化知识的过程。

Abstract: Humans can acquire a highly structured intuitive understanding of musical patterns, yet these patterns often require multiple iterations of reflection and re-listening to internalize fully. To capture such an internalization process, we present a computational model for the learning of jazz harmonic patterns based on library learning. Given a corpus of harmonic progressions, our model searches over a space of programs composed of primitive harmonic relations in order to discover concise generative explanations of the corpus. The model first enumerates possible programs for each piece, and then jointly learns a library of harmonic patterns and refactored programs. To efficiently navigate the vast joint space of programs and libraries, we integrate deductive parsing with library learning on e-graphs. We explore how well our model captures aspects of human musical pattern learning by evaluating the intuitiveness of both programs and libraries, as well as similarities to human-written harmonic derivations.

</details>


### [212] [Threshold-Guided Optimization for Visual Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04653)
*Jinbin Bai,Yu Lei,Qingyu Shi,Aosong Feng,Yi Xin,Zhuoran Zhao,Fei Shen,Kaidong Yu,Jason Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于阈值的对齐框架（Threshold-Guided Alignment），通过将成对偏好优化转化为基于标量评分的二元决策任务，解决了视觉生成模型在仅有独立标量反馈时难以进行偏好对齐的问题。该方法在扩散和掩码生成范式上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的成对偏好优化方法依赖于带标注的偏好对，这在反馈以独立标量评分形式收集时扩展性受限。作者希望设计一种能够直接从标量反馈中进行对齐的方法，避免对成对数据的依赖。

Method: 1. 重新审视KL正则化对齐目标，证明最优策略隐式地将每个样本的奖励与一个难以计算的实例级基线进行比较。2. 提出用数据驱动的全局阈值代替该基线，该阈值从经验评分统计中估计。3. 将对齐问题转化为无配对数据上的二元决策任务，实现从标量反馈的直接优化。4. 引入置信加权项，强调与阈值偏差较大的样本，提高样本效率。

Result: 在扩散和掩码生成两种范式上，跨越三个测试集和五个奖励模型的实验表明，该方法在偏好对齐方面持续优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的阈值引导框架是一种简单但有原则的替代方案，可以在无需成对比较的情况下有效对齐视觉生成模型。

Abstract: Aligning large visual generative models with human feedback is often performed through pairwise preference optimization. While such approaches are conceptually simple, they fundamentally rely on annotated pairs, limiting scalability in settings where feedback is collected as independent scalar ratings. In this work, we revisit the KL-regularized alignment objective and show that the optimal policy implicitly compares each sample's reward to an instance-specific baseline that is generally intractable. We propose a threshold-guided alignment framework that replaces this oracle baseline with a data-driven global threshold estimated from empirical score statistics. This formulation turns alignment into a binary decision task on unpaired data, enabling effective optimization directly from scalar feedback. We also incorporate a confidence weighting term to emphasize samples whose scores deviate strongly from the threshold, improving sample efficiency. Experiments across both diffusion and masked generative paradigms, spanning three test sets and five reward models, show that our method consistently improves preference alignment over previous methods. These results position our threshold-guided framework as a simple yet principled alternative for aligning visual generative models without paired comparisons.

</details>


### [213] [Feature importance analysis for patient management decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04666)
*Michal Valko,Milos Hauskrecht*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了4486名心脏术后患者的电子健康记录，发现医生的实验室检查和用药决策通常可由少量关键特征准确预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解临床数据中哪些特征和属性对医生决策（尤其实验室检查和开药）影响最大。

Method: 对4486名心脏术后患者的电子健康记录数据进行统计分析，提取335项实验室检查和407项用药决策的汇总统计，识别影响医生决策的关键特征子集。

Result: 医生的实验室检查和用药决策在多数情况下可由少量特征子集准确预测。

Conclusion: 临床决策可被少量关键特征有效预测，揭示了影响医生决策的核心因素。

Abstract: The objective of this paper is to understand what characteristics and features of clinical data influence physician's decision about ordering laboratory tests or prescribing medications the most. We conduct our analysis on data and decisions extracted from electronic health records of 4486 post-surgical cardiac patients. The summary statistics for 335 different lab order decisions and 407 medication decisions are reported. We show that in many cases, physician's lab-order and medication decisions can be well predicted from a small subset of all features.

</details>


### [214] [ITBoost: Information-Theoretic Trust for Robust Boosting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04671)
*Ye Su,Longlong Zhao,Diego Garcia-Gil,Jipeng Guo,Gangchun Zhang,Jinxin Chen,Jinsong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出信息论信任提升（ITBoost）方法，通过最小描述长度原则分析样本残差轨迹的复杂性，在梯度提升过程中动态评估样本可靠性，从而在标签噪声环境下提升模型鲁棒性，同时在干净数据上保持最佳平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度提升在标签噪声下性能退化严重，因为算法会不加区分地强调大梯度样本，无法区分这些误差是来自信息丰富的困难样本还是不可靠的标签。

Method: 提出ITBoost方法，利用最小描述长度（MDL）原则衡量每个样本残差轨迹的复杂度，将残差模式波动不规则的样本视为低信任度样本，在学习过程中降低其权重。

Result: 在多种表格数据基准上，ITBoost在噪声环境下相比主流提升方法和深度表格模型展现出更强的鲁棒性，并在干净数据上保持最佳平均性能。理论上推导了更紧的泛化界。

Conclusion: 通过追踪样本残差随迭代的演化而非单次误差来评估样本可靠性，能有效提升梯度提升在标签噪声下的鲁棒性，ITBoost兼顾了噪声鲁棒性与干净数据性能。

Abstract: Gradient boosting remains a strong and widely used method for tabular data learning, but its performance often degrades when training labels are noisy. This behavior is largely related to the way boosting algorithms emphasize samples with large gradients, without explicitly accounting for whether such errors originate from informative hard cases or from unreliable labels. We address this issue by reconsidering how sample reliability is evaluated during boosting. Instead of relying on instantaneous error, we examine the evolution of each sample's residuals across iterations. Based on this insight, we propose Information-Theoretic Trust Boosting (ITBoost), which uses the Minimum Description Length principle to measure the complexity of residual trajectories. Samples whose residual patterns fluctuate in an irregular manner are treated as less trustworthy and are down-weighted during learning. Theoretically, we derive a tighter generalization bound for ITBoost under label noise. Empirical results on various tabular benchmarks indicate that ITBoost provides improved robustness in noisy environments over leading boosting and deep tabular models, while retaining best average performance on clean data.

</details>


### [215] [HEXST: Hexagonal Shifted-Window Transformer for Spatial Transcriptomics Gene Expression Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04682)
*Keunho Byeon,Jin Tae Kwak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HEXST是一种针对六边形采样空间转录组学数据设计的几何对齐Transformer模型，通过定制化的移位窗口注意力机制和六边形旋转位置编码，结合对比敏感差分目标和单细胞基础模型先验，在七个数据集上实现了优于现有模型的基因表达预测性能，有效保留了基因特异性空间异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组学成本高、通量有限，难以大规模部署；现有计算方法大多假设笛卡尔或几何无关的局部性，忽略了广泛使用的六边形采样点阵平台特性，且逐点回归目标往往导致基因表达谱过度平滑，掩盖了基因特异性的空间异质性。

Method: 提出HEXST，直接在六边形点坐标上运行，通过定制的移位窗口注意力机制和六边形旋转位置编码实现高效的局部到全局上下文建模；在逐点回归基础上引入对比敏感差分目标和来自预训练单细胞基础模型的转录组先验，增强基因空间对比度。

Result: 在七个空间转录组学数据集上，HEXST始终优于现有最先进模型，提供准确且鲁棒的空间基因表达预测，同时保持基因对比度和空间异质性。

Conclusion: HEXST通过几何对齐的设计有效解决了六边形采样平台中的基因表达预测问题，兼顾了预测精度与空间异质性保留，为空间转录组学的大规模应用提供了可行的计算方案。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics offers spatially resolved gene expression profiling within tissue sections, but its cost and limited throughput hinder large-scale deployment. To extend this capability to routine practice, recent computational methods aim to infer spatial gene expression directly from ubiquitous hematoxylin and eosin-stained histology slides. However, most existing models assume Cartesian or geometry-agnostic locality, despite the hexagonal sampling of widely used spot-array platforms, and point-wise regression objectives often yield over-smoothed gene expression profiles, obscuring gene-specific spatial heterogeneity. To address these, we propose HEXST, a geometry-aligned Transformer for spatial gene expression prediction from histology. HEXST operates directly on hexagonal spot coordinates to enable efficient local-to-global contextual modeling via tailored shifted-window attention mechanism and hexagonal rotary positional encoding. To enhance gene-wise spatial contrast, HEXST complements point-wise regression with a contrast-sensitive differential objective and transcriptomic priors from a pretrained single-cell foundation model during training. Across seven spatial transcriptomics datasets, HEXST consistently outperforms state-of-the-art models, providing accurate and robust spatial gene expression predictions while preserving gene-wise contrast and spatial heterogeneity.

</details>


### [216] [Ensuring Reliability in Programming Knowledge Tracing: A Re-evaluation of Attention-augmented Models and Experimental Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04727)
*Jaewook Kim,Hyeoncheol Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视编程知识追踪（PKT）模型，发现其性能提升很大程度上依赖于模型配置（如注意力维度设置）和序列构建实践（如忽略时间戳导致时序因果性破坏）。在通过统一超参数选择和交叉验证等受控实验后，注意力增强模型与标准DKT的性能差距显著缩小，表明架构复杂度提升并不总能带来实质性性能改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PKT模型报告了强实证性能，但其可靠性容易受到细微的实现和实验设计选择（如注意力维度、序列顺序、超参数设置）的影响，需要系统性地审视这些因素对性能评估的影响。

Method: 通过网格搜索在单一指定折上选择超参数并统一固定于所有折；重新审视注意力维度设置问题；修正学生尝试的排序方式（使用ServerTimestamp）；系统探索最大序列长度的影响；在CodeWorkout数据集上重新评估。

Result: 在受控一致设置下，注意力增强模型与标准DKT的性能差距显著缩小；增加架构复杂度并不总能转化为更优性能；发现注意力维度设置和序列时间顺序对性能估计有显著影响。

Conclusion: 本文为编程知识追踪提供了可靠且可比较评估的实践指导，强调了实验设计选择对模型性能评估的关键影响。

Abstract: Programming Knowledge Tracing (PKT) has recently advanced through hybrid approaches that integrate attention-based feature modeling for code representation with RNN-based sequential prediction. While these models report strong empirical performance, their reliability can be sensitive to subtle implementation and experimental design choices. This study revisits representative PKT models and shows that reported gains can be substantially influenced by model configuration and sequence construction practices. We identify issues in attention dimension settings that affect performance estimates, and demonstrate that improper ordering of student attempts, such as ignoring ServerTimestamp, can violate temporal causality and lead to overly optimistic results. To ensure consistent evaluation, hyperparameters are selected via grid search guided by a single designated fold and then fixed uniformly across all folds during cross-validation. We further analyze the role of assignment-wise characteristics and systematically explore the impact of maximum sequence length. Using this protocol, we re-evaluate PKT models on the CodeWorkout dataset. Our results show that, under controlled and consistent settings, the performance gap between attention-enhanced models and standard DKT is significantly reduced, and increased architectural complexity does not consistently translate into superior performance. Beyond individual model comparisons, this work provides practical guidance for reliable and comparable evaluation in programming knowledge tracing.

</details>


### [217] [Using Common Random Numbers for Simulation-based Planning with Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04732)
*Sandarbh Yadav,Frederic J Maliakkal,Harshad Khadilkar,Shivaram Kalyanakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在基于模拟的规划中使用公共随机数（CRN）来降低相对效用的方差，并提出一种简单方法：当模拟调用超过一定深度的rollout策略时使用CRN，可证明方差减少，实验验证了性能提升，并在养老金发放和Ludo游戏UCT算法中展示了实际应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机环境中，基于模拟的规划广泛使用rollout进行决策，但现有方法未充分研究公共随机数对模拟过程方差的影响。作者希望探索CRN是否能在保证理论正确性的前提下降低方差，从而提升任务性能。

Method: 提出一种简单方案：当模拟调用超过一定深度的rollout策略时使用公共随机数（CRN），并从理论上证明该方法能降低相对效用的方差。通过合成任务、养老金发放任务（单步前瞻规划）和Ludo游戏（UCT算法）进行实验验证。

Result: 理论分析证明了所提方案能降低相对效用方差；合成任务实验确认了性能提升；养老金发放任务和Ludo游戏中的部署进一步验证了实际应用中的有效性。

Conclusion: 在基于模拟的规划中使用CRN（超过一定深度时）是一种简单且可证明有效的方差降低技术，具有广泛的实际应用潜力，能提升任务性能并适用于多种规划算法。

Abstract: Simulation-based planning with rollouts is a widely-deployed technique for decision making in stochastic environments. The primary instrument of simulation-based planning is a sampling model, which is repeatedly called to generate trajectories and estimate the utilities of available actions. Among the actions thus explored, one with the maximum estimated utility is then executed. In this paper, we examine the effect of using common random numbers in the simulation process. We obtain a simple recipe for (provably) reducing variance in relative utility when simulations invoke a rollout policy beyond some depth. Experiments on synthetic tasks confirm that our scheme improves task performance. The broader significance of our innovation is apparent from two practical applications: (1) single-step lookahead planning in a pension-disbursement task, and (2) a deployment of the well-known UCT algorithm for the game of Ludo.

</details>


### [218] [OSAQ: Outlier Self-Absorption for Accurate Low-bit LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04738)
*Zhikai Li,Zhen Dong,Xuewen Liu,Jing Zhang,Qingyi Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OSAQ提出了一种基于Hessian矩阵零空间的加性权重变换方法，通过离线吸收来抑制LLM权重量化中的离群值，无需引入推理开销，在2-bit量化中与GPTQ结合可将困惑度降低40%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型参数规模巨大导致推理时资源消耗高和延迟大，后训练权重量化虽能缓解内存瓶颈，但权重中的系统性离群值严重影响低比特量化性能，现有方法（如缩放、旋转）效果仍不理想。

Method: 利用Hessian矩阵在不同输入下的低秩一致性，识别其稳定零空间，然后通过线性组合零空间中的向量构造加性权重变换，在不影响任务损失的前提下抑制权重离群值；该变换可离线吸收到权重中，无需层间变换或推理开销；构造过程有闭式解，无需训练或迭代。

Result: OSAQ有效抑制离群值并提升低比特量化性能。在2-bit量化中，OSAQ与GPTQ结合相比原始GPTQ困惑度降低超过40%。

Conclusion: OSAQ通过二阶低秩特性引导的加性权重抑制方法，在不引入推理开销的情况下显著提升了LLM低比特权重量化的性能，尤其适用于极低比特场景。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities. However, their massive parameter scale leads to significant resource consumption and latency during inference. Post-training weight-only quantization offers a promising solution by reducing model size and accelerating token generation through alleviating the memory-bound issue. Nevertheless, the presence of inherent systematic outliers in weights continues to be a major obstacle. While existing methods, such as scaling and rotation, attempt to address this issue, the performance remains unsatisfactory. In this paper, we propose Outlier Self-Absorption Quantization (OSAQ), which performs additive weight suppression guided by the second-order low-rank property for low-bit weight-only quantization of LLMs. Specifically, we observe that the Hessian exhibits low-rank consistency across different inputs, with certain directions consistently showing vanishing curvature. Leveraging this property, we identify a stable null space of the Hessian and then construct an additive weight transformation by linearly combining the vectors within this null space, thereby suppressing weight outliers without affecting the task loss. This additive transformation can be absorbed into the weights offline, requiring no inter-layer transformations and introducing no inference overhead. Moreover, the construction is efficiently achieved by a closed-form solution, without resource-intensive training or iterative procedures. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OSAQ effectively suppresses outliers and enhances low-bit quantization performance. For instance, in 2-bit quantization, OSAQ, when integrated with GPTQ, achieves over 40% lower perplexity compared to vanilla GPTQ.

</details>


### [219] [Knowledge-Free Correlated Agreement for Incentivizing Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04747)
*Leon Witt,Togrul Abbasli,Kentaroh Toyoda,Wojciech Samek,Lucy Klinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KFCA方法，在不依赖真实标签、公开测试集或分布知识的情况下奖励联邦学习中的客户端贡献，通过诚实多数假设解决标签翻转攻击问题，并在LLM适配器微调和PCB检测任务中验证了实时奖励计算的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的联邦学习激励机制（如Correlated Agreement）容易受到标签翻转攻击，且通常需要依赖真实标签或公开测试集，限制了其在去中心化场景中的实用性。

Method: 提出Knowledge-Free Correlated Agreement (KFCA)，在分类报告和诚实多数假设下，通过不依赖任何先验知识的方式计算客户端贡献，实现严格真实性（strictly truthful）。

Result: 在联邦LLM适配器微调和真实PCB检测任务上的实验表明，KFCA支持高效的实时奖励计算，适用于去中心化和基于区块链的激励机制设计。

Conclusion: KFCA为联邦学习提供了一种无需知识依赖、防标签翻转的安全激励机制，适合在去中心化环境中部署。

Abstract: We introduce Knowledge-Free Correlated Agreement (KFCA) to reward client contributions in federated learning (FL) without relying on ground truth, a public test set, or distribution knowledge. Under categorical reports and an honest majority, KFCA is strictly truthful, addressing the label-flipping vulnerability of Correlated Agreement (CA). We evaluate KFCA on federated LLM adapter tuning and a real-world PCB inspection task, showing efficient real-time reward computation suitable for decentralized and blockchain-based incentive designs.

</details>


### [220] [AxMoE: Characterizing the Impact of Approximate Multipliers on Mixture-of-Experts DNN Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04754)
*Omkar B Shende,Marcello Traiola,Gayathri Ananthanarayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了近似乘法对混合专家(MoE)架构的影响，提出了AxMoE。评估了三种MoE变体与密集基线在多种CNN和ViT架构上的表现，发现：无需重训练时密集基线鲁棒性最强；重训练后不同架构恢复程度差异显著，ResNet-20可完全恢复，而VGG在激进近似下不可逆退化，ViT-Small上Hard MoE在激进近似下优于密集模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上的DNN推理需要同时提升精度、计算效率和能效。近似计算和MoE架构分别作为两种独立的高效推理路径已被研究，但二者之间的相互作用完全未被探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，首次研究近似乘法对MoE架构的影响。

Method: 评估三种MoE变体（Hard MoE、Soft MoE、Cluster MoE）与密集基线，覆盖三种CNN架构（ResNet-20、VGG11_bn、VGG19_bn）在CIFAR-100上，以及Vision Transformer（ViT-Small）在Tiny ImageNet-200上。使用EvoApproxLib库中的8种8位有符号乘法器（包括一个精确基线），分别在无重训练和近似感知重训练两种设置下进行评估。

Result: 无重训练时，密集基线在所有CNN架构上鲁棒性最强；ViT-Small上所有拓扑退化速率相当。近似感知重训练后，恢复程度因架构、拓扑和乘法器而异：ResNet-20完全恢复；VGG在中等近似下恢复，激进近似下除Cluster MoE on VGG11_bn外均不可逆失效；ViT-Small上Hard MoE在激进近似下以相等归一化推理成本优于密集模型。

Conclusion: 本文首次揭示了近似乘法与MoE架构之间的相互作用，表明不同架构和路由策略对近似计算的鲁棒性存在显著差异，为未来近似MoE硬件-软件协同设计策略奠定了基础。

Abstract: Deep neural network (DNN) inference at the edge demands simultaneous improvements in accuracy, computational efficiency, and energy consumption. Approximate computing and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have each been studied as independent routes towards efficient inference, the former by replacing exact arithmetic with low-power approximate multipliers, the latter by routing inputs through specialized expert sub-networks to enable conditional computation. However, their interaction remains entirely unexplored. This paper presents AxMoE, the first study of the impact of approximate multiplication on MoE DNN architectures. We evaluate three MoE variants: Hard MoE, Soft MoE, and Cluster MoE against dense baselines across three CNN architectures (ResNet-20, VGG11_bn, VGG19_bn) on CIFAR-100 and a Vision Transformer (ViT-Small) on Tiny ImageNet-200 dataset, using eight 8-bit signed multipliers (including one exact baseline) from the EvoApproxLib library. Results show that, without retraining, the Dense baseline is the most resilient topology across all CNN architectures, whereas on ViT-Small, all topologies degrade at comparable rates regardless of routing strategy. After approximate-aware retraining, recovery varies substantially across architectures, topologies, and multipliers. ResNet-20 achieves full recovery across the entire multiplier range, whereas VGG architectures recover at moderate multipliers but fail irreversibly at aggressive ones for all topologies except Cluster MoE on VGG11_bn; on ViT-Small, Hard MoE outperforms Dense under aggressive approximation at equal normalized inference cost. These results pave the way for future approximate MoE hardware-software co-design strategies.

</details>


### [221] [Cognitive Twins: Investigating Personalized Thinking Model Building and Its Performance Enhancement with Human-in-the-Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04761)
