*Wu-Yuin Hwang,Nur Alif Ilyasa,Muhammad Irfan Luthfi,Yuniar Indrihapsari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出个性化思维模型(PTM)，一种基于Marzano新教育目标分类学的五层可解释学习者表征，通过大语言模型、句子嵌入、降维和共识聚类构建，并在40名参与者七周实验中通过自动评估(F1约75%)、用户评估(Likert均分约4.3)和语义对齐验证证明其保真度良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习者模型缺乏对学习者深层思维模式(元认知、价值观等)的表征能力，且可解释性不足。本文旨在构建一个层次化、可解释的认知表征，克隆学习者的思维模型并建立认知数字孪生。

Method: 基于Marzano新教育目标分类学，设计五层结构(行为实例→行为模式→认知习惯→元认知倾向→自我系统价值观)。采用LLM(Gemini 2.5 Pro)推理、句子嵌入、降维和共识聚类流水线构建。通过三项方法评估：原子信息点匹配的自动评估、用户Likert量表评估、语义对齐验证。

Result: 自动评估F1分数74.57%(HITL前)和75.48%(HITL后)；用户评估均分4.26和4.30(五分制)；主题一致性从行为层0.436上升到核心价值层0.626，词汇重叠从0.114下降到0.007。

Conclusion: PTM生成的输出具有可接受的保真度，用户普遍认为能反映其思维过程，且各层之间呈现出符合语义抽象规律的模式。

Abstract: This paper presents the Personalized Thinking Model (PTM), a hierarchical and interpretable learner representation designed for AI supported education. PTM organizes evidence from learner journals into a five-layer structure covering behavioral instances, behavioral patterns, cognitive routines, metacognitive tendencies, and self-system values. PTM is grounded in Marzano's New Taxonomy of Educational Objectives and tries to clone learner's thinking model and build cognitive twin. It was constructed using a pipeline that combines large language model inference (Gemini 2.5 Pro), sentence embeddings, dimensionality reduction, and consensus clustering. This paper evaluates PTM fidelity through three methods applied to 40 participants in a seven-week study. First, automatic evaluation using atomic information point matching yielded an overall F1 score of 74.57% before human-in-the-loop (HITL) refinement and 75.48% after refinement. Second, user evaluation using a Likert scale produced mean ratings of 4.26 and 4.30 on a five-point scale for pre and post-HITL conditions respectively. Third, semantic alignment verification showed that topic coherence increased from 0.436 at the behavioral layer to 0.626 at the core value layer, while lexical overlap with journal vocabulary decreased from 0.114 to 0.007 across those same layers. These results suggest that the PTM produces outputs with acceptable fidelity, was generally perceived by users as reflecting their thinking, and showed a pattern consistent with semantic abstraction across layers.

</details>


### [222] [Bilinear Mamba-Koopman Neural MPC for Varying Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04793)
*Matan Pagi,Zohar Sorek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Bilinear Mamba-Koopman Neural MPC，通过在潜动态中引入控制依赖耦合来扩展标准Koopman神经MPC模型，使得有效算子能适应当前输入。该模型是线性条件独立公式的严格泛化，参数增加不到1%，支持精确模型雅可比矩阵，可在标准信赖域假设下实现单调下降和KKT收敛。在CartPole和RSCP基准测试中，模型在时变和非时变场景下匹配或提升了预测精度，尤其在时变RSCP任务中闭环增益显著，并且在延迟重规划实验中表现出更优的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Koopman神经MPC模型强制系统算子独立于当前控制输入，这种条件独立性约束限制了模型在单个MPC时域内适应时变动态的能力，尤其在执行陈旧规划时表现欠佳。现有方法无法在保持凸性的同时引入控制-状态耦合，亟需一种最小化扩展方案来增强模型对时变条件的适应性。

Method: 提出双线性Mamba-Koopman神经MPC框架，在潜动态中引入控制依赖的低秩耦合项，使原有时不变Koopman算子变为控制输入的函数。通过低秩结构（参数增加<1%）保持计算效率，同时推导出精确的模型雅可比矩阵，支持基于信赖域的序列凸规划（SCP）求解，并给出单调下降和KKT收敛的理论保证。

Result: 在CartPole和RSCP基准上，在训练噪声平均后，模型在每个单元上匹配或提升了预测精度，在存在控制-状态耦合的结构性场景中增益显著。闭环控制方面，RSCP时变任务中迭代SCP显著改善时域内适应性和训练稳定性；CartPole时变任务中增益温和但一致。延迟重规划实验中，双线性模型在陈旧规划执行下退化更平缓，CartPole上有持续优势，RSCP上鲁棒性裕度更大。

Conclusion: 控制依赖的潜动态为时变条件下鲁棒MPC提供了一种简单有效的机制。双线性Koopman模型作为标准线性条件独立公式的严格泛化，在几乎不增加参数的情况下提升了预测精度和控制鲁棒性，特别在时变系统和延迟重规划场景中表现出显著优势。

Abstract: Koopman-based neural MPC models generate time-varying dynamics from historical data, but preserve convexity by enforcing that the system operator is independent of the current control input. This conditional independence constraint limits adaptation to changing dynamics within a single MPC horizon, particularly under time-varying conditions and under stale-plan execution.
  We propose Bilinear Mamba-Koopman Neural MPC, a minimal extension that introduces control-dependent coupling in the latent dynamics, allowing the effective operator to adapt to the current input. The resulting model is a strict generalization of the standard linear, conditional-independence formulation, adds less than 1% parameters through a low-rank structure, and admits exact model Jacobians that enable efficient Sequential Convex Programming (SCP) with monotone-descent and KKT convergence results under standard trust-region assumptions.
  Across CartPole and RSCP benchmarks in time-invariant and time-varying regimes, the proposed model matches or improves forecasting accuracy on every cell when training noise is averaged out, with strict gains where control-state coupling is structurally present. Its main closed-loop gains appear in the RSCP TV task, where iterative SCP improves adaptation within the horizon and substantially stabilizes training; in CartPole TV, the gains are modest but consistent. In delayed re-planning experiments on the time-varying variants, the bilinear model degrades more gracefully under stale-plan execution, maintaining a consistent advantage on CartPole TV and a substantially larger robustness margin on RSCP TV. These results show that control-dependent latent dynamics provide a simple and effective mechanism for robust MPC under varying conditions.

</details>


### [223] [Improving FMQA via Initial Training Data Design Considering Marginal Bit Coverage in One-Hot Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04825)
*Taiga Hayashi,Yuya Seki,Kotaro Terada,Yosuke Mukasa,Shuta Kikuchi,Shu Tanaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对FMQA方法中one-hot编码导致某些二进制变量在初始训练数据中从未激活的问题，提出通过拉丁超立方采样和Sobol'序列实现完全边际位覆盖，确保每个二进制变量至少激活一次，在17和32变量的人动力飞行器机翼形状优化基准上，两种方法平均最终巡航速度均优于基线FMQA，且32变量问题上优势更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FMQA在通过one-hot编码处理整数或离散化连续变量时，均匀随机初始采样会导致许多二进制变量在初始训练数据中从未激活，对应的FM参数无法从观测响应中获得直接梯度更新，影响优化效果。

Method: 使用拉丁超立方采样(LHS)和Sobol'序列两种空间填充采样方法设计初始训练数据，实现完全边际位覆盖，确保每个通过one-hot编码得到的二进制变量至少取值为1一次，分别得到LHS-FMQA和Sobol'-FMQA。

Result: 在人动力飞行器机翼形状优化基准上（17和32个设计变量），两种提出方法的平均最终巡航速度数值上均高于基线FMQA，且在32变量问题上的优势更加明显。

Conclusion: 通过空间填充采样方法实现完全边际位覆盖，能够有效解决FMQA中二进制变量激活不足的问题，提升优化性能，尤其在更高维度问题上效果更显著。

Abstract: Factorization machine with quadratic-optimization annealing (FMQA) is a black-box optimization method that combines a factorization machine (FM) surrogate with QUBO-based search by an Ising machine. When FMQA is applied to integer or discretized continuous variables via one-hot encoding, uniform random initial sampling can leave many binary variables never active in the initial training data, and the corresponding FM parameters receive no direct gradient updates from the observed responses. We address this by designing the initial training data to achieve complete marginal bit coverage, namely, ensuring that every binary variable obtained by one-hot encoding takes the value one at least once. We use two space-filling sampling methods, Latin hypercube sampling (LHS) and the Sobol' sequence, yielding LHS-FMQA and Sobol'-FMQA. On the human-powered aircraft wing-shape optimization benchmark with 17 and 32 design variables, both proposed methods achieved numerically higher mean final cruising speeds than the baseline FMQA, with the advantage more pronounced on the 32-variable problem.

</details>


### [224] [Trustworthy Federated Label Distribution Learning under Annotation Quality Disparity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04827)
*Junxiang Wu,Zhiqiang Kou,Hongwei Zeng,Wenke Huang,Biao Liu,Hanlin Gu,Yuheng Jia,Di Jiang,Yang Liu,Xin Geng,Qiang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedQual，一个面向联邦标签分布学习（Fed-LDL）的质量感知框架，通过全局语义锚引导的客户端质量自适应训练和基于有效可靠信息的服务器端重聚合，解决异构标注质量下的信任问题，并构建了四个新基准数据集，理论证明客户端特定校准优于统一校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标签分布学习（LDL）依赖高质量标签分布，但其获取成本高且易含噪声；在联邦学习场景中，数据隔离进一步导致各客户端标注质量异构，传统基于样本量的聚合策略（如FedAvg）失效。因此，需要一种能够感知并适应客户端标注质量差异的联邦LDL方法。

Method: 提出FedQual框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 质量自适应客户端训练：利用全局语义锚引导低质量客户端校准，同时保留高质量客户端的自主性；(2) 可靠性感知服务器聚合：根据客户端有效可靠信息量而非原始样本量重新加权聚合贡献。

Result: 构建了四个新的Fed-LDL基准数据集（FER-LDL、FI-LDL、PIPAL-LDL、KADID-LDL），在可控标注质量差异下进行实验，验证了FedQual的有效性。理论证明在异构监督质量下，客户端特定校准严格优于任何统一校准。

Conclusion: FedQual通过质量感知机制有效解决了联邦LDL中异构标注质量带来的信任问题，实验和理论分析均支持其优越性。

Abstract: Label Distribution Learning (LDL) models supervision as an instance-wise probability distribution, enabling fine-grained learning under inherent ambiguity, but its success relies on high-fidelity label distributions that are costly to obtain and thus often noisy. Motivated by privacy-sensitive applications, we study Federated Label Distribution Learning (Fed-LDL), where data isolation further induces heterogeneous annotation quality across clients, making local updates unevenly reliable and breaking sample-size-based aggregation (e.g., FedAvg). To address this trust dilemma, we propose FedQual, a quality-aware Fed-LDL framework with two coupled mechanisms: (i) quality-adaptive client training guided by a global semantic anchor that calibrates low-quality clients while preserving high-quality autonomy, and (ii) reliability-aware server aggregation that reweights client contributions by effective reliable information rather than raw sample size. To enable rigorous evaluation, we construct four new Fed-LDL benchmarks (FER-LDL, FI-LDL, PIPAL-LDL, and KADID-LDL) with controlled annotation quality disparity. We further provide a theoretical guarantee showing that under heterogeneous supervision quality, client-specific calibration is strictly better than any uniform calibration. Extensive experiments on the proposed benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of FedQual.

</details>


### [225] [Concurrence of Symmetry Breaking and Nonlocality Phase Transitions in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04830)
*Yifan F. Zhang,Fangjun Hu,Guangkuo Liu,Mert Okyay,Xun Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散模型在生成动力学中的相变现象，发现非局域性和对称性破缺两种临界时间在现代化扩散Transformer中几乎同时发生，首次在实践中统一了这两种相变概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在生成过程中存在一个关键时间窗口，呈现出相变现象。已有两种互补的观点：对称性破缺（轨迹分岔进入能量景观的不同语义极小值）和非局域性（局部去噪失效）。但两者是否同时发生尚不清楚，作者旨在统一这两种相变概念。

Method: 通过评估现代扩散Transformer的生成轨迹动力学和结果，观察非局域性和对称性破缺两种临界时间的发生时刻，并分析其同步性。

Result: 观察到非局域性和对称性破缺的临界时间几乎同时发生，两种相变在现代化扩散Transformer中呈现出近同时性。

Conclusion: 首次在实践中统一了两种相变概念，为判断扩散模型何时以及为何依赖条件控制和全局去噪提供了具体诊断方法，有助于模型效率的原则性评估以及避免不必要计算的架构和采样方案设计。

Abstract: Diffusion models undergo a phase transition in a critical time window during generation dynamics, with two complementary diagnoses of criticality. The symmetry breaking picture views the critical window as when trajectories bifurcate into different semantic minima of the energy landscape, whereas the nonlocality picture views the critical window as when local denoising fails. We study whether two notions of such phase transitions are concurrent in modern diffusion transformers. By evaluating the dynamics and outcomes of the generation trajectory, we observe a near-simultaneous occurrence of the non-locality and symmetry breaking critical times. Our work is the first to unify the two notions of phase transitions in practice: it provides a concrete diagnostic for when and why diffusion models rely on conditioning and global denoising, enabling principled evaluation of model efficiency and guiding the design of architectures and sampling schemes that avoid unnecessary computation.

</details>


### [226] [Replay-Based Continual Learning for Physics-Informed Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04832)
*Yizheng Wang,Mohammad Sadegh Eshaghi,Xiaoying Zhuang,Timon Rabczuk,Yinghua Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于回放的持续学习方法，将物理信息引入基于Transolver的神经算子中，无需标签数据即可在新分布数据上实现快速适应，同时缓解灾难性遗忘，在三个物理问题上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子在分布内数据上表现良好，但在面对分布外数据时性能显著下降，缺乏有效的持续学习能力来适应新数据分布。

Method: 基于Transolver架构，采用回放式持续学习策略：通过蒸馏约束保留少量旧数据以缓解灾难性遗忘，同时利用迁移学习LoRA实现对新数据的快速适应；完全物理驱动，无需标签数据。

Result: 在Darcy流、二维超弹性脑肿瘤和三维线性弹性三重周期极小曲面三个代表性问题上，该方法有效缓解了灾难性遗忘，保持了快速适应能力，并显著提高了训练效率，减少了额外内存和计算开销。

Conclusion: 所提出的回放式持续学习策略能够有效提升神经算子对分布外数据的适应能力，相比传统联合训练策略在效率和资源消耗上具有优势。

Abstract: Neural operators generally demonstrate strong predictive performance on in-distribution (ID) problems. However, a critical limitation of existing methods is their significant performance degradation when encountering out-of-distribution (OOD) data. To address this issue, this work introduces continual learning into physics-informed neural operators, with particular emphasis on neural operators built upon the Transolver architecture, and proposes a simple yet effective replay-based continual learning strategy. The proposed method is fully physics-informed and does not require labeled data, relying solely on input fields together with physical constraints for training. When new OOD data become available, a small number of past data are incorporated through a distillation-based constraint to preserve previously acquired knowledge and alleviate catastrophic forgetting. Meanwhile, a transfer learning LoRA is employed to enable rapid adaptation to the new data. The proposed framework is systematically validated on three representative physical problems, including the Darcy flow problem in fluid mechanics, a two-dimensional hyperelastic brain tumor problem in biomechanics, and a three-dimensional linear elastic Triply Periodic Minimal Surfaces problem in solid mechanics. The results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively mitigates catastrophic forgetting on previously learned data while maintaining fast adaptability to new data. Compared with conventional joint training strategies, the proposed method significantly improves training efficiency while reducing additional memory usage and computational cost.

</details>


### [227] [Bridging Input Feature Spaces Towards Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04834)
*Moshe Eliasof,Krishna Sri Ipsit Mantri,Beatrice Bevilacqua,Bruno Ribeiro,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ALL-IN方法，通过将节点特征投影到共享随机空间并基于协方差统计构建表示，实现跨不同输入特征图数据集的迁移学习，无需架构调整或重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图学习缺乏共享输入空间，不同数据集的特征在语义、值范围和维度上存在差异，导致图模型无法跨数据集泛化，限制了其作为基础模型的应用。

Method: 将节点特征投影到共享随机空间，通过协方差统计构建节点表示，消除对原始特征空间的依赖；理论证明了节点-协方差算子及节点表示在分布上对输入特征排列不变，且期望算子对一般正交变换具有不变性。

Result: ALL-IN在多种节点级和图级任务上，面对未见数据集和新输入特征时均取得了强性能，且无需架构改变或重新训练。

Conclusion: 提出了一种输入无关、可迁移的图模型构建方向，为图基础模型的跨数据集泛化提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Unlike vision and language domains, graph learning lacks a shared input space, as input features differ across graph datasets not only in semantics, but also in value ranges and dimensionality. This misalignment prevents graph models from generalizing across datasets, limiting their use as foundation models. In this work, we propose ALL-IN, a simple and theoretically grounded method that enables transferability across datasets with different input features. Our approach projects node features into a shared random space and constructs representations via covariance-based statistics, thus eliminating dependence on the original feature space. We show that the computed node-covariance operators and the resulting node representations are invariant in distribution to permutations of the input features. We further demonstrate that the expected operator exhibits invariance to general orthogonal transformations of the input features. Empirically, ALL-IN achieves strong performance across diverse node- and graph-level tasks on unseen datasets with new input features, without requiring architecture changes or retraining. These results point to a promising direction for input-agnostic, transferable graph models.

</details>


### [228] [Regime-Conditioned Evaluation in Multi-Context Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04895)
*Noel Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对40篇迁移贝叶斯优化论文的审计，发现98%的研究未将预算/搜索空间比作为控制变量，提出便携式制度得分(PRS)来预测不同制度下的最优采集函数选择，并验证了无免费排行榜命题，最终提出RegimePlanner方法在多项基准中取得领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迁移BO比较通常估计采集函数选择对隐藏制度变量的平均处理效应，而实践者需要针对其特定先验质量、预算比率和指标的条件效应。作者希望揭示并量化这些制度变量对采集函数排名的影响。

Method: 1) 对40篇迁移BO论文进行审计；2) 在GDSC2基准上改变预算B验证排名反转；3) 提出PRS=(B/|A|)(1-rho)量化制度效应；4) 构建层次模型在79个条件上验证；5) 提出RegimePlanner在线估计rho并切换采集函数；6) 进行预注册预测验证。

Result: 98%论文未将B/|A|作为控制轴；改变预算导致排名反转（B=50时Greedy优于UCB 0.050，B=100时UCB优于Greedy 0.035）；层次模型beta=0.50(p=1.1e-9)；19%条件落在|优势|<0.01的等价区；RegimePlanner在HPO-B的16个搜索空间全部获胜，超匹配单上下文Oracle 18%；预注册预测准确率67.5%，EMA先验族内超90%。

Conclusion: 无条件排名不稳定，因为条件平均处理效应(CATE)跨制度变号时，报告的平均处理效应(ATE)成为基准混合比例的函数。建议实践协议：报告B/|A|、rho、K和指标以及任何声称的采集函数优势。

Abstract: Published transfer-BO comparisons often estimate an average treatment effect of acquisition choice over hidden regime variables, while practitioners need the conditional effect for their specific prior quality, budget ratio, and metric. An audit of 40 transfer-BO papers from NeurIPS, ICML, ICLR, AISTATS, UAI, TMLR, JMLR, and AutoML-Conf (2022-2025) finds that 98% never vary B/|A| as a controlled axis. On the same GDSC2 benchmark, changing only the budget reverses the ranking: at B=50, Greedy outperforms UCB by 0.050 Hit@1, while at B=100, UCB outperforms Greedy by 0.035. We capture this transition with the Portable Regime Score PRS=(B/|A|)(1-rho), where rho is the prior rank correlation and can be estimated from pilot contexts before the main comparison. Across 79 conditions spanning chemistry, drug-response biology, and HPO, a hierarchical model gives beta=0.50 (p=1.1e-9), and 19% of conditions fall in an equivalence zone where |advantage|<0.01 Hit@1. In five published reversal cases, PRS predicts the winner from pre-comparison observables. A No-Free-Leaderboard proposition explains why unconditional rankings are unstable: when CATE changes sign across regimes, the reported ATE becomes a function of benchmark mixture. RegimePlanner, which estimates rho online and switches acquisition accordingly, wins all 16 HPO-B search spaces at B=100 and exceeds the matched {Greedy,UCB} per-context oracle on GDSC2 by 18%. Pre-registered predictions achieve 27/40=67.5% overall accuracy and above 90% within EMA prior families. The practical protocol is simple: report B/|A|, rho, K, and metric alongside any claimed acquisition advantage.

</details>


### [229] [A geometric relation of the error introduced by sampling a language model's output distribution to its internal state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04899)
*Albert F. Modenbach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现GPT类语言模型在预测概率分布分散的生成点上对单token变化敏感，将其视为几何性质，推导出依赖于token嵌入几何的$\mathfrak{so}(n)$值1-形式。尽管源于纯几何，其曲率具有语义意义：在国际象棋推理任务中，曲率与现成指令调优模型的世界模型耦合，变换按棋盘区域聚类并遵循棋子重要性。这表明token空间几何直接反映模型内部问题表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究GPT类语言模型在预测概率分布分散的生成点上对单token变化敏感性的几何本质，以及这种几何性质如何反映模型的内部问题表征。

Method: 将语言模型对单token变化的敏感性视为几何性质，推导出$\mathfrak{so}(n)$值1-形式，该形式仅依赖于token嵌入的几何结构，并研究其曲率性质。

Result: 发现尽管曲率源自纯几何，但在国际象棋推理任务中具有语义意义：曲率与指令调优模型的世界模型耦合，变换按棋盘区域聚类，且尊重棋子的重要性。

Conclusion: Token空间的几何结构直接反映了模型内部如何表征问题，为理解语言模型的内部表示提供了几何视角。

Abstract: GPT-style language models are sensitive to single-token changes at generation points where the predicted probability distribution is spread across multiple tokens. Viewing this sensitivity as a geometric property, we derive an $\mathfrak{so}(n)$-valued 1-form that depends only on the geometry of the token embeddings. Despite this purely geometric origin, we show that its curvature is semantically meaningful: On chess reasoning tasks, the curvature couples to the world model of an off-the-shelf instruction-tuned model, with transformations clustering by board region and respecting piece importance. Our findings suggest that token space geometry directly reflects how models internally represent problems.

</details>


### [230] [Breaking the Quality-Privacy Tradeoff in Tabular Data Generation via In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04911)
*Xinyan Han,Yan Lu,Xiaoyu Lin,Yuanyuan Jiang,Yuanrui Wang,Xuanyue Li,Wenchao Zou,Xingxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DiffICL方法，通过上下文学习范式利用预训练结构先验，在小样本表格数据合成中同时提升数据质量和隐私保护，打破了传统方法中质量与隐私的权衡困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格生成模型在小数据场景下存在明显的质量-隐私权衡：提升数据质量往往以增加训练样本记忆程度为代价，从而削弱隐私保护。这是因为小训练集使得模型难以区分可泛化结构与样本特定模式。

Method: 提出DiffICL，将表格数据生成转化为上下文学习问题。不从头拟合每个数据集，而是利用从大量数据集中预训练的结构先验，从有限上下文中推断数据分布，而非记忆单个样本。

Result: 在14个真实世界数据集上，DiffICL同时提升了数据质量和隐私保护，生成的合成数据能有效进行数据增强。

Conclusion: 通过更好的训练范式可以改善质量-隐私权衡问题。

Abstract: Tabular data synthesis aims to generate high-quality data while preserving privacy. However, we find that existing tabular generative models exhibit a clear tradeoff in the small-data regime: improving data quality typically comes at the cost of increased memorization of training samples, thereby weakening privacy protection. This tradeoff arises because small training sets make it difficult for dataset-specific generative models to distinguish generalizable structure from sample-specific patterns. To address this, we propose DiffICL, which formulates tabular data generation as an in-context learning problem. Instead of fitting each dataset from scratch,DiffICL leverages pretrained structural priors learned from a large collection of datasets, enabling it to infer data distributions from limited context rather than memorizing individual samples. We evaluate DiffICL on 14 real-world datasets. Results show that DiffICL improves both data quality and privacy, and generate synthetic data that provides effective data augmentation. Our findings suggest that the quality-privacy tradeoff can be improved through better training paradigms.

</details>


### [231] [Koopman Identification of Nonlinear Systems via Reservoir Liftings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04917)
*Weibin Gu,Chen Yang,Lu Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RC-Koopman框架，将储备池计算（Reservoir Computing）解释为一种有状态的有限维Koopman字典，通过谱半径控制时间记忆深度，利用回声状态属性（ESP）保证提升Koopman近似的良态性和数值条件，并基于相关性设计谱半径选择算法。实验表明，该方法在非线性动力学的重建精度与动态稳定性之间取得了优于EDMD和Hankel提升方法的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Koopman算子理论在非线性动力系统中学习可处理的线性表示时，常受限于字典选择、时序记忆编码和数值病态问题。现有方法（如EDMD和Hankel提升）在这三个维度上存在不足，亟需一种能同时解决这些问题的框架。

Method: 借鉴储备池计算（RC）范式，将储备池解释为有状态的有限维Koopman字典，其时间深度由谱半径显式控制；利用回声状态属性（ESP）保证提升近似的良态性和数值条件；提出基于相关性的谱半径选择算法，使储备池记忆与主导系统时间尺度对齐。

Result: 在合成基准测试上，RC-Koopman在非线性动力学重建精度与动态稳定性之间取得了优于EDMD和Hankel提升方法的平衡。分析揭示了储备池的有限记忆决定了哪些Koopman特征函数可从提升特征中观测到。

Conclusion: RC-Koopman通过将储备池计算与Koopman算子理论有机结合，为非线性动力系统的线性表示学习提供了一种兼具理论保证和实用性能的新范式，有效缓解了字典选择、记忆编码和数值条件三大挑战。

Abstract: Learning tractable linear representations of nonlinear dynamical systems via Koopman operator theory is often hindered by dictionary selection, temporal memory encoding, and numerical ill-conditioning. Inspired by Reservoir Computing (RC) paradigm, this paper introduces the RC-Koopman framework, which interprets reservoir as a stateful, finite-dimensional Koopman dictionary whose temporal depth is explicitly controlled by its spectral radius. We show that the Echo State Property (ESP) guarantees well-posedness and favorable numerical conditioning of the lifted Koopman approximation. A correlation-based spectral radius selection algorithm aligns reservoir memory with dominant system timescales. Analysis reveals how the finite memory of the reservoir determines which Koopman eigenfunctions remain observable from the lifted features. Evaluation on synthetic benchmarks demonstrates that RC-Koopman achieves a favorable balance between reconstruction accuracy of the underlying nonlinear dynamics and dynamical stability, compared to Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition (EDMD) and Hankel-based lifting approaches. Code available at: https://github.com/NEAR-the-future/RC-Koopman.git

</details>


### [232] [Adaptive Inverted-Index Routing for Granular Mixtures-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04952)
*Klaus-Rudolf Kladny,Maximilian Mordig,Bernhard Schölkopf,Michael Muehlebach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AIR-MoE提出了一种基于向量量化的自适应倒排索引路由架构，通过两阶段过程（粗筛候选集+细粒度评分）近似实现top-k专家路由，在保持模型架构不变的前提下显著降低细粒度MoE的计算开销，并在实验中优于现有路由方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度MoE（使用更多小型专家）能提升性能，但专家数量的增加导致路由计算成本急剧上升，甚至成为计算瓶颈。现有方法往往对专家结构施加约束，缺乏通用的低开销路由方案。

Method: AIR-MoE采用两阶段路由：第一阶段利用向量量化（VQ）将token分配到码字，通过倒排索引快速构建候选专家集（粗筛）；第二阶段仅对候选集中的专家计算精确路由分数（细粒度评分）。该设计可替换标准路由器，无需修改模型架构或损失函数。

Result: 在细粒度MoE设置下，AIR-MoE相比现有路由方法取得了更优的性能，同时避免了全量专家评分带来的计算开销。理论分析给出了AIR-MoE召回率的下界。

Conclusion: AIR-MoE通过倒排索引与量化编码实现了高效且通用的细粒度MoE路由，在计算效率与模型性能之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) models enable scalable transformer architectures by activating only a subset of experts per token. Recent evidence suggests that performance improves with increasingly granular experts, i.e., many small experts instead of a few large ones. However, this regime substantially increases routing cost, which can dominate computation. We introduce adaptive inverted-index routing for MoE (AIR-MoE), an inverted-index-inspired routing architecture based on vector quantization (VQ). In a first stage, AIR-MoE performs coarse shortlisting by assigning tokens to VQ codewords to construct a candidate set of experts. In a second stage, fine scoring computes exact routing scores restricted to this shortlist. This two-stage procedure approximates true top-k routing while avoiding full expert scoring and, in contrast to prior work, imposing no structural constraints on expert parameters. AIR-MoE serves as a drop-in replacement for standard routers and requires no modifications to the model architecture or loss function. We further provide a lower bound on the mass recall achieved by AIR-MoE that yields insights into its inner workings. Empirically, we demonstrate that AIR-MoE achieves improved performance compared to existing routing approaches in granular MoE settings.

</details>


### [233] [KernelBench-X: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Evaluating LLM-Generated GPU Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04956)
*Han Wang,Jintao Zhang,Kai Jiang,Haoxu Wang,Jianfei Chen,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KernelBench-X是一个面向LLM生成Triton内核的细粒度基准，通过15个类别176个任务系统评估了5种方法，发现任务结构比方法设计更能决定正确性（解释9.4% vs 3.3%方差），迭代精进提升编译率却降低性能，正确内核中46.6%比PyTorch基线更慢，量化任务完全失败（0/30）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成Triton内核的能力边界不清楚，缺乏系统性的细粒度评估来回答"这种方法在什么情况下失效、为什么失效"这一基础经验性问题。

Method: 构建KernelBench-X基准，包含15个类别176个任务；对5种代表性方法进行类别感知的正确性与硬件效率评估；使用偏差分析（deviance explained）量化任务结构与方法设计的贡献度。

Result: (1) 任务结构解释的正确性方差（9.4%）远大于方法设计（3.3%），72%的Fusion任务全部失败而Math任务稳定成功；(2) 迭代精进使编译率从52.3%升至68.8%，但平均加速比从1.58×降至1.44×；(3) 46.6%的正确内核慢于PyTorch基线，跨硬件加速比方差达21.4倍；(4) 量化任务0/30成功但编译率非零，暴露数值计算契约的系统性误解。

Conclusion: 未来进展取决于处理全局协调、显式建模数值精度以及将硬件效率纳入生成过程。

Abstract: LLM-based Triton kernel generation has attracted significant interest, yet a fundamental empirical question remains unanswered: where does this capability break down, and why? We present KernelBench-X, a benchmark designed to answer this question through category-aware evaluation of correctness and hardware efficiency across 176 tasks in 15 categories. Our systematic comparison of five representative methods yields three main findings. First, task structure determines correctness more than method design. Category explains nearly three times more variance in semantic correctness than method (9.4% vs 3.3% explained deviance), and 72% of Fusion tasks fail across all five methods while Math tasks are solved consistently. Second, iterative refinement improves correctness, but not performance. Across GEAK iterations, compile rate rises from 52.3% to 68.8% while average speedup declines from $1.58\times$ to $1.44\times$; newly rescued kernels consistently underperform persistently correct ones ($1.16\times$ vs $1.58\times$ speedup in round~0$\to$1). Third, correctness does not imply efficiency. 46.6% of correct kernels are slower than the PyTorch eager baseline, and cross-hardware speedup variance reaches $21.4\times$. Besides, quantization remains completely unsolved (0/30 successes) despite non-trivial compilation rates, revealing systematic misunderstanding of numerical computation contracts rather than surface-level syntax errors. These findings suggest that future progress depends on handling global coordination, explicitly modeling numerical precision, and incorporating hardware efficiency into generation. The code is available at https://github.com/BonnieW05/KernelBenchX

</details>


### [234] [Delving into Non-Exchangeability for Conformal Prediction in Graph-Structured Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04957)
*Ruichao Guo,Xingyao Han,Luo Wenshui,Zhe Liu,Chen Gong,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出频谱图条件可交换性（SGCE）概念及基于图小波变换的频谱保形预测方法（SCALE），通过对高频残差进行保形校准并保留低频全局趋势，在交通时间序列预测中实现了有效的覆盖率和效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图结构多元时间序列的预测不确定性量化尚不充分，保形预测（CP）虽能提供覆盖保证但依赖可交换性假设，而图时间序列中跨节点耦合破坏了该假设，导致CP直接应用不可靠。

Method: 提出频谱图条件可交换性（SGCE），利用谱图理论将耦合信息归入低频分量，高频分量近似可交换；基于图小波变换分解低/高频分量，对高频残差通过自适应门控（基于低频嵌入）进行保形校准，实现SCALE方法。

Result: 在真实交通数据集上，SCALE不仅实现了有效的覆盖保证，而且在覆盖-效率权衡上持续优于现有最先进的保形预测方法。

Conclusion: SGCE和SCALE通过频谱域的条件可交换性设计，成功将保形预测应用于非可交换的图时间序列，为图结构预测的不确定性量化提供了一条有效路径。

Abstract: Point forecasting for graph-structured multivariate time series is a fundamental problem, but rigorous uncertainty quantification for such predictions is still underexplored. Conformal prediction (CP) offers uncertainty estimation with a solid coverage guarantee under the exchangeability assumption, which requires the joint data distribution to be unchanged under permutation. However, in graph-structured time series, inherent cross-node coupling can violate the exchangeability condition, making direct application of CP unreliable. Inspired by the spectral graph theory, such coupling resides in global trends and can be characterized by the low-frequency components, while high-frequency components are nearly exchangeable. Therefore, we propose a novel concept named Spectral Graph Conditional Exchangeability (SGCE), which conditions exchangeable high-frequency components on low-frequency ones to preserve global trends and enable effective CP in the spectral domain. Based on SGCE, we further propose Spectral Conformal prediction via wAveLEt transform (SCALE). SCALE uses graph wavelets to decompose low/high-frequency components and conformalizes high-frequency residuals via adaptive gating over a low-frequency embedding. Experimental results on real-world traffic datasets show that SCALE not only achieves valid coverage but also consistently improves the coverage-efficiency trade-off over the state-of-the-art CP methods.

</details>


### [235] [EP-GRPO: Entropy-Progress Aligned Group Relative Policy Optimization with Implicit Process Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04960)
*Song Yu,Li Li,Wenwen Zhao,Zhisheng Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对GRPO在token级信用分配上的三个缺陷（均匀粒度、均匀极性、零方差崩溃），提出EP-GRPO框架，通过熵进度对齐实现密集自监督引导，在数学推理基准上取得更优的准确率和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO存在三种信用分配失败：1) 对所有token使用统一的粒度，忽略其异构信息价值；2) 统一的极性机制，错误惩罚正确步骤并奖励错误步骤；3) 零方差崩溃导致基于结果的梯度消失。系统量化证实token信息量高度不均匀、步骤级极性错配普遍存在且训练浪费严重。

Method: 提出EP-GRPO框架，利用模型内在信息流提供密集自监督信号。包含三个核心组件：1) 熵门控调制：优先关注高熵决策节点；2) 隐式过程信号：基于策略散度锚定结果优势，提供定向token级反馈，无需外部奖励模型；3) 累积熵映射：实现进度对齐的优势归一化，在零奖励方差下自然维持梯度流。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的大量实验表明，EP-GRPO在准确率和效率上均优于GRPO及其变体。

Conclusion: EP-GRPO通过熵进度对齐机制有效解决了GRPO的信用分配问题，为强化学习推理提供了更精细、高效的训练范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), particularly Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), has advanced LLM reasoning. However, GRPO suffers from three credit assignment failures: uniform token-level granularity that ignores heterogeneous informational value, uniform polarity that penalizes correct steps and rewards incorrect ones, and zero-variance collapse that erases outcome-driven gradients. We systematically quantify these failures, revealing highly non-uniform token informativeness, widespread step-level polarity misalignment, and substantial training waste.
  To address these limitations, we propose Entropy-Progress Aligned GRPO (EP-GRPO), a framework that mines the model's intrinsic information flow for dense, self-supervised guidance. EP-GRPO integrates entropy-gated modulation to prioritize high entropy decision pivots, implicit process signals from policy divergence anchored to outcome advantages for directional token-level feedback without external reward models, and cumulative entropy mapping that enables progress-aligned advantage normalization, naturally maintaining gradient flow under zero reward variance.
  Extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that EP-GRPO achieves superior accuracy and efficiency compared to GRPO and its variants. The code will be available.

</details>


### [236] [Reliable Modeling of Distribution Shifts via Displacement-Reshaped Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04965)
*Philip Naumann,Jacob Kauffmann,Klaus-Robert Müller,Grégoire Montavon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出位移重塑最优传输（ReshapeOT），通过观察样本位移的二阶矩估计马氏距离来重塑地面度量，使传输路径更符合真实位移模式，计算轻量且可与任何OT求解器集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优传输直接比较输入空间中的分布时，地面度量的设计至关重要，否则优化结果可能违背真实的位移几何结构。本文旨在利用观测到的样本位移作为额外知识来改进地面度量。

Method: 用位移二阶矩估计的马氏距离替代欧氏距离作为地面度量，在输入空间中构建"高速公路"引导传输方向；计算轻量，可无缝集成到任何基于成本矩阵的OT求解器中，并支持核化扩展。

Result: 合成与真实数据实验表明，ReshapeOT在传输可靠性上取得显著提升，并在两个实际应用场景中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过整合观测位移信息重塑地面度量是一种高效且实用的方法，有望推动OT在分布偏移建模中的实际应用。

Abstract: Optimal transport (OT) is a central framework for modeling distribution shifts. Because OT compares distributions directly in input space, a well-designed ground metric between observations is essential to ensure that the optimizer does not violate the true geometry of change. We propose Displacement-Reshaped Optimal Transport (ReshapeOT), a method that reshapes the ground metric by integrating observed sample displacements as an additional source of knowledge. Technically, ReshapeOT replaces the Euclidean metric with a Mahalanobis distance estimated from displacement second moments. This effectively carves expressways through the input space, inviting transport solutions that better align with observed displacements. Our method is computationally lightweight, integrates seamlessly into any OT solver that operates on a cost matrix, and can be kernelized for further flexibility. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data show that ReshapeOT achieves substantial gains in transport reliability. We further demonstrate our method's usefulness in two practical use cases.

</details>


### [237] [Skill Neologisms: Towards Skill-based Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04970)
*Antonin Berthon,Nicolas Astorga,Mihaela van der Schaar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"技能新词"（skill neologisms）——在模型词汇表中集成并优化的软token，无需权重更新即可选择性扩展模型能力。实验表明，预训练LLM已存在与程序性知识相关的token，且技能新词可学习提升特定技能能力，支持与分布外技能组合，独立训练的新词可零样本组合，为基于技能的持续学习提供了可扩展路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在扩展模型新技能时面临两难：微调及参数高效变体存在灾难性遗忘风险，而基于上下文的方法表达能力有限且受限于有效上下文长度。需要一种可扩展的方式选择性扩展模型能力而不进行权重更新。

Method: 提出技能新词（skill neologisms），即在模型词汇表中集成软token，针对特定技能进行优化。首先观察预训练LLM中已存在与程序性知识相关的token，然后学习技能新词提升特定技能能力，并测试其与分布外技能的组合能力以及独立训练新词的零样本组合能力。

Result: 1）预训练LLM中已存在与程序性知识相关的token；2）技能新词可学习并提升特定技能能力；3）技能新词可与分布外技能组合；4）独立训练的技能新词可零样本组合。

Conclusion: 技能新词为基于技能的持续学习提供了一条可扩展路径，通过软token方式实现了无需权重更新的技能扩展与组合。

Abstract: Modern LLMs show mastery over an ever-growing range of skills, as well as the ability to compose them flexibly. However, extending model capabilities to new skills in a scalable manner is an open-problem: fine-tuning and parameter-efficient variants risk catastrophic forgetting, while context-based approaches have limited expressiveness and are constrained by the model's effective context. We explore skill neologisms--i.e., soft tokens integrated in the model's vocabulary and optimized to improve capabilities over a specific skill--as a way to selectively extend model capabilities to new skills without weight updates. We first observe that off-the-shelf pre-trained LLMs already demonstrate tokens associated with procedural knowledge. We then show that skill neologisms can be learned to improve model capabilities on specific skills while being composable with out-of-distribution skills, and that independently trained skill neologisms can be composed zero-shot. These results suggest that skill neologisms may provide a scalable path towards skill-based continual learning.

</details>


### [238] [Learned Neighbor Trust for Collaborative Deployment in Model-Agnostic Decentralized Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05009)
*Michael Lanier,Luise Ge,Sastry Kompella,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LNTrust方法，让分布式节点在训练时学习信任函数，从而在推理时通过邻居集成提升部署性能，在异构IoT场景下显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式蒸馏方法仅在训练时协调，推理时各节点独立运行，忽略了异构IoT场景中更强邻居在推理时仍可用的特性，未能充分利用节点间的协作潜力。

Method: 提出LNTrust：每个节点基于本地验证证据学习一个轻量级信任函数，该函数在训练时门控辅助蒸馏，在推理时定义部署集成，实现训练-推理一致的协作机制。

Result: 在多个数据集和拓扑结构上，LNTrust相比最强输出仅基线在部署准确率上有大幅提升，且通信开销显著低于先前方法。

Conclusion: 通过将训练时学到的信任关系直接迁移到推理阶段，LNTrust有效解决了异构设备场景下分布式蒸馏的部署-推理不匹配问题。

Abstract: Many decentralized distillation methods are designed around training-time coordination, yet deploy each node in isolation even when more capable neighbors remain available at inference time. This is an incomplete objective for settings such as IoT, where devices are heterogeneous, data is scarce and skewed, and a node's strongest neighbors may far exceed its own local capacity. We study how nodes should train so that their predictions compose well at deployment, and how each node should learn whom to trust. Under a server-free, model-agnostic protocol where nodes exchange only queries and soft predictions, we propose Learned Neighbor Trust (LNTrust) wherein each node learns a compact trust function over its neighborhood from local validation evidence. This trust function gates auxiliary distillation during training and defines a deployment ensemble at inference, so that collaboration learned during training transfers directly to deployment. Across datasets and topologies, LNTrust improves deployed accuracy over the strongest output-only baseline by large margins while using significantly less communication than previous methods.

</details>


### [239] [Graph-SND: Sparse Aggregation for Behavioral Diversity in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05020)
*Shawn Ray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Graph-SND，通过将SND的完全图平均替换为任意图G上的加权平均，将计算复杂度从O(n²)降至O(|E|)，实现了语义不变、可扩展的多样性度量。实验验证了无偏性、浓度界、加速比（~10倍）及闭环多样性控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SND衡量多智能体强化学习中的行为异质性，但其计算复杂度为O(n²)，随团队规模二次增长，成为规模化应用的瓶颈。需要一种语义不变但计算高效的替代方案。

Method: 将SND的完全图平均替换为任意图G上的加权平均，支持三种模式：完全图K_n恢复SND、固定稀疏图O(|E|)局部多样性、随机边采样实现Horvitz-Thompson无偏估计。理论上证明了扩张图的转发指数畸变界、低秩距离结构的谱改进以及随机d-正则图的无条件概率界。

Result: VMAS上验证了恢复性、无偏性、浓度性及壁钟缩放；n=100的PPO实验中Bernoulli-0.1 Graph-SND跟踪全SND且加速~10倍；随机d-正则扩张图在Θ(n log n)边时SND_G^u/SND∈[0.9987,1.0013]；n=50的DiCo控制中，每调用成本降低~9.5倍且设定点跟踪无显著差异。

Conclusion: SND的聚合瓶颈可被移除，且不改变度量语义。Graph-SND作为即插即用的稀疏替代方案，可扩展到完全图SND无法企及的规模，同时支持被动测量和闭环多样性控制。

Abstract: System Neural Diversity (SND) measures behavioral heterogeneity in multi-agent reinforcement learning by averaging pairwise distances over all $\binom{n}{2}$ agent pairs, making each call quadratic in team size. We introduce Graph-SND, which replaces this complete-graph average with a weighted average over the edges of an arbitrary graph $G$. Three regimes follow: $G=K_n$ recovers SND exactly; a fixed sparse $G$ defines a localized diversity measure at $O(|E|)$ cost; and random edge samples yield an unbiased Horvitz-Thompson estimator and a normalized sample mean with $O(1/\sqrt{m})$ concentration in the sampled edge count $m$. For fixed sparse graphs we prove forwarding-index distortion bounds for expanders and a spectral refinement under low-rank distance structure; for random $d$-regular graphs we prove an unconditional probabilistic $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(D_{\max}/\sqrt{n})$ bound. On VMAS we verify recovery, unbiasedness, concentration, and wall-clock scaling, with a PettingZoo TVD panel checking non-Gaussian transfer. In a 500-iteration $n=100$ PPO run, Bernoulli-$0.1$ Graph-SND tracks full SND while reducing per-call metric time by about $10\times$, and frozen-policy GPU timing up to $n=500$ follows the predicted $\binom{n}{2}/|E|$ speedup. Random $d$-regular expanders empirically achieve $\mathrm{SND}_{G}^{\mathrm{u}}/\mathrm{SND} \in [0.9987, 1.0013]$ at $Θ(n \log n)$ edges. In DiCo diversity control at $n=50$, Bernoulli-$0.1$ Graph-SND preserves set-point tracking with paired reward differences indistinguishable from zero across nine matched cells while cutting per-call metric cost by ${\sim}9.5\times$. Together, these results show that the SND aggregation bottleneck can be removed without changing the metric's semantics, yielding a drop-in sparse alternative that scales beyond complete-graph SND and supports both passive measurement and closed-loop diversity control.

</details>


### [240] [CuBridge: An LLM-Based Framework for Understanding and Reconstructing High-Performance Attention Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05023)
*Xing Ma,Yangjie Zhou,Wu Sun,Zihan Liu,Jingwen Leng,Yun Lin,Shixuan Sun,Minyi Guo,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CuBridge是一个基于LLM的框架，通过“提升-转换-降低”工作流，将专家手写的CUDA注意力核函数适配到新的注意力变体和GPU平台，生成正确且高性能的代码，显著优于现有框架、编译器和基于LLM的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力机制的CUDA实现在性能与灵活性之间存在权衡：专家手写核函数效率高但难以适配，框架和编译器灵活但性能差。近期尝试用LLM生成GPU核函数，但对注意力这类复杂算子仍存在正确性和性能差距。

Method: CuBridge采用三步工作流：(1) Lift：从专家手写CUDA注意力核函数出发，将其提升为可执行的中间表示(IR)，显式表达执行编排并抽象底层CUDA语法；(2) Transfer：根据用户提供的PyTorch规范，生成并验证目标IR程序；(3) Lower：通过参考引导的降低过程，重构优化的CUDA代码。

Result: 在多种注意力变体和GPU平台上，CuBridge一致地生成正确的核函数，并在性能上大幅优于通用框架、编译器方法和先前的LLM方法。

Conclusion: CuBridge证明了基于LLM的结构化工作流可以有效桥接专家手写核函数的高效性与自动化适配的灵活性，为复杂算子的跨平台代码生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Efficient CUDA implementations of attention mechanisms are critical to modern deep learning systems, yet supporting diverse and evolving attention variants remains challenging. Existing frameworks and compilers trade performance for flexibility, while expert-written kernels achieve high efficiency but are difficult to adapt. Recent work explores large language models (LLMs) for GPU kernel generation, but prior studies report unstable correctness and significant performance gaps for complex operators such as attention.
  We present CuBridge, an LLM-based framework that adapts expert-written attention kernels through a structured lift-transfer-lower workflow. CuBridge starts from expert-written CUDA attention kernels and lifts them into an executable intermediate representation that makes execution orchestration explicit while abstracting low-level CUDA syntax. Given a user-provided PyTorch specification, CuBridge generates and verifies a target IR program, then reconstructs optimized CUDA code via reference-guided lowering. Across diverse attention variants and GPU platforms, CuBridge consistently produces correct kernels and substantially outperforms general frameworks, compiler-based approaches, and prior LLM-based methods.

</details>


### [241] [Preference-Based Self-Distillation: Beyond KL Matching via Reward Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05040)
*Xin Yu,Liuchen Liao,Yiwen Zhang,Yingchen Yu,Lingzhou Xue,Qinzhen Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出偏好型自我蒸馏（PBSD），通过奖励正则化视角重新审视在线自我蒸馏，将目标策略优化为奖励加权后的教师分布，在数学推理和工具使用任务上优于现有自蒸馏基线方法，具备更好的训练稳定性和token效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线自我蒸馏方法依赖于KL散度匹配上下文增强的教师模型，存在训练不稳定、推理性能随时间下降以及缺乏外部教师提供的探索多样性等问题。

Method: 提出PBSD框架，推导奖励正则化目标函数，其解析最优解为奖励加权的教师分布；通过优化教师和学生样本之间的偏好差距，同时保持在线学生采样，实现比原始教师更优的目标策略。

Result: 在数学推理和工具使用基准测试上，PBSD在不同模型规模下一致取得最佳平均性能，比现有自蒸馏基线训练更稳定，同时保持token效率。

Conclusion: PBSD通过偏好学习和奖励正则化替代固定教师KL匹配，解决了自蒸馏的不稳定性和探索不足问题，为在线自我蒸馏提供了更有效的范式。

Abstract: On-policy distillation is an efficient alternative to reinforcement learning, offering dense token-level training signals. However, its reliance on a stronger external teacher has driven recent work on on-policy self-distillation, where the same model serves as both teacher and student under different prompt contexts. Yet, existing self-distillation methods largely reduce learning to KL matching toward the context-augmented teacher model. This approach often suffers from training instability and can degrade reasoning performance over time. Moreover, self-distillation from the same model with prompt augmentation lacks the exploratory diversity provided by a genuine external teacher. To address these limitations, we move beyond fixed-teacher KL matching and propose \textbf{P}reference-\textbf{B}ased \textbf{S}elf-\textbf{D}istillation (\textbf{PBSD}), which revisits on-policy self-distillation through a reward-regularized perspective. Instead of directly matching the teacher distribution, we derive a reward-regularized objective whose analytic optimum is a reward-reweighted teacher distribution, yielding a target policy provably superior to the original teacher under this objective. Practically, PBSD optimizes preference gaps between teacher and student samples while maintaining on-policy student sampling. We support this framework with a statistical analysis of the induced preference-learning problem, formally establishing when on policy self-distillation is preferable to learning from an external teacher in our setting. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and tool-use benchmarks across multiple model scales demonstrate that PBSD consistently achieves the strongest average performance among comparable baselines, showing improved training stability over prior self-distillation baselines while preserving token efficiency.

</details>


### [242] [Provable imitation learning for control of instability in partially-observed Vlasov--Poisson equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05081)
*Xiaofan Xia,Qin Li,Wenlong Mou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于宏观观测的模仿学习方法，用于稳定Vlasov-Poisson等离子体动力学系统，证明了学习策略的稳定性保证，并展示了该方法的自适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核聚变中Vlasov-Poisson等离子体动力学的稳定化是一个关键控制问题，但理想控制器需要全相空间状态信息，而实际实验只能获得稀疏的宏观诊断数据。这之间存在巨大差距，需要弥合。

Method: 采用模仿学习方法，将基于全状态观测的专家策略蒸馏为仅依赖宏观测量值的控制器；通过行为克隆损失的最优性刻画稳定性边界，并引入熵的概念量化初始分布复杂度。

Result: 学习策略的误差下界由观测约束下的最小行为克隆损失决定；该最小损失可用初始分布的熵来表征；数值实验表明学习策略能在远超非自适应基线的时域内稳定系统。

Conclusion: 从理论上证明了利用宏观观测学习稳定反馈策略的可行性，并展示了该方法对低复杂度结构的自适应能力。

Abstract: We consider the stabilization of Vlasov--Poisson plasma dynamics, a central control problem in nuclear fusion. Our focus is the gap between what an ideal controller would use and what experiments can actually observe: while optimal policy may rely on the full phase-space state, practical feedback is typically limited to sparse macroscopic diagnostics. We therefore study imitation learning methods that distill a fully observed expert policy into controllers operating only on macroscopic measurements. We show the stability guarantees of the learned policy, where the error floor depends on the minimal behavior cloning loss achievable under the observation constraints. We further characterize this minimal loss in terms of a notion of entropy that quantifies the complexity of the initial distribution. Our results demonstrates the theoretical feasibility of learning stabilizing feedback policies for kinetic plasma dynamics from macroscopic observations, and exhibits the adaptivity of the learning approach to low-complexity structures. Through extensive numerical experiments, we validate our theory and show that the learned policies can stabilize the system using only macroscopic observations, within a significantly longer time horizon than non-adaptive baseline controllers.

</details>


### [243] [Order Matters: Improving Domain Adaptation by Reordering Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05084)
*Andrea Napoli,Paul White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ORDERED提出通过优化训练数据采样顺序来减少域差异估计的方差，在无监督域适应中实现更稳定的随机梯度估计，并在两个图像分类基准上提升了目标域准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督域适应中域差异估计在随机设置下存在高方差问题，这制约了理论优势的发挥，亟需一种方差缩减技术来提高估计稳定性。

Method: 提出ORDERED框架，将数据采样顺序作为优化变量，针对相关对齐（CORAL）和最大均值差异（MMD）两种域差异损失，推导了随机估计误差与采样顺序的函数关系，并设计了实用的优化算法来求解最优采样顺序。

Result: 在模拟实验中相比相关方法显示了更低的方差；在两个域偏移图像分类基准上取得了更高的目标域准确率。

Conclusion: 通过优化数据采样顺序可以系统性地减少域差异估计的随机误差，是一种新颖且有效的无偏随机方差缩减技术，为UDA中的高方差问题提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Domain shift remains a key challenge in deploying machine learning models to the real world. Unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) aims to address this by minimising domain discrepancy during training, but the discrepancy estimates suffer from high variance in stochastic settings, which can stifle the theoretical benefits of the method. This paper proposes Optimal Reordering of Data for Error-Reduced Estimation of Discrepancy (ORDERED), a novel unbiased stochastic variance reduction technique which reduces the discrepancy estimation error by optimising the order in which the training data are sampled. We consider two specific domain discrepancy losses (correlation alignment and the maximum mean discrepancy), formulate their stochastic estimation error as a function of the data sampling order, and propose a practical optimisation algorithm. Our simulations demonstrate reduced variance compared to related methods, and experiments on two domain shift image classification benchmarks show improved target domain accuracy.

</details>


### [244] [Gated Multimodal Learning for Interpretable Property Energy Performance Prediction and Retrofit Scenario Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05088)
*Yunfei Bai,Aaron Tesfa Tsion,Raul Rosales,Barbara Shollock,Wei He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种门控多模态模型，融合EPC表格变量、评估师文本和GIS空间特征，用于预测建筑能效与环境影响评分。在威斯敏斯特案例中，模型MAE为4.39，R²约0.75。消融实验证实多模态优于单/双模态。可解释性分析揭示了各模态的关键特征，并进一步应用于墙体、屋顶、窗户改造场景的排放与成本评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EPC依赖现场检查，难以实现城市尺度的快速评估。为实现可持续城市的弹性发展，需要可扩展的住宅建筑脱碳评估方法。

Method: 构建门控多模态模型，融合三类数据：EPC表格变量、评估师自由文本、GIS地理空间特征（足迹几何、高度、面积、朝向）。采用样本级门控学习属性特定的模态权重，辅以频带分类头稳定训练。

Result: SAP评分MAE=4.03，EI评分MAE=4.76，R²分别为0.757和0.748，均MAE=4.39。多模态融合优于单/双模态基线。门控权重显示依赖评估师文本，SHAP突出燃料类型、建筑形态、建造年代，文本遮蔽关注屋顶和墙体，空间属性以高度和足迹面积为主。

Conclusion: 该框架为建筑改造筛选、干预优先级排序和净零住房转型提供了可扩展的房产级证据支持。

Abstract: Achieving resilient and sustainable cities requires scalable approaches to decarbonising residential buildings, which account for about 20% of UK greenhouse gas emissions and 25% of energy-related emissions in the European Union. Energy Performance Certificates (EPCs) support regulation and retrofit planning, but their reliance on on-site inspections limits timely city-scale assessment. This study introduces a gated multimodal model to predict Standard Assessment Procedure (SAP) energy efficiency and Environmental Impact (EI) scores by integrating EPC tabular variables, assessor-written free text, and Geographic Information System (GIS)-derived spatial features describing footprint geometry, height, area, and orientation. Sample-wise gating learns property-specific modality weights, while an auxiliary band classification head stabilises training. In a Westminster, London case study, the model predicts SAP and EI scores with MAEs of 4.03 and 4.76 points and R2 values of 0.757 and 0.748, respectively, achieving a mean MAE of 4.39. Ablation results show that full multimodal fusion outperforms unimodal and bimodal baselines for both score prediction and band-level classification. Interpretability analyses provide decision-relevant evidence: gating weights indicate strong reliance on assessor text; SHAP highlights main fuel, built form, and construction age band; text occlusion prioritises roof and wall fields; and spatial attribution is dominated by height and footprint area, with sensitivity to footprint shape. The validated framework is further applied to retrofit scenarios for wall insulation, roof insulation, and window glazing upgrades, indicating projected improvements in SAP, EI, annual energy cost, and equivalent CO2 emissions. Overall, the framework provides scalable property-level evidence for retrofit screening, intervention prioritisation, and net-zero housing transitions.

</details>


### [245] [Unified Framework of Distributional Regret in Multi-Armed Bandits and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05102)
*Harin Lee,Min-hwan Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过统一框架研究了随机多臂老虎机和情节强化学习中的遗憾分布问题，形式化了分布遗憾界，并提出了一种UCBVI风格算法，首次证实了Lattimore & Szepesvári关于多臂老虎机分布遗憾界的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗憾分析主要关注期望遗憾，缺乏对遗憾完整分布特征的刻画。本文旨在通过分布遗憾界统一表征不同置信水平下的遗憾行为，并解决Lattimore & Szepesvári提出的关于多臂老虎机分布遗憾界的开放猜想。

Method: 提出UCBVI风格算法，采用探索奖励项$\min\{c_{1,k}/N, c_{2,k}/\sqrt{N}\}$（$N$为访问计数，$c_{1,k},c_{2,k}$为用户指定参数）。通过任意参数序列推导出通用间隙无关和间隙相关的分布遗憾界，系统刻画参数对期望性能、尾部风险及实例依赖行为的权衡控制。

Result: 在极小极大和实例依赖两种机制下均实现了期望遗憾与分布遗憾之间的最优权衡。对于多臂老虎机（$A$个臂，$T$步），得到阶为$\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{AT}\log(1/\delta))$的分布遗憾界，首次证实Lattimore & Szepesvári的猜想。

Conclusion: 分布遗憾分析框架能更全面地刻画算法性能，所提算法在期望与尾部风险间达到最优折衷，并解决了领域内一个重要的理论猜想。

Abstract: We study the distribution of regret in stochastic multi-armed bandits and episodic reinforcement learning through a unified framework. We formalize a distributional regret bound as a probabilistic guarantee that holds uniformly over all confidence levels $δ\in (0,1]$, thereby characterizing the regret distribution across the full range of $δ$. We present a simple UCBVI-style algorithm with exploration bonus $\min\{c_{1,k}/N, c_{2,k}/\sqrt{N}\}$, where $N$ denotes the visit count and $(c_{1,k},c_{2,k})$ are user-specified parameters. For arbitrary parameter sequences, we derive general gap-independent and gap-dependent distributional regret bounds, yielding a principled characterization of how the parameters control the trade-off between expected performance, tail risk, and instance-dependent behavior. In particular, our bounds achieve optimal trade-offs between expected and distributional regret in both minimax and instance-dependent regimes. As a special case, for multi-armed bandits with $A$ arms and horizon $T$, we obtain a distributional regret bound of order $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{AT}\log(1/δ))$, confirming the conjecture of Lattimore & Szepesvári (2020, Section 17.1) for the first time.

</details>


### [246] [Rollout Pass-Rate Control: Steering Binary-Reward RL Toward Its Most Informative Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05112)
*Tianshu Zhu,Wenyu Zhang,Xiaoying Zuo,Lun Tian,Haotian Zhao,Yucheng Zeng,Jingnan Gu,Daxiang Dong,Jianmin Wu,Dawei Yin,Dou Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Prefix Sampling (PS)方法，通过回放轨迹前缀将采样组的通过率控制在50%这一最有效信息量点，在SWE-bench风格智能体强化学习中实现2.01x(14B)和1.55x(32B)的加速，同时提升SWE-bench Verified峰值从0.273到0.295。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SWE-bench风格智能体强化学习依赖昂贵的有状态轨迹，但大量计算浪费在通过率偏斜的采样组上，二元奖励提供的对比信号弱。本文将此低效问题重构为通过率控制问题。

Method: 提出Prefix Sampling (PS)：回放轨迹前缀以引导偏斜组向50%通过率靠拢——成功前缀作为多数失败组的"先发优势"，失败前缀作为多数成功组的"障碍"。通过重放重建前缀状态，同时排除重放token的损失，将优化限制在当前策略生成的延续部分。

Result: SWE-bench风格RL中：Qwen3-14B端到端加速2.01x，Qwen3-32B加速1.55x；14B的SWE-bench Verified峰值从0.273提升至0.295。AIME 2025数学推理实验验证了相同的通过率控制模式。

Conclusion: 50%通过率是最具信息量的工作点，能最大化奖励熵、组过滤存活概率、RLOO优势能量及正反例对比结构。PS方法有效提升了样本效率与计算效率。

Abstract: SWE-bench-style agentic reinforcement learning relies on expensive stateful trajectories, yet substantial compute is wasted on sampled rollout groups with skewed pass rates, where binary rewards provide a weak contrastive signal. We frame this inefficiency as a pass-rate control problem and show that a 50% pass rate is the most informative operating point: it maximizes reward entropy, the probability of surviving group filtering, RLOO advantage energy under GRPO, and success--failure contrastive structure. Guided by this principle, we propose Prefix Sampling (PS), which replays trajectory prefixes to steer skewed groups toward this regime: successful prefixes serve as head starts for mostly failing groups, while failing prefixes serve as handicaps for mostly passing groups. In stateful agent environments, prefix states are reconstructed through replay while replayed tokens are excluded from the loss, restricting optimization to continuations generated by the current policy. On SWE-bench-style agentic RL, PS delivers end-to-end wall-clock speedups of 2.01x on Qwen3-14B and 1.55x on Qwen3-32B while preserving or improving final verified performance. For 14B, the SWE-bench Verified peak rises from the baseline peak of 0.273 to 0.295 under PS. Additional mathematical reasoning experiments on AIME 2025 show the same pass-rate control pattern and decompose the gains into replay, bidirectional coverage, and adaptive control.

</details>


### [247] [Manifold Steering Reveals the Shared Geometry of Neural Network Representation and Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05115)
*Daniel Wurgaft,Can Rager,Matthew Kowal,Vasudev Shyam,Sheridan Feucht,Usha Bhalla,Tal Haklay,Eric Bigelow,Raphael Sarfati,Thomas McGrath,Owen Lewis,Jack Merullo,Noah Goodman,Thomas Fel,Atticus Geiger,Ekdeep Singh Lubana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过干预实验证明，神经表征的几何结构并非偶然现象，而是因果性地塑造行为。研究者提出的“流形引导”方法（沿激活流形$M_h$进行干预）能产生遵循行为流形$M_y$的自然行为轨迹，而线性干预则产生不自然输出。实验涵盖语言模型（推理任务、上下文学习）和视频世界模型（物理动力学），证实了表征几何与行为几何之间的双向对应关系，并将控制问题的核心从“寻找正确方向”重新定义为“寻找正确几何”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究神经表征中的几何结构是否因果性地塑造模型行为，而非仅仅是伴随现象。

Method: 1) 拟合激活流形$M_h$和行为流形$M_y$；2) 沿$M_h$进行流形引导干预 vs 线性干预；3) 在激活空间中优化干预以产生沿$M_y$的路径，观察是否恢复$M_h$曲率。实验涉及语言模型（循环/序列推理任务、图结构上下文学习任务）和视频世界模型（物理动力学任务）。

Result: 流形引导干预产生遵循$M_y$的行为轨迹，线性干预产生不自然输出；优化激活干预以沿$M_y$行进可恢复$M_h$的曲率。

Conclusion: 神经表征的几何结构因果性地决定行为，控制问题应从“寻找正确方向”重新定义为“寻找正确几何”。

Abstract: Neural representations carry rich geometric structure; but does that structure causally shape behavior? To address this question, we intervene along paths through activation space defined by different geometries, and measure the behavioral trajectories they induce. In particular, we test whether interventions that respect the geometry of activation space will yield behaviors close to those the model exhibits naturally. Concretely, we first fit an activation manifold $M_h$ to representations and a behavior manifold $M_y$ to output probability distributions. We then test the link $M_h \leftrightarrow M_y$ via interventions: we find that steering along $M_h$, which we term manifold steering, yields behavioral trajectories that follow $M_y$, while linear steering -- which assumes a Euclidean geometry -- cuts through off-manifold regions and hence produces unnatural outputs. Moreover, optimizing interventions in activation space to produce paths along $M_y$ recovers activation trajectories that trace the curvature of $M_h$. We demonstrate this bidirectional relationship between the geometry of representation and behavior across tasks and modalities. In language models, we use reasoning tasks with cyclic and sequential geometries as well as in-context learning tasks with more complex graph geometries. In a video world model, we use a task with geometry corresponding to physical dynamics. Overall, our work shows that geometry in neural representation is not merely incidental, but is in fact the proper object for enabling principled control via intervention on internals. This recasts the core problem of steering from finding the right direction to finding the right geometry.

</details>


### [248] [On the Hardness of Junking LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05116)
*Marco Rando,Samuel Vaiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM中的"自然后门"问题，即无需对抗性指令、仅通过优化token序列即可触发有害输出的现象。作者形式化定义了"垃圾问题"（junking problem），提出贪心随机搜索方法，发现该问题比标准越狱攻击更困难，但简单策略仍能以高成功率解决，表明自然后门存在且易恢复。困惑度分析显示这些序列位于模型分布的低概率区域，支持其来自训练过程隐式涌现的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱攻击依赖精心设计的提示语义结构，但最近观察到即使没有对抗性提示，仅靠优化的token序列也能引发有害行为，暗示自然后门的存。然而这一现象尚未被系统研究，尤其是寻找自然后门的难度尚未被评估。

Method: 形式化定义junking problem：寻找最大化有害响应目标前缀生成概率的token序列；提出贪心随机搜索方法评估这些序列是否容易发现。

Result: junking problem比标准越狱攻击更难，但简单贪心随机搜索策略能以高成功率解决；发现的token序列位于模型分布的低概率区域。

Conclusion: 自然后门确实存在且易于恢复，提示语义信息在攻击设计中至关重要，同时为LLM安全对齐提供了新的研究视角。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are known to be vulnerable to jailbreak attacks, which typically rely on carefully designed prompts containing explicit semantic structure. These attacks generally operate by fixing an adversarial instruction and optimizing small adversarial components (e.g., suffixes or prefixes). In this setting, prompt structure is fundamental for performance, and recent results show that even simple random search can achieve strong performance when combined with sophisticated prompt design. Recently, it has been observed that harmful behaviors can be elicited even without the adversarial prompt, relying solely on optimized token sequences. This suggests the existence of natural backdoors, i.e., token sequences naturally emerged during LLMs training that trigger unsafe outputs without any meaningful instruction. However, despite these observations, this setting remains largely unexplored, and in particular the hardness of finding natural backdoors has not been assessed yet. In this work, we provide a first proof-of-concept study investigating the hardness of this task, which we refer to as the junking problem. We formalize it as the problem of finding token sequences that maximize the probability of generating a target prefix of harmful responses, propose a greedy random-search method to assess is such sequences can be discovered easily. Our results show that this problem is harder than standard jailbreak attacks, confirming the importance of semantic information in prompt design. At the same time, we find that our simple strategy is sufficient to solve it with a high success rate, suggesting that natural backdoors are present and easily recoverable. Finally, through perplexity analysis, we observe that the discovered token sequences lie in low-probability regions of the model distribution, supporting the hypothesis that they emerged implicitly from the training process.

</details>


### [249] [On the Wasserstein Gradient Flow Interpretation of Drifting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05118)
*Arthur Gretton,Li Kevin Wenliang,Alexandre Galashov,James Thornton,Valentin De Bortoli,Arnaud Doucet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Wasserstein梯度流（WGF）视角分析Deng等人(2026)提出的生成式漂移模型（GMD），指出其算法实际对应不同WGF过程，并可将该思想扩展到MMD、切片Wasserstein距离和GAN判别函数等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从WGF的理论视角重新审视GMD框架，揭示其算法与理论表述之间的差异，并探索将该思想推广到其他WGF的可能性。

Method: 将GMD置于Wasserstein梯度流的几何框架下进行分析，对比Deng等人提出的两种算法（理论算法与实际实现算法）分别对应何种WGF过程。

Result: （1）理论算法对应KL散度的WGF固定点，附加Parzen平滑；（2）实际实现算法类似Sinkhorn散度的WGF固定点，但缺失某些理想性质；（3）该思路可推广至MMD、切片Wasserstein距离和GAN判别函数对应的WGF。

Conclusion: GMD可通过WGF框架统一理解，但其实际实现与理论表述存在偏差，同时WGF视角为生成模型提供了更广泛的理论基础。

Abstract: Recently, Deng et al. (2026) proposed Generative Modeling via Drifting (GMD), a novel framework for generative tasks. This note presents an analysis of GMD through the lens of Wasserstein Gradient Flows (WGF), i.e., the path of steepest descent for a functional in the space of probability measures, equipped with the geometry of optimal transport. Unlike previous WGF-based contributions, GMD can be thought of as directly targeting a fixed point of a specific WGF flow. We demonstrate three main results: first, that one algorithm proposed by Deng et al. (2026) corresponds to finding the limiting point of a WGF on the KL divergence, with Parzen smoothing on the densities. Second, that the algorithm actually implemented by Deng et al. (2026) corresponds to a different procedure, which bears some resemblance to the fixed point of a WGF on the Sinkhorn divergence, but lacks certain desirable properties of the latter. Third, the same same idea can be extended to the limiting point of other WGFs, including the Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), the sliced Wasserstein distance, and GAN critic functions.

</details>


### [250] [Physiologically Grounded Driver Behavior Classification: SHAP-Driven Elite Feature Selection and Hybrid Gradient Boosting for Multimodal Physiological Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05120)
*Sahar Askari,Mohammad Mahdi Mirza Ali Mohammadi,Fatemeh Ensafdoust,Amin Golnari,Saeid Sanei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可解释且可扩展的框架，利用多模态生理信号（EEG、EMG、GSR）通过SHAP特征选择、贝叶斯优化的XGBoost/LightGBM模型及加权软投票集成来解码驾驶行为，在大型多模态数据集上达到80.91%的准确率和0.79的宏F1分数，消融实验验证了多模态融合的必要性，SHAP分析揭示了各模态的生理学贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶行为解码方法主要依赖单一模态生理信号，缺乏可解释性和可扩展性，难以充分利用多模态生理信号的互补信息来提升解码性能。

Method: 提出一个多模态融合框架：1）对EEG、EMG、GSR信号进行预处理；2）提取时域、频域及衍生生理指标特征；3）采用SHAP进行精英特征选择，保留250个最优特征；4）使用Optuna贝叶斯优化对XGBoost和LightGBM进行超参数调优；5）构建加权软投票集成模型融合两个梯度提升框架。

Result: 集成模型在测试集上达到80.91%准确率和0.79宏F1分数，显著优于单模态基线和传统机器学习模型；消融实验表明多模态融合相比最佳单模态（EEG）带来8%的性能提升。

Conclusion: 多模态生理信号融合对驾驶行为解码有效且必要；EEG贡献主要预测权重，GSR和EMG在高唤醒度和高强度机动场景中提供关键的判别信号；SHAP分析验证了模型的生理学可解释性。

Abstract: An interpretable and scalable framework for decoding driving behaviors from multimodal physiological signals is proposed in this study. We utilize multimodal physiological driving behavior large-scale dataset comprising synchronized electroencephalogram (EEG), electromyography (EMG), and galvanic skin response (GSR) signals. Our approach involves rigorous preprocessing followed by a domain-specific feature extraction pipeline targeting time-domain, frequency-domain, and derived physiological indices. To address high dimensionality, we employ SHAP-based elite feature selection, retaining the top 250 features to reduce computational overhead while preserving predictive power. Hyperparameter optimization for extreme gradient boosting (XGBoost) and light gradient boosting machine (LightGBM) models is conducted using Bayesian optimization via Optuna. Finally, a weighted soft-voting ensemble is constructed to leverage the complementary strengths of both gradient boosting frameworks. The results demonstrate that the proposed ensemble achieves a test accuracy of 80.91% and a macro-F1 score of 0.79, significantly outperforming single-modality baselines and traditional machine learning models. Ablation studies confirm an 8% performance gain over the best single modality (EEG), validating the necessity of multimodal fusion. SHAP analysis further validates the physiological plausibility of the model, revealing that the EEG contributes the majority of predictive weight, GSR and EMG features provide critical discriminatory signals for high-arousal and motor-intensive maneuvers.

</details>


### [251] [Adaptive Policy Selection and Fine-Tuning under Interaction Budgets for Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05123)
*Alper Kamil Bozkurt,Xiaoan Xu,Shangtong Zhang,Miroslav Pajic,Yuichi Motai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应策略选择与微调方法，通过上置信界（UCB）在在线交互预算约束下高效选择并微调离线预训练策略，解决了离线到在线强化学习中离线策略评估不可靠和策略微调潜力未知的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有O2O-RL流程中，基于OPE的策略选择不可靠，而在线评估又消耗本可用于微调的大量交互；同时，无法先验判断预训练策略在部署后能否通过微调提升，导致单一策略部署方案在非平稳环境中不实用。

Method: 首先使用不同离线RL算法和超参数训练候选策略集，通过OPE获得初始性能估计；然后基于上置信界（UCB）方法自适应地选择并微调策略，在在线交互预算约束下高效分配交互资源。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，所提方法优于现有的O2O-RL基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于UCB的自适应策略选择与微调方法能有效利用在线交互预算，在O2O-RL场景中实现更优的性能。

Abstract: In offline-to-online reinforcement learning (O2O-RL), policies are first safely trained offline using previously collected datasets and then further fine-tuned for tasks via limited online interactions. In a typical O2O-RL pipeline, candidate policies trained with offline RL are evaluated via either off-policy evaluation (OPE) or online evaluation (OE). The policy with the highest estimated value is then deployed and continually fine-tuned. However, this setup has two main issues. First, OPE can be unreliable, making it risky to deploy a policy based solely on those estimates, whereas OE may identify a viable policy with substantial online interaction, which could have been used for fine-tuning. Second--and more importantly--it is also often not possible to determine a priori whether a pretrained policy will improve with post-deployment fine-tuning, especially in non-stationary environments. As a result, procedures committing to a single deployed policy are impractical in many real-world settings. Moreover, a naive remedy that exhaustively fine-tunes all candidates would violate interaction budget constraints and is likewise infeasible. In this paper, we propose a novel adaptive approach for policy selection and fine-tuning under online interaction budgets in O2O-RL. Following the standard pipeline, we first train a set of candidate policies with different offline RL algorithms and hyperparameters; we then perform OPE to obtain initial performance estimates. We next adaptively select and fine-tune the policies based on their predicted performance via an upper-confidence-bound approach thereby making efficient use of online interactions. We demonstrate that our approach improves upon O2O-RL baselines with various benchmarks.

</details>


### [252] [Conditional outlier detection for clinical alerting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05124)
*Milos Hauskrecht,Michal Valko,Shyam Visweswaran,Iyad Batal,Gilles Clermont,Gregory Cooper*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一种基于电子健康记录数据驱动的异常检测方法，用于识别患者管理过程中可能存在的异常（潜在错误）行为。基于4486名心脏手术后患者数据的专家评审结果表明，该方法具有较低的误报率，且异常强度与报警率正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者管理过程中的异常行为可能暗示潜在的医疗错误，但传统方法难以系统性地识别这些异常。作者希望利用历史患者数据构建数据驱动模型，自动检测异常管理行为并触发警报，以辅助临床决策和患者安全。

Method: 利用电子健康记录中存储的历史患者病例数据，构建数据驱动的异常检测模型，将当前患者的管理行为与相似历史患者进行比较，识别异常行为并触发警报。使用4486名心脏手术后患者的数据进行实验，并邀请专家小组对检测结果进行评价。

Result: 基于异常的警报系统具有合理的低误报率；更强的异常信号与更高的报警率之间存在正相关关系。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的异常检测方法可有效识别患者管理中的潜在错误，具备临床部署的可行性，且异常强度可作为区分是否需要报警的有效指标。

Abstract: We develop and evaluate a data-driven approach for detecting unusual (anomalous) patient-management actions using past patient cases stored in an electronic health record (EHR) system. Our hypothesis is that patient-management actions that are unusual with respect to past patients may be due to a potential error and that it is worthwhile to raise an alert if such a condition is encountered. We evaluate this hypothesis using data obtained from the electronic health records of 4,486 post-cardiac surgical patients. We base the evaluation on the opinions of a panel of experts. The results support that anomaly-based alerting can have reasonably low false alert rates and that stronger anomalies are correlated with higher alert rates.

</details>


### [253] [Joint Treatment Effect Estimation from Incomplete Healthcare Data: Temporal Causal Normalizing Flows with LLM-driven Evolutionary MNAR Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05125)
*Olivia Jullian Parra,Sara Zoccheddu,David Catalan Cerezo,Tom Forzy,Franziska Ulrich,William Sutcliffe,Jakob Martin Burgstaller,Oliver Senn,Patrick Owen,Nicola Serra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段因果推断流水线，用于处理电子健康记录（EHR）中高达50%-80%的非随机缺失（MNAR）数据。第一阶段提出CausalFlow-T，一种DAG约束的归一化流模型，结合LSTM编码的患者历史进行精确可逆反事实推断，避免变分推断的近似误差。第二阶段引入LLM驱动的进化插补器，通过提出可执行的插补算子而非单个条目来处理缺失值。在合成和半合成基准测试中，该方法在30%-80% MNAR缺失率下优于现有方法。在瑞士2型糖尿病患者的真实EHR数据中，估计GLP-1受体激动剂相比SGLT-2抑制剂的方案依从体重损失差异为-0.98 kg，与随机对照试验证据一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的治疗效果估计方法通常将因果推断、缺失值处理和时序结构分开处理，在处理EHR数据中高达50%-80%的非随机缺失（MNAR）生物标志物和时间变化的混杂因素时鲁棒性不足。需要一种能够同时处理这些挑战的统一方法。

Method: 两阶段流水线：(1) CausalFlow-T：DAG约束的归一化流模型，使用LSTM编码患者历史，进行精确可逆反事实推断；(2) LLM驱动的进化插补器：利用大语言模型（LLM）提出可执行的插补算子，而非直接插补单个条目，在30%-80% MNAR缺失率下进行评估。

Result: 消融实验表明DAG约束和精确推断分别解决不同的失败模式，二者不可相互替代。在30%-80% MNAR缺失率下，LLM驱动的进化插补器在生物标志物和因果指标上取得最佳综合排名，在点级准确性和时间外推方面领先，同时保持ATE恢复。在瑞士2型糖尿病患者真实EHR数据中，估计GLP-1受体激动剂相比SGLT-2抑制剂的体重损失差异为-0.98 kg [95% CI -1.01, -0.96]，与随机对照试验证据一致。

Conclusion: 提出的两阶段流水线能够从现实世界中不完整的纵向EHR数据中获得与RCT证据一致的因果估计，为在随机对照试验不可行时利用观察性数据进行因果推断提供了鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Target trial emulation (TTE) enables causal questions to be studied with observational data when randomized controlled trials (RCTs) are infeasible. Yet treatment-effect methods often address causal estimation, missingness, and temporal structure separately, limiting their robustness in electronic health records (EHRs), where time-varying confounding and missing-not-at-random (MNAR) biomarkers can reach 50%--80%. We propose a two-stage pipeline for treatment effect estimation from incomplete longitudinal EHRs. First, CausalFlow-T, a directed acyclic graph (DAG)-constrained normalizing flow with long short-term memory (LSTM)-encoded patient history, performs exact invertible counterfactual inference, avoiding approximation errors from variational inference and separating confounding through explicit causal structure. Ablations on four synthetic and one semi-synthetic benchmark with known counterfactuals show that DAG constraints and exact inference address distinct failure modes: neither compensates for the other. Second, because CausalFlow-T requires completed inputs, we introduce an LLM-driven evolutionary imputer that proposes executable imputation operators rather than individual entries, and evaluate it with three large language model (LLM) backends, including two open-source models. Across 30%--80% MNAR missingness, this imputer achieves the best pooled rank over biomarker and causal metrics, leading in point-wise accuracy and temporal extrapolation while preserving average treatment effect (ATE) recovery as statistical baselines degrade. On Swiss primary-care EHRs from adults with type 2 diabetes initiating a GLP-1 receptor agonist or SGLT-2 inhibitor, the pipeline estimates a per-protocol weight-loss difference of -0.98 kg [95% CI -1.01, -0.96] favoring GLP-1 receptor agonists, consistent with randomized evidence and obtained from realistically incomplete real-world EHRs.

</details>


### [254] [Low-Cost Black-Box Detection of LLM Hallucinations via Dynamical System Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05134)
*Dan Wilson,Mohamed Akrout*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Koopman算子理论的单样本幻觉检测方法，将LLM视为黑箱动力系统，通过嵌入模型投影响应到高维流形，拟合事实与幻觉状态的转移算子，并利用偏好校准机制优化阈值，实现低资源开销下的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测方法依赖计算昂贵的采样一致性检查或外部知识检索，亟需一种低成本、单样本通过的检测方案。

Method: 将LLM响应通过嵌入模型投影至高维流形，视作潜状态空间动力学的可观测量；利用Koopman算子理论分别拟合事实与幻觉状态下的转移算子，定义基于预测误差的微分残差分数；引入偏好感知校准机制，基于少量示范样本优化分类阈值。

Result: 在三个数据基准上实现SOTA性能，且资源开销显著降低。

Conclusion: 该方法在单样本推理场景下有效检测幻觉，无需二次采样或外部知识，兼顾性能与效率，并支持用户偏好自适应调节。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) frequently generate plausible but non-factual content, a phenomenon known as hallucination. While existing detection methods typically rely on computationally expensive sampling-based consistency checks or external knowledge retrieval, we propose a new method that treats the LLM as a black-box dynamical system. By projecting LLM responses into a high-dimensional manifold via an embedding model, we characterize the resulting vector sequences as observable realizations of the model's latent state-space dynamics. Leveraging Koopman operator theory, we fit the transition operators for both factual and hallucinated regimes and define a differential residual score based on their respective prediction errors. To accommodate varying user requirements and domain-specific sensitivities, we introduce a preference-aware calibration mechanism that optimizes the classification threshold based on a small set of demonstrations. This approach enables low-cost hallucination detection in a single-sample pass, avoiding the need for secondary sampling or external grounding. Extensive testing across three data benchmarks demonstrates that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance with reduced resource overhead.

</details>


### [255] [Human-AI Co-Mentorship in Project-Based Learning: A Case Study in Financial Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05144)
*Freyaa Chawla,Ahan Chawla,Rishi Singh,Joe Germino,Grigorii Khvatskii*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个由高中生和低年级本科生组成的团队，在研究生导师指导和AI工具辅助下，于2025年夏季完成的金融AI研究项目。尽管参与者缺乏AI和金融背景，但通过AI工具驱动的流程设计（而非传统课堂先学后练的模式），他们成功实现了ETF价格预测模型。每日站会用于调试和概念解答，学生各自深入计算机科学或金融领域，取得了显著进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在AI工具辅助下，如何让缺乏领域背景的高中生能够跳过传统先学理论再应用的教学模式，直接从事高层次的金融AI问题定义与解决方案设计，并验证这种教学范式的可行性。

Method: 采用AI驱动的流程设计教学法：学生先识别解决问题的步骤序列，然后使用AI工具执行每一步；通过日常站会进行代码调试和概念解答；学生基于个人兴趣深入计算机科学或金融领域，同时协作推进项目。

Result: 学生在有限的技术背景条件下，通过AI工具迭代开发了必要代码，构建了具有真实世界应用价值的ETF价格预测模型；每个学生都能在自己感兴趣的领域（计算机科学或金融）深入探索，并在整个夏天取得了显著进步。

Conclusion: AI工具可以有效改变传统教学模式，使缺乏背景知识的学生能够聚焦于高层次的问题定义和概念理解，而非被底层技术实现所困扰。这种以AI为中介的流程设计教学法在高阶思维培养和跨学科协作方面具有重要潜力。

Abstract: This paper reflects on a AI research project carried out by a team of high-school and early-undergraduate students under the mentorship of graduate researchers and ably assisted by AI tools. We share our experience in not only on the learning experience for the high school students, but also on how AI tools accelerated the process that enabled the high school students to focus on higher order problem formulation and solution. Although the participants entered the project with limited background in both AI and finance, they showed strong enthusiasm for technical market analysis and ETF price prediction. Traditional learning settings would first teach the necessary methods in a classroom setting and only later let students apply them. In contrast, our project emphasized workflow design: students identified the sequence of steps needed to address the problem and then used AI-driven tools to execute each step.
  We note that the high school students developed the necessary code through iterating with the AI tools, and we used our daily stand-ups to debug and answer conceptual questions. Each of the student was able to dig deeper into their area of interest whether computer science or finance, while collaboratively making a significant advance over the summer of 2025. This project was an important pedagogical exercise on how AI tools can be used for mentoring high school students, allowing them to focus on their specific interests and using the daily stand-ups to focus on problem definition and conceptual understanding. Despite their limited technical qualifications, the students were able to leverage AI tools to build meaningful models with real-world application.

</details>


### [256] [Superposition Is Not Necessary: A Mechanistic Interpretability Analysis of Transformer Representations for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05151)
*Alper Yıldırım*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过稀疏自编码器（SAEs）对PatchTST的可解释性分析发现，时间序列Transformer的FFN层并未出现强叠加（superposition）现象，表征保持稀疏且对潜在干预不敏感，这解释了为何简单线性模型（如DLinear）在该任务上依然具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Transformer在时间序列预测中被广泛使用，但其内部表征机制是否真正发挥作用尚不明确，同时简单线性模型的持续竞争力缺乏物理解释。作者希望用可解释性工具揭示这一现象的根本原因。

Method: 首先证明单层窄维度Transformer在基准测试中可匹配深层配置性能；然后训练稀疏自编码器（SAEs）提取PatchTST中FFN层的后GELU中间激活，字典大小设置为0.5x至4.0x原生维度；通过词典扩展实验和因果干预分析评估叠加（superposition）程度。

Result: 词典扩展至4.0x时性能变化仅0.214%，大量过完备字典维度处于非活跃状态；对主导潜在特征进行因果干预时，预测结果几乎不受影响。所有设置下均未发现FFN表征依赖强叠加的证据，表征保持稀疏且稳定。

Conclusion: 时间序列预测任务的FFN表征不需要叠加（superposition）机制，标准基准任务可能不要求语言模型中那种丰富的组合式表征，这解释了为何简单线性模型在该领域仍具有竞争力。

Abstract: Transformer architectures have been widely adopted for time series forecasting, yet whether the representational mechanisms that make them powerful in NLP actually engage on time series data remains unexplored. The persistent competitiveness of simple linear models such as DLinear has fueled ongoing debate, but no mechanistic explanation for this phenomenon has been offered. We address this gap by applying sparse autoencoders (SAEs), a tool from mechanistic interpretability, to probe the internal representations of PatchTST. We first establish that a single-layer, narrow-dimensional transformer matches the forecasting performance of deeper configurations across commonly used benchmarks. We then train SAEs on the post-GELU intermediate FFN activations with dictionary sizes ranging from 0.5x to 4.0x the native dimensionality. Expanding the dictionary yields negligible downstream performance change (average 0.214%), with large portions of overcomplete dictionaries remaining inactive. Targeted causal interventions on dominant latent features produce minimal forecast perturbation. Across all evaluated settings, we observe no empirical evidence that the analyzed FFN representations rely on strong superposition. Instead, the representations remain sparse, stable under aggressive dictionary expansion, and largely insensitive to latent interventions. These results demonstrate that superposition is not necessary for competitive performance on standard forecasting benchmarks, suggesting they may not demand the rich compositional representations that drive transformer success in language modeling, and helping explain the persistent competitiveness of simple linear models

</details>
